Chapter 3001: Falling Into An Ancient Superstar!

“It’s… the boxing champion!” Judging from the unique identification of the distress signal, it was ‘Iron Fist’, the model of the boxing champion.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. ‘Hunting Falcon’ locked onto ‘Iron Fist’, while he sent telepathic thoughts to read the more detailed distress messages.

As the data and information flowed in, the awkward situation of Iron Fist and the broken parameters of the starship appeared on the mainframe crystal processor of Falcon.

The boxing champion was truly unlucky.

His starship did not jump to the rainbow dust belt outside of the ancient giants like other starships did. Instead, as Li Yao had feared, it directly jumped to the newly generated ringthe stone belt.

The cosmic dust the size of sand was enough to collapse a starship that had just jumped out of the four-dimensional space, not to mention meteorites the size of fingernails and fists. They were like ten thousand cannonballs that were bombarding every corner of Iron Fist. No human captain could deal with such a dense rain of bullets. Chances were that the starship would break up the moment they jumped out of the four-dimensional space.

Thankfully, the information life-form named ‘Fist King’ had planned the route and the defense of the spiritual shield with his amazing computational ability. He had also manipulated the mustard-level adhesive to repair the damage at a high efficiency. That was how he managed to maintain 30% of the impetus of Iron Fist and complete the soul-stirring ‘landing’.

But as soon as he entered the stone belt, a new problem occurred.

Their ‘landing spot’ was too close to the ancient star. It had been captured by the enormous gravity of the planet and was falling toward the surface of the planet.

If they had 100% impetus, or even more than 50%, they might have been able to escape.

But right now, he was only left with 30% of the impetus. Besides, the structure of the starship was seriously damaged. He did not dare overload the power unit at all. Like a drowning man in a swamp, the more he struggled, the faster he would sink. He could only watch himself sinking deeper and deeper until he was swallowed by the darkness.

Through the remote monitoring system, Li Yao could see that all the parameters of the power units on Iron Fist had turned into orange, crimson, crimson, and black, followed by a pale, dead gray.

He seemed to be able to hear the noises of the power units exploding and being paralyzed and the main structure of the starship being twisted and broken due to metal fatigue. There was no force that could stop the fall of Iron Fist. They would either fall into the ocean of methane and hydrogen from the ancient giants and be crushed into iron lumps less than a hundred meters in diameter from several kilometers long, or they would be swallowed by the lightning. The fragile structure of the warship would be torn apart together with the flesh and blood of the human beings. The latter way was more straightforward. “Punching King!”

n

The interference of the magnetic field was too severe. Even though Li Yao’s ‘Hunting Falcon’ was closest to the Fist King’s ‘Iron Fist’, it still took him a lot of effort to reach out to the other party through point-to-point communication. “What’s the situation on your side? Is there any hope of escape? Launch the escape capsule now. I’ll meet you in the periphery of the stone belt!”

“It’s useless.”

The other side of the communication channel was silent for a long time. There was nothing but rattling noises, as if a super sandstorm had just broken out. The Fist King’s voice was intermittent. “The interior of the starship… is seriously damaged… Many people are injured and unable to reach their escape capsules… The escape zone has been pierced through by the meteor shower, too. Most of the escape capsules have been destroyed. It is not enough for all the crew members to escape… Even if they board the escape capsules, the power and plate armor of the escape capsules are not enough to get rid of the gravity of the ancient planet and the storm inside the rings… There is no way that they can escape.”

“…What about you?” Li Yao felt that his nose and his heart were aching at the same time. He gritted his teeth and shouted, “You can get out, can’t you? What are you waiting for? Release yourself now. It will be too late if you are late!”

Different from ordinary human beings, the boxing champion was essentially a super-large database. It did not have a real body. Although it required a super crystal processor to maintain the efficiency of the database, such crystal processors were not only available on Iron Fist but also on other starships.

In other words, the Fist King had multiple clones that were stored on different starships. Even if Iron Fist failed, he could still be ‘reborn’ as long as he sent out a lot of data in time. At best, his computational ability and logical thinking ability would be slightly reduced, and he would lose part of the memory data. However, the information life-forms were both strong and weak. The launch of the data depended on the rhythm of the spiritual waves. However, the closer they were to the atmosphere of the ancient giants, the more intense the spiritual interference would be, and the more likely they would encounter the terrifying ionizing magnetic storm. If the core database of the boxing champion was swallowed by the ionizing magnetic storm when it was launched, they would be doomed forever.

One second of delay equaled to one second of risk. Li Yao had no idea what the Fist King was waiting for. Time was of the essence. It was impossible for him to repair Iron Fist. He would only be wasting his precious computational ability and hope of survival! “I… can’t leave yet.”

Amid the intense interference, the Fist King’s deep voice came over. “I’m trying to stabilize Iron Fist and resist the gravity of the ancient star before the fuel runs out so that it can stay above the atmosphere of the ancient star longer.” “Why?”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Confused, Li Yao was more than anxious. “Judging from the damage data of Iron Fist, it is impossible for you to repair it. What’s the point of a stalemate above the methane atmosphere of an ancient star when you escaped with its own impetus?”

“Yes.”

The Fist King said calmly, “Our situation is very bad. Many experts and scholars on board are dead. Those who are still alive understand that they don’t have long to live, but they don’t want to die meaninglessly. These people, especially the astronomers, beg me to try to maintain the stability of Iron Fist so that they can have more time to scan and study the internal data of the ancient giants.

“You know that human beings rarely had the opportunity to approach such a super-large, unstable planet, much less observe and study its data.

“The astronomers on Iron Fist want to take the opportunity to collect more data and send it to your ‘Falcon’. The data will fill the gap of mankind’s research on the giant planets and give you more hope for your future exploration. At the very least, the more you know about the ancient giants, the more likely you will be able to deal with the accident and escape, won’t you? “I think what the scholars said makes a lot of sense. So, how should we put it in the language of human beings? ‘Play with Your Highness at the risk of your life’? Yes, that’s it.

“Li Yao, don’t worry about me. I will seize the last opportunity to send out my core database. Right now, you just need to make sure that the reception unit of ‘Hunting Falcon’ is functioning normally and that all the data sent from ‘Iron Fist’ is collected. All the experts and scholars here are gritting their teeth and making sure that their beliefs are firm and their souls are clear. Before they are torn apart by the planet, collect as much data as possible and send it over!”

Li Yao was greatly moved.

He did not expect such an answer.

Very soon, the data about the ancient giantsthe constituents of the atmosphere, the frequency and intensity of the ionizing thunderstorms, the different densities and gravity of the planets-flooded into ‘Hunting Falcon’ through the stone belts.

Li Yao’s eyes were somewhat red.

He seemed to be able to see the scenes on Iron Fist. The experts and scholars, the astronomers, the spiritual energy scientists, the meteorologists, and the authorities of the basic subjects who were too weak to fight back, all knew that they would not be able to run away from the disaster. They knew that they were about to meet the most brutal doom, but they did not waste even a second to lament or grieve. They widened their eyes, bit their lips, and made up their mind to show their determination and pride as the only weapons they had left in front of their most formidable enemy, the ancient giants.

Human beings could be annihilated, but they could never be defeated. Even at the last second of their lives, they had to figure out the mysteries of the universe.

It was the belief of the scholars.

Whether it was the federation or the imperium, whether it was the Cultivators or the Immortal Cultivators, the scholars, especially the top scholars in basic subjects, were often the most special and the purest people.

The reason was simple. Even if an Immortal Cultivator in the Nascent Soul Stage was interested in astronomy, they wouldn’t scheme against each other, fight for power, and kill each other like the combat-type Immortal Cultivators did.

It was too childish, too stupid, and too boring to ask the astronomers to look back and indulge in the petty benefits of hairless monkeys when they were used to the changes of the universe billions of years ago.

At the last moment of their lives, both the scholars of the federation and the imperium released the most glamorous fire of life and turned into the eyes of all mankind, trying to see more of the magnificence and mystery of the universe. This was the significance of their existence. With dazzling brilliance, Iron Fist finally stopped falling

However, the attraction force of the ancient star and the impetus of the starship made the metal fatigue of the main structure of the starship soar. Everybody could tell that the boxing champion would not last long.

The tears in Li Yao’s eyes almost melted into magma.

The parameters of the ancient star collided with those of Iron Fist. They found the only possibility from the countless impossibilities.

“Punching King!”

Li Yao roared, “Hold on! I’m coming to save you! I’m coming to save you!”

“…Don’t come close.”

The Fist King’s calm voice finally broke the ice. He was somewhat surprised. “The meteorites and the ionizing magnetic storms here are too intense. One moment of carelessness, and you will fall into the gravity swirl of the ancient giants. You cannot joke around with the lives of everybody on ‘Hunting Falcon’. We have a backup plan. In such a situation, we can only abandon the starships that are in desperate straits!

“I know.”

Li Yao grinned, revealing his white teeth. “Therefore, it was not Falcon but me who came to your rescue!

Chapter 3002: This Is My Enemy!

“…You’re crazy!”

The Fist King’s crystal processor seemed to be crackling in the communication channel. He immediately understood Li Yao’s plan. “You are going to rescue us by breaking through the stone belts with the Giant God Soldier and infiltrating the gravity ring of the ancient giants? That’s impossible. Iron Fist is too heavy for a single Giant God Soldier such as yourself. Even ‘Incendiary’, the toughest Giant God Soldier in the Pangu Universe, won’t work. You will only exhaust the fuel of the Giant God Soldier, damage its structure, and be swallowed by the ancient giants together with us.

“Besides, the structure of Iron Fist is too fragile. Your ‘incendent’ will not only fail to push us out of the gravity ring but also destroy the structure of Iron Fist and dismantle us in advance!

“Therefore, all the experts and scholars here appreciate your kindness, but there is no need for you to do such a meaningless thing as to be buried together with Iron Fist!”

“Why are you so pessimistic? In the war against the universe, every bit of our hard work is not meaningless!

Li Yao smiled. He pressed the handrail softly and leaned against the back of the seat, activating the traps hidden inside the seat. Suddenly, the seat shivered and turned into a floating platform, sliding all the way to the preparation workshop of the Giant God Soldier and allowing him to enter the ‘Arsonist’s’ residence directly.

Little Black, the liquid crystal processor for the Giant God Soldier, covered him up as naturally as a fish breathing in water. Li Yao completed the transition from a starship to a Giant God Soldier and connected to the Fist King’s channel again. He continued, “Of course, one ‘Arsonist’ is not enough to save Iron Fist. However, if dozens or even hundreds of Giant God Soldiers are deployed, some of which will appear in certain locations on the shell of Iron Fist as auxiliary power units according to my delicate calculations, while some others will be responsible for blocking the screaming meteoroids in the stone belts and creating an undisturbed vacuum temporarily. There is a slim chance that we can drag Iron Fist back!

“Such a possibility is too unlikely. How many Exos will agree to your crazy decision?” The Fist King’s voice was full of suspicion and objection. “Besides, the spiritual interference is so intense right now. Have you reached out to all the starships and established an unimpeded battle network? Will the experts, especially the Immortal Cultivators of the Imperium of True Human Beings, agree to your crazy plan if you can get the approval of hundreds of operators of the Giant God Soldiers?

“Well, I think so.”

Li Yao scratched his nose. “Anyways, I’ve sent out the distress signal and the description of the situation. The interference of the electromagnetic force is indeed too strong. Therefore, the battle network will be delayed for sure. I believe that the controllers of the Giant God Soldiers—be they Cultivators or Immortal Cultivators—will come to help in a few minutes.”

“What?”

The boxing champion found it hard to believe. “You mean to say that you haven’t gotten the consent and tacit understanding of anybody, and you don’t know whether or not somebody will fight side by side with you, and you are going to risk it all? You are truly a lunatic. A complete lunatic!” “Same to you.”

Li Yao mumbled, “Like you said, ‘I’m willing to risk my life for you’. No matter what, I cannot stand by and watch the real elites of the human civilization be annihilated in the gravity swirl of the ancient giants!

Chi! Chi! Chi!

The three airtight doors were opened one after another. Before that, the pyromaniacs had already burst into flames and leapt into the furious sea of stars!

“Don’t come close, Li Yao!”

The boxing champion roared, “You can’t save us. It’s not worth it!”

“I don’t care whether it is worth it or not. I only care about the satisfaction! I’m already here!”

The ‘Arsonist’ turned into a streak of brightness in dark gold and crimson and crossed the distance of tens of thousands of kilometers in the blink of an eye, entering the extremely unstable stone belt.

Thousands of pieces of meteoroids rushed close, only to be crushed and obliterated by Li Yao’s surging spiritual flames. They seemed to be dragging a crimson cloak that was tens of thousands of kilometers long behind the arsonist.

Now, even without his communication, the friendly starships far away could detect the abnormal energy reactions in the stone belts and sense his intentions.

“Do you know why I’ve always been lazy and don’t want to spar with you? Do you know why I push and dodge Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and even Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators whenever I can?

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Inside the ‘Arsonist’, Li Yao was laughing crazily.

The smile on his face seemed to have turned into the aura of a giant soldier.

Everything in front of him, be it the meteoroids and meteoroids dozens of meters in diameter, the high-energy particle streams, the super radiation, or even the electric arcs spurting out of the atmosphere of the ancient star, were all slashed into pieces and vaporized.

“It’s not that I’m underestimating the heroes of the world, and it’s not because I’m confident that I can deal with the hundreds of Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators at the same time. It’s because I’ve lost all interest in fighting against my compatriots-human beings! Even if I defeat all the human beings and become the strongest of them, so what? In front of the boundless universe, I’m still a speck of dust, the biggest, most meaningless speck of dust!

“Human beings are no longer my targets.

“The universe is my enemy.

“Fighting against the heaven and the earth can be a lot of fun. I’m not just here to rescue you. I’m also fighting against the silly giant, the ancient star.

“Come on, Gu Gaoxing. Give it your all and release your strongest attraction force. Let’s see if you can swallow me or I can take back ‘Iron Fist’ right under your nose!”

Li Yao laughed crazily. The ‘cloak’ on his back seemed to have turned into a red tassel that was ready to throw a gun. He was not scared at all and darted toward the struggling Iron Fist, or the gravity ring and even the atmosphere of the ancient giants. Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

Swish! Swish! Swish! Swish!

Squeak squeak squeak!

The closer they got to the ancient star, the more chaotic the magnetic field was, and the more intense the spiritual interference was. A lot of invisible high-energy particles even pierced through the spiritual shields and the composite plate armor of the larsonist’. They even pierced through Little Black and Li Yao’s skin, muscles, and bones.

Li Yao’s ears were filled with the noise of interference and the metal fatigue of the Giant God Soldier. Many of his cells had been burnt by the high-energy particle stream, and his vigorous vitality had stagnated.

But he gritted his teeth and pressed on, as if he were going against a super hurricane.

Finally, in a sea of lightning that was almost boiling, he saw Iron Fist!

Right now, Iron Fist was as miserable as a shark that had been skinned.

But at the very least, its main structure was still intact, and there were no large-scale eruptions of materials and fuel. The few spurts on its abdomen and rear were still spurting lackluster flames.

Not far below Iron Fist, in the surging cloud of methane, thick bolts of lightning darted out like the arms of devils. Attracted by the spiritual shield and the spurting flames, they snatched at Iron Fist and brushed past its abdomen at a close distance.

“Punching King!” Li Yao exclaimed in joy. He steered the ‘Arsonist’ to the head of Iron Fist and fired dozens of crystal cannons into the atmosphere of the ancient star to slow down the new thunderstorm.

Without waiting for the Fist King’s reply, the arsonist stuck to the head of Iron Fist, which was falling down. The output power of the power unit was triggered to the maximum, and thousands of streams of brightness spurted out, trying to catch Iron Fist and push it away!

Iron Fist was a very new arsenal warship. It was one of the largest and highest-quality warships in the entire exploration fleet and more than three kilometers long.

In comparison, the arsonist was no taller than forty meters. He was like an ant trying to lift a giant bone.

In only a few seconds, the joints of the ‘Arsonist’ exploded, and the exhaust flames turned pale because of the excessive consumption of crystal fuel. His arms pierced into the plate on the head of Iron Fist. “What are you doing exactly?” The boxing champion could not wait to take out a hammer from the mechanical arm and extend it into the spiritual residence of the ‘arsonist’ to smack Li Yao’s head. “This is not a fight but a suicide. Nobody is going to go crazy with you. Do you understand?” “Of course… I understand!” Li Yao was fighting against the super gravity and the electromagnetic waves of the ancient star all by himself. Blood was flowing out of his nose and the corner of his mouth, but the intense blood could not erase the confidence in his smile at all. “It’s you. You just don’t understand, Fist King. That’s all.”

“The operation to explore the ‘Ancient Tomb of the Supreme Emperor’—the Divine Tomb—the Black Tomb’—will certainly encounter countless more perilous situations and temptations that no righteous man can resist. However, our team is made of three different forces. Although we have gone through training, we are far from being united.

“If we had just jumped over and abandoned our comrades on the starship during the first crisis, we would’ve been doomed. Nobody would’ve been willing to rescue anybody who was in danger again. Everybody would’ve been thinking about how to save themselves and the most valuable treasures of the Supreme Emperor, the Pangu Clan, and the makers of the black wall!

“Do you understand now? An adventure team made of smart people won’t go far. Somebody has to play the role of a lunatic and an idiot first. They have to sacrifice themselves without any regret, without caring about the returns. The experts and scholars on board have become ‘idiots’ in the beginning, but it is still not enough. The number of ‘idiots’ is not enough for the entire team to complete the qualitative change. So, let me add to the fire!

“I don’t just want to save the people on the starship. I also want to save something in the hearts of the experts, something… that has never been awakened but must exist. Those things are the key to our mission!

Chapter 3003: Fighting To The Moment Of Hope!

BOOM!

Along with Li Yao’s roar, the output power of the ‘Arsonist’ increased again, far beyond its capacity. Dozens of flames that looked like wings spurted out of his back. The crimson flames were raging, as if he were declaring war on an ancient giant that was right in front of him.

The downward momentum of Iron Fist was indeed suppressed by Li Yao alone. The new arsenal warship was even rising higher and higher, driven by the ‘incendent’, trying to get rid of the terrifying gravity trap!

Pu!

Such an unbelievable technique naturally required a high price. Li Yao felt that countless high-energy particle streams and invisible electric arcs were shooting at him from the atmosphere of the ancient star. They pierced through the giant soldier and his chest, burning his internal organs and making him vomit a mouthful of black blood.

But he continued resisting the gravity of the planet and raised Iron Fist by an inch, an inch, and another inch!

“Li Yao…”

“Vulture…”

“Black Wind King…”

Everybody on Iron Fist knew that Li Yao had come to the rescue by himself. They were all dazed for a moment when they saw the intimidating figure of the arsonist through the monitoring crystal cameras.

Especially the astronomers and spiritual energy researchers from the Imperium of True Human Beings.

In the imperium where the strong were respected, although the non-battle-type Immortal Cultivators could reach the Nascent Soul Stage and enjoy a certain social status, they had never dared to hope that a battle-type Immortal Cultivator would rescue them at the critical moment.

“Why?”

Many Immortal Cultivators were confused. “Why would they sacrifice their ‘important’ lives to save insignificant people such as themselves?”

In any case, before the question was answered, hot streams were already flowing out of the deepest part of their hearts and flowing through their bodies, triggering every cell in their body and releasing the heat from their pores.

“Even Li Yao has dedicated himself to the battle. We must not admit defeat! So what if the ancient star is strong? At least for now, we are still alive. If we are alive, we will fight without stopping. Fellow Cultivators, let’s fight until the last moment in our posts!”

The Fist King’s roar that was as hard as steel also sounded the horn to charge deep inside Iron Fist.

A shocking scene played out.

Faced with the enormous planet that was about to crush them, everybody was not scared at all. Taking a deep breath, they released the most brilliant brilliance of their belief and gave them endless courage and strength.

Dragging their wounded bodies, the astronomers rushed to the front of the crystal processor and released probe balls into the methane atmosphere of the ancient star. They seized the remaining time to collect and analyze the data inside the gas planet and infer whether or not it boasted a liquid layer or even a dense solid core.

In the meantime, the spiritual energy scientists captured the super radiation and high-energy particle streams released by the ancient giants and analyzed the lightning and thunder in the methane atmosphere, trying to find the pattern of the furious magnetic field so that they could ‘conquer’ the ancient giants.

The best medical specialists of mankind, on the other hand, focused their attention on the patients and even inserted sharp examination chips into their bodies.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

As the radiation grew stronger and the gravity became higher and higher, they recorded a series of detailed data of their own slow failure and death. Such data would certainly be of great benefit for the future human beings to survive and fight in high-gravity and super-strong environments.

In the meantime, the warriors put on their crystal suits, picked up the materials for repairing and strengthening, and rushed out of the cabins to reinforce the broken shell of Iron Fist. They were trying to buy more time in front of the chainsword wielded by the grim reaper so that more plug-in auxiliary power units would be connected.

“Li Yao, let us help you!”

Many warriors lunged to Li Yao’s side and extended their arms, too. They embedded themselves into the shell of Iron Fist and boosted the power output of the crystal suit to the maximum, providing a small, feeble, upward, and bright force for Iron Fist.

“Li Yao, we’ve done everything we could. Now, let’s see if more people will help us!”

The boxing champion mumbled.

“It will. It will. Believe in me. Believe in mankind. Believe in the future. Believe in hope!”

Li Yao’s teeth were ground again, but the smile on his face was so bright that they seemed to be burning. “It is our duty to fight until the moment of hope comes!

BOOM!

The crazy overloading finally triggered the consequences. One of the power units on the back of the arsonist suddenly exploded into a giant fireball surrounded by electric arcs. One of the wings made of flames was immediately broken.

The imbalance of the impetus caused Iron Fist to lean to the right. The screeching noises of metal fatigue were more piercing than ever. Visible cracks appeared on the shell of the starship. Pushed by the imbalance of pressure inside and outside, a lot of materials and fuel spurted out like blood.

If such a gap was not fixed in time, it was very likely that it would expand until the entire starship was cut in half.

“Hurry up and repair it!”

Countless screams burst out inside the crystal suits. All the crew members rushed to the crevice, trying to block it with their flesh and blood.

“They will come… they will come… they will come… they will definitely come!”

Li Yao’s eyes were bloodshot, and he was almost shedding tears of blood. His lips were torn apart by the sharp teeth, and his blood was flowing to his chest, which was fluctuating fast. His brain cells were almost exploding. He calculated the new dynamic balance posture after he lost a power point and tried to resist Iron Fist again.

However, the attraction force of the ancient star was too strong. He felt that the Iron Fist on his shoulder was getting heavier and heavier, as if a real planet was pressing on his shoulder.

“Even if a real planet falls, I will still push you back!

Li Yao roared and exerted his strength again to fight against the planets, the world, and the universe!

At this moment, the entire universe seemed to have collapsed and turned into an empty battlefield. The only thing left was the tug-of-war between him and the ancient star.

Just as his consciousness was becoming more and more fuzzy and his entire body was gradually sinking, a sharp roar pierced through his brain.

“Li Yao, what are you doing exactly? Why didn’t you tell me you were going to take the risk?”

It was not the boxing champion’s voice, but Ding Lingdang’s.

Was it an auditory hallucination?

“You are playing with your own life without my permission. You are getting more and more unbelievable!”

No. It was not an illusion. It was indeed a voice from the communication channel. The communication distance of the Giant God Soldier was limited. Since he could hear Ding Lingdang’s voice so clearly, it meant that she must be very close!

Li Yao was wild with joy. He was practically dancing with joy as he shouted at the top of his voice, disregarding everything:

“Honey, help me!”

“Honey, help me!”

“Honey, come and save me!”

Ding Lingdang appeared in the Red Fire Dragon

King.

Boss Bai showed up in a ‘thief’.

Long Yangjun appeared in the Heaven Crystal.

Yan Liren and the experts of the Ancient Saint World, as well as Li Yao’s senior brothers ‘Peng Hai’, as well as Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun, all appeared in the sky of the furious atmosphere of the ancient star. Their sharp eyes pierced into the depths of the planet where the lightning and the red spots were raging.

What a furious magnetic field and ionosphere. It seems that the main elements that make up the ancient giants must contain several kinds of highly-radiating crystals in order to turn the atmosphere of the planet into a swirl of fury and chaos.

Boss Bai said.

“Iron Fist has fallen deep into the atmosphere and will be tied up by the lightning at any moment. Under such circumstances, it must be calculated very precisely before it can be pushed out again. Everybody must work together.

Meng Chi thought to himself.

“Our manpower seems to be insufficient. It will be terrible if the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium stand by and watch us die on purpose so that they can take advantage of us.

Peng Hai pondered for a moment and said, “So, we need to discuss this matter further…”

Before they had the chance to make plans, Ding Lingdang had already rushed down on her Red Fire Dragon King. “Li Yao, don’t run. I’m coming!”

“…” Boss Bai.

Meng Chixin: “…”

“…” Peng Hai. The father of the federation and the previous Speaker of the federation had both rushed down to rescue him. Did they have a second choice?

“Ancient giant planet? Interesting.”

Yan Liren smiled. Looking at the lightning and thunder on the surface of the planet, there was intense excitement in his eyes. He licked his lips and said, “I would very much like to know whether or not a sword can cut through gravity when it is fast enough.

He rushed down.

“Amitabha. Impulsive. Too impulsive!”

Master Bitter Cicada sighed. He shook his head and rushed down, too.

“Li Yao, you are truly the greatest clown in the history of the federation!

Fiend Blade Peng Hai, Li Yao’s senior brother, smiled bitterly and rushed down, too.

Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun looked at each other and saw the tacit understanding in each other’s eyes. They both rushed down.

With these people taking the lead, how could the remaining operators of the Giant Soldiers stand by and watch? They dived down one after another.

Very soon, only Boss Bai and Long Yangjun, the two experts who were subtly neutral and uninvolved, were left.

Boss Bai looked at Long Yangjun. “Why are you looking at me?”

Long Yangjun wrinkled his nose. “Do you think that I’m a brainless fool such as Li Yao who would take unnecessary risks when his blood is boiling? You’ve got to be kidding me! The environment down there is too harsh. Even all the experts of the federation might not be able to rescue Iron Fist, unless the experts of the imperium come to his rescue one after another. Is that possible? Do you think that the selfish Immortal Cultivators would sacrifice their lives for such meaningless things? Foolish. How idiotic!

Chapter 3004: The Strong Swing Their Swords To The Stronger!

“Is he stupid? Yes. It is probably the first impression that many people have when they see Li Yao, including me in the beginning.

Boss Bai smiled and said, “However, if you think about it carefully, so many big shots who were a hundred times smarter and stronger than him have all fallen. Only he, this unbelievably stupid, hot-blooded high school student, has walked all the way to this day… to the peak of the Pangu Universe’s strongest experts. It’s really ‘luck’ and ‘coincidence’!”

Long Yangjun thought of something and asked, “Boss Bai, what are you trying to say?”

“What I’m trying to say is that Li Yao is a smart man. He seems to be taking reckless risks, but in fact, he has taken all the factors into consideration. He knows that nobody-not just the Cultivators of the federation, but also the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium-will abandon him.

Boss Bai said casually, “The reason is simple. He has half of the key to open the core gate of the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor, as well as a lot of information scraps from the database of the Fuxis. Also, Xiao Ming and Wen Wen are both on his side. It is impossible for them to turn a blind eye to his death. It is safe to say that he is of great value. If he dies in this place, the exploration will fail before it even begins.

Ding Lingdang, Yan Liren, and the rest of them had to keep their eyes wide open and focus on blocking all the meteoroids and stardust that might hit Iron Fist.

For ‘madmen’ such as Ding Lingdang and Yan Liren, it was not torture but excitement.

Since when had they climbed to an unreachable position and sealed all their dreams, believing that it was impossible for them to climb any higher, only to spend all their time calculating how to eliminate the dissidents and kill the newcomers who were threatening them so that they could maintain their current position at any cost?

Chapter 3007: Live A Happy Life!

The two tides collided violently, unleashing destructive energy like a solar flare.

A meteorite of high density and hardness had broken through the spiritual shield of his starship and hit the shell of the starship.

According to the picture captured by the crystal camera, the ‘meteorite’ was in the shape of a human being!

“Someone is outside!”

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. The hair all over his body stood up like spears.

In such a desolate place where few people ever set foot in, among the rushing meteoroids and cosmic dust, a man was floating and crashing into their starship?

Li Yao suddenly jumped up. Before his feet left the ground, his crystal suit had covered his toes.

Several other starships hit or scanned the human-shaped objects, too. For a moment, earsplitting alarms echoed in the entire exploration fleet.

However, the human-shaped objects did not take any further action. After they were blown away by the starships, they simply drifted away. Some of them were even blown into pieces by the strengthened plate armor and spiritual shields, but they showed no sign of aggression.

Li Yao was quick enough to catch a human-shaped object with the artificial arms on the shell of the starship before the enemy was blown away.

He went to the ‘disinfective recycling capsule’ in person to examine the mysterious humanoid object.

“Chi_”

After applying more than ten times the pressure, the atomized disinfectant sprayed out and washed away any viruses and bacteria that the mysterious humanoid object might have carried.

Li Yao approached carefully. He released his telepathic thoughts and a few remote puppets to investigate and discovered that it was a crystal suit. However, the person inside the crystal suit was already dead.

Since the crystal suit was made of super-hard alloys and special ceramics, its density and hardness were far higher than those of common meteoroids. Naturally, it was not something that a spiritual shield could crush easily, nor was it something that a meteoroid could break through easily.

However, as hard as the crystal suit was, and as tensile and absorbable as the mustard suit was, it was impossible to absorb the impact of the meteoroid.

If a meteorite weighing dozens of tons crashed into the crystal suit brutally, even if the crystal suit was not shattered, the immense impact would still shatter the bones of the Exo inside and turn his internal organs into a pool of blood.

Such was the case for the Exo.

Li Yao dissected the Exo’s crystal suit carefully. He collapsed like a snail that had lost its shell.

After making sure that his soul was gone and that he could not be more dead, Li Yao asked a professional doctor to dissect him and make sure that his brain and internal organs had been completely shattered, and that he suffered the corrosion of fatal radiation when the spiritual shield of his crystal suit collapsed. That was the cause of his death.

According to the corruption of his body and the withering speed of his cells, he had been killed seven to ten days ago. Judging from the standard of his crystal suit and the armbands and emblems on his mustard suit, it was not hard to guess the identity of the Exo who had died in the vacuum. He was a ‘soldier’ of the old Covenant Alliance and a soldier on one of the five supreme fortresses. In other words, he was Lu Qingchen’s subordinate.

There was more than one such body. Li Linghai, Boss Bai, and Long Yangjun’s starship had retrieved a lot of other bodies, too. The autopsy results were almost the same. All the soldiers of the five supreme fortresses had perished in this place seven to ten days ago.

It meant that Lu Qingchen had arrived in the Supreme Battle Fortress seven to ten days ago and located the entrance of the tomb precisely.

However, he seemed to have experienced a mutation and suffered a great loss.

The deeper they went into the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’, the more corpses they encountered from the five supreme fortresses. Not only were there intact corpses that were covered in crystal suits, there were also corpses that had been frozen into ice before they had the chance to put on their crystal suits. The dead bodies of the latter were extremely fragile and would be blown to smithereens by the spiritual shield. Li Yao felt sorry for them and sighed.

Other than the dead bodies, there were also a lot of debris of space shuttles and escape capsules, and even enormous scraps more than a hundred meters in diameter. According to Xiao Ming and Wen Wen, they were all components of the most important main structure of the five supreme fortresses. The number of the scraps was growing larger and larger, which was quite shocking.

“We are almost at our destination. Lu Qingchen’s team might show up at any moment. Be prepared for a Level 1 battle!” Li Linghai did not dare to be careless and issued an order.

Right then, almost all the starships detected the dense area of the meteoroids in front of them. A lot of metal reactions were detected, indicating that there might be one or several artificial celestial bodies.

The spiritual shields of all the starships were brighter than before. The auxiliary power units protruded out of the hollows around the shell of the starship. A large batch of space shuttles, crystal suits, and even Giant God Soldiers jumped out of the hangars one after another. The main guns had been charged with the maximum spiritual energy, too, ready to launch thunderous attacks at any moment. The people who had been engaged in a prolonged battle against the ‘ancient giant star’ not long ago were more united than ever, and their fighting will soared.

Under the cover of almost a hundred main guns, Li Yao and Ding Lingdang led a dozen Giant God Soldiers to investigate.

Deep inside the broken meteorite belt, the star fortress gradually revealed its hideous face, which looked like an enormous head. Its face was a combination of the features of human beings and the prehistoric species. There was no telling whether it was a human being, a god, or a devil. It was indeed ‘Fortress Thick Earth’, one of the five supreme fortresses!

However, the fortress at this moment was no longer as intimidating as when it was attacking Empyrean Terminus. There was no longer any brightness or brutality. It was like a dry skull.

Li Yao and Ding Lingdang exchanged a look in the video and saw the shock in each other’s eyes.

It was unlikely to be Lu Qingchen’s trick, because they did not detect any spiritual waves from the fortress, which proved that one of the most brutal star fortresses in the history of human civilization had been paralyzed or even wrecked.

It would take at least three to five days to restart such a super fortress and complete all the preparations.

After flying around the fortress, they soon found the ’cause of death’.

On the side of the fortress that faced ‘Ancient Thirteen’, there was a hole more than several kilometers long, and countless splits in the blast that extended outward. Almost all the materials, including the fuel and the crew members, were spurting out of the holes. There might have been serial explosions, too, which burnt the interior of the fortress into a black, empty shell.

“A giant meteoroid seems to be embedded deep inside the fortress. Maybe there is more than one.

Li Yao extended his telepathic thoughts and snuck into the fortress. After scanning for a while, he came to the conclusion that the meteoroid must’ve pierced through the spiritual shield and the shell of the fortress and cut into the core engine furnace as if it were tofu, triggering an irreparable explosion.

The dead bodies floating in the space nearby, the debris, and the scrap that had been burnt, were also telling the story of what had happened.

And this was far from the end.

Soon, they discovered the debris of a second star fortress nearby.

This time, it was ‘Fiery Castle’.

The Inferno Fortress, which had been burning like a star, was no longer able to release any light or heat. It was not even left with an intact body like the ‘Fortress of Earth’. Instead, it had been torn into more than a hundred giant pieces of debris, which were beating Xuan’er helplessly because of the attraction force of the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ and the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor. Most of the crew members on board were vaporized the moment of the explosion or sprayed into the unknown, falling into an endless hell in the form of an ice block.

Although they were enemies, the two supreme fortresses that represented the best technology of mankind had been stomped and savaged by the might of nature. Everybody in the exploration fleet was rendered speechless.

Chapter 3011: The Entrance Of Mithril

As more and more debris was discovered, the reason for the failure of the two supreme fortresses was also inferred. Although the meteoroid torrents around ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ were ferocious and the cosmic dust was full of fatal radiation, it was nothing for the impregnable Supreme Battle Fortress.

Perhaps, this was also the reason why Lu Qingchen was so confident and dared to rush into the place without having too many astronomical parameters scanning and calculating

Unfortunately, his luck was too bad.

Judging from the mass of the meteoroids that hit the fortress and the terrible wounds that tore it apart, it was very likely that Lu Qingchen’s fleet had been hit by the collision of ‘Ancient Thirteen’ and another satellite. The collision of the two satellites resulted in the collapse of the smaller satellites whose density and mass were relatively lower. They were shattered into billions of meteoroids with extremely high initial velocity and momentum. Even ‘Ancient Thirteen’ had lost one tenth of its mass. The incalculable materials were burning furiously and turning into streams of high-energy particles. Together with the terrifying radiation and radiation, they smashed at Lu Qingchen’s fleet. No matter how solid the spiritual shield and the composite plate armor of the Supreme Battle Fortress were, they could not resist such a furious and ferocious torrent.

It was like two giant swirls colliding, and the five supreme fortresses were merely five dry leaves nearby. How could they not be blown into pieces?

The spiritual shield was destroyed. The iron shell was shattered. Ammunition and fuel exploded one after another, resulting in a horrifying scene.

“Is this… Lu Qingchen’s ending?”

“There are only two remnants of the Supreme Battle Fortress here. Where are the other three Supreme Battle Fortresses? Li Yao was deep in thought.

For some reason, there was a prick deep inside his brain, as if something was about to happen.

Under the command of Li Linghai, part of the warriors and magical equipment experts boarded the small carriers and prepared to enter the completely paralyzed fortress to collect the files of the crystal processors inside. It would be best if they could find valuable data such as Lu Qingchen’s logbook.

Other than that, all the starships had released a lot of self-driving probe magical equipment. Crystal and crystal chips were carved into them, and their glittering ‘eyes’ pierced deep into the edge of the gravitational field of ‘Ancient Thirteen’ in search of the mysterious “high-mass unknown celestial bodies’.

Li Yao did not participate in the two tasks. He gazed at the furious sea of stars outside and racked his brain.

“What is Lu Qingchen playing at? He should know that his secret won’t be kept for long. After his fleet sets off, our main force will certainly catch up to him. Besides, we have combined most of the forces of the federation, the imperium, and the Holy Alliance. His five supreme fortresses are definitely no match for us!

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “So, so, if I were Lu Qingchen… How would I stop the pursuers… If I were… I am…”

A lot of stars were gathering in his eyes. He was about to draw a fatal trap.

“Found it!”

Right then, cheers echoed inside the communication channel. “We’ve found the entrance to the tomb of the Supreme Emperor!” “What?”

Colorful brilliance burst out of Li Yao’s pupils, interrupting his meditation.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Dozens of 3D light beams were turned on again and connected to the remote video conference of hundreds of experts. Despite the shrewdness of Empress Dowager Li Linghai, she could not help but be overjoyed and show the experts the picture data that the probe crystal cameras had just sent back.

The first picture was shot with visible light waves. At first glance, there was nothing in the dark, deep vacuum except for catkins and dust belts that were raging like clouds. Only after he poured all his spiritual energy into his eyes could he distinguish an almost transparent dot in the splendid dust under the guidance of Li Linghai.

As the probe crystal camera flew closer and closer, the dot gradually enlarged and became clearer and purer.

All the experts, including Li Yao, had a weird feeling

They had never seen such a weird celestial body. It did not look like a natural planet at all, but a pearl, no, a hundred times purer and more transparent than a pearl, a frozen tear, a clear glass, and an amber that was still immaculate after billions of years of erosion. It was-it was something that should not have appeared in the universe!

Scanning the tear with the special crystal cameras that could detect invisible light and all kinds of radiation, the tear was even more splendid. Millions of colors were mixed together and wriggling slowly as if they were alive without releasing any energy to the outside world.

“Mirror.”

Such a phrase popped up in the minds of many experts.

Yes. The entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor was like a mirror that had been molded into an absolutely precise ball. It reflected all the radiation and brilliance nearby coldly and even reflected the entire universe.

Under Li Linghai’s remote control, a crystal eye slowly flew towards the entrance of the tomb. The closer it flew, the more anxious the experts and experts became.

“It’s impossible!”

The data sent back by the crystal camera shocked all the astronomers and the spiritual energy scientists. They felt that the solid building that they had been building all their life was collapsing.

Right now, the crystal camera was less than five hundred kilometers away from the entrance of the tomb. At such a close distance, it could calculate that the mirror-like ball was no more than ten kilometers in diameter.

However, it boasted a mass equal to that of ‘Ancient Thirteen’, a giant satellite more than five thousand kilometers in diameter. In fact, its mass was even higher than that of ‘Ancient Thirteen’, because it was seizing a lot of materials from the gravitational ring of ‘Ancient Thirteen’ and sending them to its surroundings in the form of a ‘material bridge’, forming dreamy gauze and ripples.

It was less than one ten-thousandth the size of ‘Ancient Thirteen’, but its mass was much higher. Theoretically speaking, its density should be tens of thousands of times higher than that of ‘Ancient Thirteen’.

But it was impossible, because Li Linghai had clearly entered the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor before. The gravity inside was quite normal. It was safe to say that it was maintained in the most comfortable range for human beings. Although some unknown elements had been collected, the density was not very high.

What was even weirder was that any celestial body in the universe, even those that were not real celestial bodies such as ‘wormholes’, would release radiation continuously as long as they existed in the universe for one second. But at such a short distance, the crystal camera failed to detect any radiation waves.

How was that possible? Of course, the black hole would not release any radiation or information to the outside world, either. But if so, the black hole would not be discovered by most detection methods, nor would it be ‘seen’ by human beings. The environment inside the black hole was not suitable for anybody, either. Before human beings entered the center of the black hole, they would be torn into long noodles by the tremendous tearing force.

The Mausoleum of Emperors was definitely not a black hole. It was an existence that was even more mysterious and inexplicable than a black hole. At the very least, none of the laws of the Pangu Universe could analyze it.

The crystal camera was only a hundred kilometers away from the entrance of the tomb.

At this distance, it was no longer a mirror but a gate that faced the entire Pangu Universe to an unknown world.

80 kilometers, 60 kilometers, 40 kilometers, 20 kilometers, 10 kilometers, 5 kilometers, the last 10 meters, zero distance, the probe crystal camera made contact with the entrance of the tomb!

For a moment, the entrance of the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor seemed to be alive. Dozens of threads that seemed to be made of mercury suddenly rose up from the transparent surface and covered the probe crystal camera like a blossoming flower. The ‘mercury’ quickly proliferated and spread out. Very soon, the probe crystal camera was entirely covered. Then, like two drops of water that had touched each other, they completely melted.

Most of the information transmission of the crystal camera had been cut off. It seemed to have sunk into a mysterious ocean. Occasionally, some information would be sent back, but it was all nonsense that nobody could understand.

“Is it-alive?”

Although it was very unprofessional, a relatively young astronomer, on behalf of everyone, exclaimed, “It is swallowing!’

“It is not alive, but the entrance to the tomb of the Supreme Emperor is indeed highly self-organized and purposeful. You may consider it to be a piece of stellar magical equipment refined by an ancient civilization that is always under the control of… the other half of the Fuxi.

Li Linghai said, “There’s one more thing that I want you to pay attention to. It is not a real celestial body but an ‘entrance’. I’ve seen things that are more than ten kilometers long inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor, such as the most brilliant fortresses and super carriers of the Star Ocean Imperium. If those things are not dismantled and transported inside, it means that the entrance can be expanded and expanded, and they can swallow things that are dozens of times larger than themselves.

“Have you tried to analyze the materials for the ‘entrance’?”

One of the materials scientists asked, “At least in the Pangu Universe, we have never found such marvelous materials. They are like… mercury with life.”

“I tried. Unfortunately, I failed.” Li Linghai said, “I’ve utilized the most cutting-edge equipment of the Star Ocean Imperium and employed dozens of conventional metal analysis methods, but I’ve never been able to collect even a speck of dust from the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor. I call the mercury-like material ‘mithril’. It seems to be alive. When it is soft, it can be ten thousand times softer than water. When it is hard, it can be ten thousand times harder than steel. In the previous explorations, we had limited time and could not make any further attempts. I hope that our operation will go well and all the secrets will be revealed.”

Chapter 3012: Stupid” Trap

Of course.

The best experts and adventure teams of the entire Pangu Universe had been gathered for the exploration. They were many times more sophisticated than the days when Li Linghai had been alone. The experts and scholars were confident that they could solve the riddle that Li Linghai could not.

Everyone was rubbing their fists, eager to pass through the ‘Mithril Ball’ and enter the tomb of the Supreme Emperor.

But Li Yao was still frowning. He had a bad feeling

“The entrance of the Supreme Emperor Tomb is so close to the place where the two supreme fortresses collapsed? But there are no traces of the other three supreme fortresses around the entrance of the tomb. What does it mean?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself. The extremely dangerous stars in his eyes gathered again. When all the stars crashed into each other and triggered fatal flames, his face changed completely. He jumped up and shouted at the top of his voice, “Stop. Stop the exploration into the Deep Earth Fortress. Everybody, retreat. Retreat from the Deep Earth Fortress and the debris of the Inferno Fortress. Prepare for the first-degree battle. The enemy might show up at any moment!”

The experts and the expedition team were still reveling in the ecstasy of finding the entrance of the ancient tomb. Everybody was analyzing and calculating the data transmitted by the probe crystal cameras wholeheartedly. Li Yao’s roar slightly startled them, but they did not react so quickly. “…What?”

“Hurry up and put on the mustard suit, crystal suit, giant soldier, and all the defense magical equipment that you can find!

Li Yao was so excited that his voice was almost twisted. “Get as far away from the two wretched supreme fortresses as possible!”

Although they did not know what was going on, everybody had almost instinctive trust in Li Yao’s instincts as an expert in the Soul Splitting Stage. Before they realized what was going on, their bodies had activated all kinds of defense magical equipment from their Cosmos Rings out of reflex. The exploration team that was trying to enter the Deep Earth Battlesuit stopped temporarily, too.

This action had saved many lives.

One minute after Li Yao finished his sentence, all the starships detected a terrifying high-energy reaction deep inside the lifeless fortress. The super space fortress, which was on the verge of death, still had the strength to struggle. Part of the fuel and ammunition that had not exploded yet were detonated brutally!

The remains of the fortress immediately turned into a swirl of destruction.

It turned from a lifeless gray into a dazzling orange. Flames that looked like magma spurted out of the narrow and long cracks and swept everything nearby in streams of high-energy particles. Then, it turned into an enormous light ball, which suddenly expanded to hundreds of kilometers nearby.

In the vacuum of the universe, the blast was not very threatening. But after the entire star fortress was shattered, countless pieces rushed out at a high speed and momentum like a storm of bullets.

Not just the Thick Earth Fortress, the debris of the broken Inferno Fortress had exploded, too, creating countless terrifying traps around the exploration fleet and on their way forward.

There was no way that so much fuel and ammunition could have been left on the remains of the fortress and exploded simultaneously.

Unless somebody had planted a lot of crystal bombs on the debris on purpose.

It was a trap that Lu Qingchen had set up on purpose.

Maybe it was true that the collision between the two satellites had wrecked the two supreme fortresses, but with Lu Qingchen’s ruthlessness and craziness, how could he lose two fifths of his combat ability without doing anything about it?

In a cruel battlefield, it was a common tactic to hide some highly-sensitive crystal bombs below the dead bodies of the enemy and make them into booby traps. He did not expect that Lu Qingchen would be insane enough to make two super-large booby traps out of the dead bodies of the two supreme fortresses! Thankfully, Li Yao reacted intuitively at the last moment and stopped the members of the exploration team from entering the fortress. He also asked everyone, especially the experts and scholars who were not good at fighting, to activate their defensive magical equipment. Otherwise, the explosion of the debris of the two supreme fortresses would have been enough to reimburse hundreds of elite members of the exploration team and hundreds of irreplaceable experts.

Even now, the earthshaking explosions and the storm of bullets were still a major nuisance for the exploration fleet.

The carrier in the lead was almost riddled with holes because of the debris. Li Yao and the other exploration starships in the periphery were hit by the burning debris, too. Their spiritual shields were blinking violently, consuming a lot of precious energy. Some of them were even knocked off their trajectories and fell into the densest and fiercest area of the meteoroid.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The debris and meteoroids hit the shell of the starship one after another, raising glamorous flowers of energy. The atmosphere inside the cabin was quite depressing.

Everyone knew that Lu Qingchen’s plan was not so simple.

Since he was willing to build such a large-scale trap with the debris of the two Supreme Battle fortresses, he must have a lot of other tricks up his sleeve.

As he expected!

At the periphery of ‘Ancient Thirteen’, where the meteorites were densest, almost a thousand dazzling pillars of light darted close like spears, catching the exploration fleet unprepared. It was the ‘Battle Fortress Phantom Sea’, one of the five supreme fortresses that was best at stealth and mental attacks!

The two fortresses were attacking the exploration fleet from the front and the back. They did not conceal themselves at all, nor did they calculate any route of retreat. Instead, they charged at the exploration fleet unstoppably at the highest speed and with the fiercest bombardment. A lot of space shuttles and crystal suits were released from the two supreme fortresses, too. They were like dense bees, trying to draw close to the exploration fleet under the cover of cannon fire and meteoroids in the most chaotic and time-consuming ‘boarding battle’.

“Has Lu Qingchen… gone mad?”

Li Yao’s eyes were wide open, watching the absurd scene. He had no idea what was on Lu Qingchen’s mind at all.

Although the five supreme fortresses were impressive and the culmination of thousands of years of resources and technology of the Holy Alliance, in the end, they were just five ‘idiots’ with only one function.

Modern Warfare was more complicated and precise than ever. What mattered most was cooperation and system. In order to bring out the best performance of the Supreme Battle Fortress, countless divine shields, arsenals, and Giant God Soldiers were necessary. Lu Qingchen had none of them. Even the maintenance and operation of the Supreme Battle Fortress were not very satisfactory.

What was even more critical was that this was a ‘ferocious place’ where the meteoroid torrents were fierce and the magnetic field was extremely chaotic. The larger a piece of super-large magical equipment was, the more intense the spiritual energy reaction would be, and the more seriously it would be affected. Wasn’t Lu Qingchen afraid that he would suffer the same fate as the ‘Fortress of Earth’ and the ‘Fortress of Fire’, causing the two star fortresses to collapse and be obliterated among the stardust?

Although Li Yao and Li Linghai’s exploration fleet did not carry the most powerful super arsenals, there were more than a hundred of them. Naturally, they were not scared of Lu Qingchen’s final struggle. He was willing to show up and court his own death, which saved everybody a lot of trouble. “You think you can trap us with such a simple trap? Such childish ideas are not your style, Lu Qingchen!”

For the enormous and clumsy star fortresses, the Giant God Soldiers were their natural enemies. Once they crawled into the body of an enemy Giant God Soldier, they would be like a big dumb elephant whose nose had been dug by a small mouse. They would not be able to perform any techniques at all.

The only thing that could stop the invasion of the enemy soldiers was their own soldiers.

However, according to the information that Xiao Ming and Wen Wen gathered, the number of Giant God Soldiers that Lu Qingchen and the five supreme battle fortresses took away was not large. There were definitely no more than ten of them.

The people of the Covenant Alliance were never best known for their individual combat ability. The tactics of their Giant God Soldiers were rigid, and they lacked a distinctive personal style. It was very easy to figure out their pattern. Even in the ‘counterattack of the imperium’, they were not at a disadvantage when they were outnumbered by the Immortal Cultivators. Right now, they were suppressed by the coalition army of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance. Chapter 3013: The Last Supreme Battle Fortress

With the intrusion and destruction of the Giant God Soldiers, especially the destruction of the enemy’s defense array, the spiritual shields on the surface of the two supreme fortresses immediately dimmed, revealing shocking holes that looked like rotten black spots.

The storm-like attacks became sparse, too. One turret after another was demolished by the giant soldier gods mercilessly.

The giant soldier that Lu Qingchen took away did not show up in the battle. There was barely any force inside the two supreme fortresses that could stop Ding Lingdang and the other experts. I won’t let him.”

The radiation and the high-energy particle streams alone would not have been enough to deal serious damage to the Giant God Soldier.

“Sacrificing two Supreme Battle fortresses for nothing just to cause the ‘skin injuries’ of dozens of Giant God Soldiers? Is Lu Qingchen that stupid?”

“Rest assured. We will not enter the core activities of the two supreme fortresses, especially their arsenals and fuel tanks. We will keep a safe distance from them. Whatever tricks Lu Qingchen has up his sleeve, we will cruise on the surface of the supreme fortress and knock out its firing points and defense arrays one by one. “When all the firepower points, turrets, defense arrays, and power units on the surface of the warship have been destroyed so that it is unable to attack, dodge, or run away, we will retreat immediately and let the warship concentrate its fire from a distance, slowly wearing down its defense. However thick its armor is, it won’t be able to resist for long.

“The only problem right now is that the battle between us has disrupted the balance of the spiritual energy. The torrents of meteoroids and the interference of the magnetic field seem to be even more furious and disordered, as if… an unprecedented thunderstorm is brewing. Is this Lu Qingchen’s purpose, not to rely on the two supreme fortresses but to annihilate us with the power of nature so that we will suffer the same fate as the ‘Fortress of Earth’ and the ‘Fortress of Fire’?

Li Yao thought of something. It was the only possibility.

Otherwise, with the incomplete combat system of the two supreme fortresses, which did not even carry any Giant God Soldiers, they would be no match for the hundreds of Giant God Soldiers in the exploration fleet.

Lu Qingchen was well aware of that.

Then, what if he borrowed the strength of ‘Ancient Thirteen’? For example, he could create another collision of the heaven and the earth on the ‘Ancient Thirteen’, which would crush the ‘Ancient Thirteen’, whose structure was very fragile in the first place, and set off a tide of matter, magnetic field, radiation, and high-energy particle streams.

Theoretically speaking, it was indeed a surging tide that could engulf the entire exploration fleet and all the Giant God Soldiers.

The real question was, how could Lu Qingchen blow up ‘Ancient Thirteen’, a satellite more than five thousand kilometers in diameter?

Unless Lu Qingchen had the ability to drill a hole in the crust of ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ in advance and fill it with an astronomical number of crystal bombs, it would be impossible for him to blow ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ into pieces during the explosion. It was impossible. Lu Qingchen had only arrived seven to ten days ago. He had neither the time nor the crystal bombs to carry out such an absurd strategy. Even so, with Li Yao’s reminder, Li Linghai still increased the surveillance between the battlefield and ‘Gu Thirteen’ to ensure that no anomalies were overlooked.

The astronomical parameters of the stone belts —the speed of the meteoroids, the density of the cosmic dust, the intensity of the electromagnetic interference, and so on-were all under high surveillance.

As of now, although the intensity of the interference and the density of the cosmic dust had caused significant damage to the spiritual shield and the power unit of the starship, it was still bearable.

Lu Qingchen’s two supreme fortresses, on the other hand, gradually slowed down and started spinning in an irregular pattern. It was obvious that Ding Lingdang and the other Giant God Soldiers had blown up the main power units, resulting in the imbalance of power output.

The two supreme fortresses were like hippos and rhinos whose legs had been broken. No matter how thick their leather armor was, how powerful their impact was, and how sharp their front teeth and horns were, they were doomed.

Bright fireballs were popping up on the surface of the two supreme fortresses at almost the same time. Even the last bit of their spiritual shield, which was as thin as a cicada’s wing, was gone, revealing their pale and twisted plate armor.

Cheers were echoing inside the communication channel. It turned out that Ding Lingdang and the other experts had successfully completed the task of breaking the main gun, the main defense array, and the main power unit.

In the next moment, although the exploration fleet was small in size, the agile, fast-firing attack starships could swarm forward like piranhas and tear apart the two paralyzed giants.

But Li Yao was frowning harder and harder. The ugly, malicious, deep, and crazy face of Lu Qingchen popped up in his head again.

“Loud thunder, small rain. Lu Qingchen, oh Lu Qingchen, did you spend so much time and effort to set up such a trap at the cost of two supreme fortresses and countless puppets just to buy us more time?” Li Yao felt that it was impossible.

Lu Qingchen was someone who would summon all his will and wisdom to fight to the end even when a wisp of his remaining soul had been captured by ‘Fuxi’. How could he declare his abandonment with such a suicidal, idiotic tactic?

Where was the Supreme Battle Fortress and the ‘Withering Wood Fortress’ hidden?

If Lu Qingchen had already entered the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor, there was no need for him to bring such a huge fortress with him. Therefore, was the fortress hiding somewhere to ambush them?

However, the Supreme Battle Fortress could not reverse the situation even if it joined the battlefield.

So, so—

Cold sweat was appearing on Li Yao’s forehead.

He raised his voice and asked Li Linghai.

Li Linghai’s voice was full of confusion and uneasiness, too. “It was a weird battle. Lu Qingchen’s traps were too simple and weak. Was that all he could do about the five supreme fortresses, or…”

Right then, they both heard the earsplitting alarms.

Discovering the unusual super-high-energy reaction, the last fortress showed up, but it was not in the dense meteoroid belt in front and behind them, nor was it in the dark vacuum between the meteoroid belt and the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’. Instead, it was in the space between the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ and the ancient star, which was closer to the gravitational ring of the ancient star.

In the fierce battle a few hours ago, everybody’s attention had been attracted by the ‘Fortress Phantom Sea’ and the ‘Fortress Sharp Gold’. Even Li Yao and Li Linghai, who could smell that something was not right in the battlefield, had spread all their attention between the stone belts and the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’. They were worried that Lu Qingchen was planning to make use of the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’, but none of them paid any attention to the side of the ancient giant.

It was not until this moment, when the ‘Withered Wood Fortress’ released its strongest spiritual energy and spurted out magnificent exhaust flames thousands of kilometers long, that its high-energy reaction was detected by the exploration fleet.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Countless monitoring crystal cameras turned to the direction of the ancient star and captured thousands of pictures from various angles, assembling a soul-stirring and unbelievable scene.

The ‘Withering Wood Fortress’ was the weirdest of the five supreme fortresses in the first place. It did not seem to be made of metal. Instead, it seemed to have been refined by the Kuafu Clan in the primeval era with vines as thick as boas on the surface.

But right now, the ‘vines’ had been embedded into a giant meteoroid and tied the meteoroid to the fortress.

The distance was too far for him to analyze the diameter and mass of the meteoroid. But judging from the comparison between it and the Dry Wood Fortress, the ratio of the two was at least ten to one. The meteoroid was ten times larger than the Dry Wood Fortress. It was not a meteoroid at all but an asteroid!

Since the very beginning, the Dry Wood Fortress had been ‘bound’ to the planet. It had been floating in the periphery of the ancient star’s gravity circle quietly. It had even entered the ancient star’s gravity circle and was slowly falling toward it. Since the surface of the Dry Wood Fortress was covered in a lot of exotic materials that were neither metal nor iron, it was very difficult to be detected by metal probe magical equipment. Also, with such a large meteorite as cover, nobody had expected that it would be hiding in this place.

It was not until the ‘Sharpen Gold Fortress’ and the ‘Phantom Ocean Fortress’ jumped out of the darkness and attracted the attention of more than 90% of the firepower, computational ability, and probe magical equipment of the exploration fleet that the Dry Wood Fortress activated the main power unit and spurted out the strongest exhaust flames, pushing the meteorites ten times larger than itself to accelerate toward the ancient giant!

Chapter 3014: Super Electric Torrent

“Stop it!”

Li Yao and Li Linghai’s roars echoed inside the cockpit of every starship.

Although they did not understand why Lu Qingchen would sacrifice the Dry Wood Fortress and push a gargantuan meteoroid toward the ancient star instead of ‘Ancient Thirteenth’, whose structure was weaker and whose mass was smaller, Li Yao, Li Linghai, and all the experts in the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage were all alarmed.

Lu Qingchen was definitely not talking nonsense. What they saw was definitely his sharpest killing move.

There was no time.

Lu Qingchen had taken every detail into consideration and even captured their subtle thoughts precisely. He calculated that the exploration fleet would focus their attention on the two fortresses named ‘Phantom Ocean’ and Sharpen Gold’ and ‘Ancient Thirteenth’, and that they would not notice the vast vacuum on one side of the ancient star at all.

It did make sense now that he thought about it. The ancient giants were the kings of the planetary families. Although they had suffered heavy losses in the two supernova explosions and were in the active period when their structure was extremely unstable, they were still not something that the insignificant human fortresses could shake. Therefore, none of the fast attack starships were deployed near the gravity field of the ancient giants and could stop the furious advance of the Dry Wood Fortress.

In the blink of an eye, thousands of colorful spears of light were released from the hundreds of fast-attack starships and darted toward the Dry Wood Fortress. However, they were too far away from each other. When the furious energy exceeded the effective range, the magnetic field under their control was soon gone. The fatal rays of light were scattered and blurred.

Even though they could hit the Dry Wood Battle Fortress, the Supreme Battle Fortress had always been known for its thick skin and high defense ability. Just now, when Ding Lingdang invaded the fortress with dozens of Giant God Soldiers, it had taken her quite a few hours to destroy the defense system. The iron-like plate armor of the fortress had not been completely torn open yet, not to mention that spiritual energy weapons were merely tickling the plate armor of the Dry Wood Battle Fortress at such a long distance.

As for physical weapons such as spiritual bullets and space torpedoes, they were too far away and too slow to catch up with the Dry Wood Fortress.

Besides, even if he did, so what?

So what if the Dry Wood Fortress was riddled with holes because of the bombardment of the light beams and the explosive bullets? So what if it completely collapsed? Its acceleration had reached the limit, and it resonated strongly with the gravitational field of the ancient planet. It was like an unstoppable tide that had returned to the ocean. Nothing could stop it.

Li Yao, Li Linghai, Li Jialing, Boss Bai, Long Yangjun, Ding Lingdang… All the experts could do nothing but watch the thousands of spears of light explode at the rear end of the Dry Wood Fortress, raising clusters of brilliant fireworks, which seemed to add a few points of acceleration to the fortress and accelerated the meteoroid that it was pushing forward. In the end, the meteoroid turned into a red light spot and pierced into the atmosphere where the ancient giant was filled with hydrogen, helium, and methane!

The point of impact of the Dry Wood Fortress was one of the most conspicuous ‘Great Red Spot’ on the ancient giants.

The so-called Great Red Spot was the largest cyclone in the atmosphere of the ancient giants. It could be more than ten thousand kilometers in diameter. It was the most magnificent and the most intense weather change among all the planets with atmospheres.

Since the atmosphere of the ancient giants contained a lot of red phosphorus and methane, the high-speed friction of the tornadoes would release burning redness. Therefore, if observed from the orbit, they looked like bloody mouths all over the ancient giants.

Pushing the meteoroid into such a bloody mouth was like a bug landing in a swamp. At first, there was not even the tiniest splash. “That’s it?”

Li Yao and the other experts looked at each other in bewilderment, not knowing what the strategy was.

се

If the Supreme Battle Fortress was pushed to the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’, it was possible that the structure of the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ would be completely altered during the fierce collision. The furious energy stored in the core of the ‘Ancient Thirteenth’ would be activated, and a new ‘Great Collision of Heaven and Earth’ would be created. But attacking the ancient giant, the Great Red Spot, would be like tickling a dinosaur with a grass. There would be no reaction at all.

However, ten seconds later, they realized that they were wrong.

The Great Red Spot, which had swallowed the Dry Wood Battle Fortress, suddenly expanded into almost a hundred new cantilevers at a visible speed, as if the dynamic balance that it had maintained for hundreds of years had been broken and its speed of revolution increased by three to five times.

“This is—”

All the experts, including Li Yao and Ding Lingdang, felt that their blood was freezing.

Such terrifying thunderstorms and torrents of plasma were similar to the extreme weather conditions when they rescued Iron Fist. But the energy released this time was hundreds of times more than last time.

Just think about it—the tens of thousands of raging lightning bolts, each of which was at least tens of thousands of kilometers long and almost a hundred kilometers in diameter, had an immeasurable range of destruction. Yet, they were all condensed together and exploded into dazzling sparks!

The brightness and the radiation, the interference of the magnetic field, and the flow of high-energy particles were comparable to the tongues of fire when a star was shooting at an entire galaxy.

It was the unbelievable, apocalyptic scene that Li Yao and the exploration fleet were faced with.

“It’s impossible. Such intense magnetic field disturbances and energy releases can only happen when a super-large meteoroid strikes a planet. The Dry Wood Fortress and the large meteoroid are far from enough to trigger such a destructive thunderstorm!”

“It doesn’t make sense. The magnetic field released by the energy is too weird. It seems to be targeting us. Why? The atmosphere is the most suitable environment for the scourge of lightning. Why did the lightning condense into a torrent of plasma and soar into the vacuum? It doesn’t make sense!”

All the astronomers, meteorologists, and spiritual energy scientists screamed in shock.

In the operation to rescue Iron Fist just now, the torrent of plasma had already made their building of faith on the verge of collapse. But the super torrent of plasma right in front of their eyes, which was hundreds of times more powerful and out of the atmosphere of the ancient giants, had blown the ‘undefinable truth’ that they had built up over the past hundred years into smithereens!

Several meteorologists screamed at the same time.
