The self-detonation of the two supreme fortresses did not deal much damage to the exploration fleet. It was even safe to say that the two self-detonations that Lu Qingchen had orchestrated were not designed to maximize the damage of the warship.

Under the ravaging torrents of energy, the shells of the two supreme fortresses caved in at first, before they protruded out violently. All the starships reported to him one after another.

A non-aggressive spiritual energy ripple was like the eternal radiation of a star in the sea of stars. Since it was not very sharp, it was difficult to find a way to neutralize it and disperse it.

Although the spiritual energy ripples were not aggressive by themselves, they were fatally attractive to the super plasma torrents that were bursting out of the ancient giants. When the super plasma torrents sensed the spiritual energy ripples, they were like sharks that had smelled the scent of blood. They were faster and more precise, and they lunged at the battlefield precisely.

He sacrificed the last three supreme fortresses in order to lure the exploration fleet into the ‘hunting range’ of the ‘super plasma torrent’. Then, he would use the ‘blood’ from the self-detonation of the supreme fortresses to attract the devils lurking on the ancient giants and swallow the entire exploration fleet!

Chapter 3015: Plasma Lifeform

Li Yao’s eyes were bloodshot, and he gnashed his teeth. “Haven’t you noticed yet? This cluster of… ‘lightning creatures’ are searching for their target with energy waves. The spiritual shield, spiritual magnetic field, and exhaust flames of our starship are all releasing energy to the outside world unstoppably. It is as idiotic as dancing and shouting in a silent dark forest!

“It is a pure body of energy, and its speed is much higher than ours. If the stalemate continues, even if we activate all our defensive magical equipment, our spiritual shield and spiritual magnetic field will still be blown up by it. We might as well take the risk!” “How do I fight?”

Boss Bai joined the discussion, his voice unprecedentedly solemn. “Turn off the spiritual shield and the magnetic field. We can only last half an hour to an hour in such a furious stone belt. If we cut off all the impetus, there is no way that we can get out. We are like dry leaves in the middle of a raging ocean. We can only drift with the current. In the end, won’t we be blown to smithereens?”

“Do you want to bet that it will perish or retreat in half an hour?”

“If it were an ordinary lightning, half an hour would be enough for it to release all its energy. But for this… extremely weird torrent of plasma, I really don’t want to gamble!” “Of course, we have to gamble.”

Li Yao’s lips curled into a crazy smile of confidence. “However, I never like to let other people roll the dice. It’s definitely not my style to wait for the enemy’s victory. I’m going to roll the dice myself!

“Listen, what we are going to do next is of paramount importance. You must listen to my arrangements. This will determine the life and death of everyone and even the rise and fall of the entire civilization!

“Then, turn off all the magical equipment units on all the starships. Boss Bai, just like in your ambush in the Blue Sky Space Zone, turn yourself into a cold meteorite and don’t even fart.

Once the spiritual shield and the power output are turned off, you will be swept away by the torrent of materials released by ‘Ancient Thirteen’ at any moment. However, there is no other choice. You have to hold on for half an hour to forty-five minutes. Even if your starship is riddled with holes, even if somebody is seriously wounded by the meteorites, you must not move!

We cannot allow him to control the core strength of the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor!” “Li Yao, are you going to distract it?”

Ding Lingdang’s voice joined in, “Are you crazy? This thing is at least a thousand kilometers in diameter. It’s like a tiny sun. Its core temperature is at least ten thousand degrees. Once you are swallowed by it, you will immediately be burnt to ashes. Not even half of your telepathic thoughts will be able to escape!”

“I know. But we don’t have a better solution right now. Hiding in this place is a dead end. We might as well try our luck.”

Li Yao paused for a moment and said in a comforting tone, “Besides, uh, I know what I’m doing. After my most thorough calculation, the risk factor is not as high as it seems. It is searching for its prey with spiritual waves. As long as I can control the output of my spiritual flames precisely and conceal all my spiritual energy and vitality when necessary, I will be fine. I will be fine!”

Chapter 3017: Luring The Thunder!

“Then I’ll go with you!” Ding Lingdang said without any hesitation, “Let’s distract it together. If anything goes wrong, we can make up for it!” “There’s no need. You’ve seen its speed and energy level. If my calculations are correct, one person is enough to lure it away. If… there are errors in my calculations, then it doesn’t matter how many people there are.” Li Yao smiled and said, “You just entered the Divinity Transformation Stage not long ago. You are the type to soar into the sky. Although the spiritual energy inside your body is immense, your control over it is still not good enough. It is very easy for it to detect the feeble spiritual waves that you release. You are all the same. Nobody is a match for me in the field of delicate control over spiritual energy and extreme micro-manipulation. “Therefore, you should focus on your mission. After I distract it, try your best to control the starship, enter the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor, and capture Lu Qingchen. It is an equally important mission.

“As for me, well, I’m not bragging, but as the best expert of the Pangu Universe, when I want to flee, I will not be stopped even if billions of deities work together!

After Li Yao finished his speech, he cut off the communication without giving Ding Lingdang and the others any chance to argue. On the surface of the ‘Arsonist’, interweaving fluorescent red lines appeared again. All the red lines gathered in his eyes and blossomed in glamorous colors.

The airtight door below the launch platform slid open silently. After a short fall, he was already used to the weightlessness. Li Yao stood in the dark, deep sea of stars again, feeling free and refreshed.

The stars were where he belonged.

Fighting a torrent of plasma more than a thousand kilometers in diameter with a temperature of more than ten thousand degrees alone was a battle worthy of his courage and wisdom!

Of course, before the battle, he would have to borrow some ‘toys’ from ‘Falcon’ and the few fast attack starships nearby.

Under Li Yao’s suggestion and Li Linghai’s command, all the commanders of the exploration fleet gritted their teeth and stopped all the operations of the starship. It could not be helped. In the unpredictable and brutal unknown space, the intuition of a division expert might be the only weapon that they could count on.

The spiritual shields and magnetic fields of the starships immediately vanished. The power units that had been glittering and spurting colorful flames all over the starship dimmed, too.

Inside the cabin, the artificial gravity field had been canceled. The crew members in helmets and armor were all floating in midair, securing themselves to their posts with soft restraints and buffer gel. But they could not stop the water cups, mini crystal processors, and daily necessities from dancing in the air.

The Spiritual Nexus and the tactical data link between starships had been cut off, too. They were now in the absolute ‘communication silence’. Every pathway and every cabin was plunged into darkness. Everybody could only adjust the emergency lights to the minimum brightness so that their blurred shadows would be illuminated. They prayed silently in the darkness, praying that Li Yao’s judgement was correct and that their plate armor was solid enough to resist the increasingly intense meteoroid showers.

BAM! BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM BAM!

Without the neutralization of the spiritual shield and the interference of the magnetic field, the meteoroid showers immediately hit the shells of every starship like hailstones. Earsplitting noises were echoing nonstop, indicating that the shell of the starship, the keel, or the girders of the starship were under tremendous pressure and had reached the limit of metal fatigue. They were moaning in desperation. If one was unlucky, one could even hear the hissing noises, which indicated that the shell of the starship had been torn apart in an unknown place, and the gas inside the cabin was leaking out quickly. The sea of stars was boundless, and the stone belts were in chaos. Billions of tons of materials were spurting out of the broken crust of ‘Ancient Thirteen’ every second and rushing into space like mud slides. The starships of human beings were merely dry leaves in the mud slides. Without any impetus, they could not control their own fate at all and were soon dispersed, losing their direction.

The sweeping torrents of super plasma, on the other hand, created a terrifying accelerating magnetic field in the stone belts, which was equivalent to the creation of powerful ‘electromagnetic cannons’ out of thin air. Not only did the meteoroids, which were already as fast as lightning, increase their speed by a level, their revolutions were also hundreds of times faster.

They drilled through the shell, the cabin walls, the pathways, and even the entire starship without any trouble.

The noises grew more and more ear-piercing. Many starships lost pressure and cooled down quickly. A thin layer of frost appeared on the mainframe crystal processor and the units. Some unlucky starships were hit by the fuel tanks. Liquid fuel and coolant leaked out like blood.

At the rate of the damage, there was no way that they could survive forty-five minutes to an hour.

Half an hour was already his limit.

But other than gritting their teeth and enduring the damage within the minimum range, there was nothing they could do, because the destructive super plasma torrent was right next to them, eyeing them covetously!

“What… an unparalleled beast!”

Maybe only those like Li Yao, who was floating alone in the sea of stars, could truly feel its soul-stirring beauty and danger when there was no barrier between the super plasma torrent and the vacuum.

It was truly like a newborn sun, a certain creature that had broken out of its cocoon and turned into a butterfly. Billions of lightning bolts outlined its nerves, veins, veins, bones, and muscles, and every lightning bolt was bouncing and changing into billions of forms every second, trying new methods of entanglement and condensation, which were… mutations and evolutions. Thousands of mutations and evolutions were taking place every second, turning every lightning swirl on its body into ever-changing kaleidoscope.

Li Yao was deeply immersed in the dangerous blue dream. His eyes had been dyed blue and purple without him knowing.

As he expected, the super plasma torrent was indeed searching for prey with the energy waves.

Or rather, its instincts drove it to look for the objects that contained intense energy. Only by seizing all the energy could its ‘life’ be continued.

For the items that had run out of energy, such as the fast attack starship that had been paralyzed in the vacuum just now, it was meaningless, if not against its ‘instinct’, to waste its precious energy to crush them completely. It was just like how ferocious beasts would not attack a hard stone in vain.

In the vacuum of the universe, every drop of energy was more precious than water in a desert. Even though it seemed magnificent and beautiful, as time went by, it would gradually dissipate and eventually perish.

It had to seize enough energy before it was annihilated in order to be considered a successful ‘hunting’.

At this moment, it was hesitating, as if it was deeply troubled by the sudden disappearance of its prey.

Even though Li Linghai, Ding Lingdang, and Boss Bai could turn off all the units of the starship in time, the remaining spiritual waves would not dissipate instantly. The temperature of the starship was also spreading out radiation nonstop.

The spiritual energy ripples and the heat radiation were all keenly perceived by the super plasma torrent. It was merely calculating whether or not it was worthwhile to launch an attack at the cost of its own spiritual energy in order to reduce the energy source by several levels.

Li Yao could not wait for it to react.

He was going to help it make a choice.

Li Yao snapped his fingers.

Thousands of spiritual bullets of the main gun of the starship were floating around the ‘Arsonist’. They were all designed to pierce through the heavy plate armor that was more than ten meters thick. They were the ‘toys’ that Li Yao used to attract the super plasma torrents. The first spiritual energy bullet exploded silently.

Although soundwaves and blast waves could not be transmitted in the vacuum, colorful spiritual waves were still spreading out like a tide, as if the most brilliant fireworks had bloomed in front of Li Yao.

Licking his dry lips, Li Yao mumbled to himself with a weird smile, his eyes sunken like those of a gambler who was taking a gamble.

Even the vivid ‘blue warships’ outlined by lightning were aimed at Li Yao’s direction.

Faced with the horrifying scenes where the common Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators were all scared out of their wits, Li Yao, on the other hand, roared like a wild animal, his hair standing on end and his eyes bloodshot, “Come on. I am your real opponent. Let me see what you are capable of. Let me show you the most brilliant escaping expert in the hundred thousand years of history of the civilization of mankind. Let’s see how magnificent he is after he burns his life away, transcends his peak, and breaks his limits!”

Chapter 3018: Speed Of Life And Death

Enhanced by the magical equipment, Li Yao’s roars turned into sharp electric arcs and slashed at the super plasma torrent brutally.

The thousands of cannonballs around him were accelerated by his life force, too. Dozens of them rushed out and exploded on the super plasma torrent.

The ‘incinerator’ of the Giant God Soldier, on the other hand, was emitting overwhelming spiritual flames, enlarging him into an awe-inspiring giant who was standing on the stars.

Bluffing was Li Yao’s specialty in the first place. Now that he was in the Soul Division Stage, he had trained the technique to the acme of perfection. The spiritual waves that he released right now were as powerful as a small fleet!

The super plasma torrent had indeed been fooled.

After a moment of consolidation and transformation, it lunged at Li Yao like a Lightning Snake King with thousands of heads.

When the lightning thousands of kilometers long suddenly expanded above his head into tens of thousands of kilometers long and hundreds of kilometers wide, it was not an exaggeration to say that every cell in Li Yao’s body was about to melt.

If the liquid crystal processor ‘Little Black’ had not covered his body and protected every inch of his skin, his hair and hair would’ve been burnt.

“Run!”

Li Yao screamed and stomped on a meteorite that had been locked onto for a long time. He suddenly accelerated and dashed forward in the stone belt.

Behind him was lightning and thunder, and in front of him was a rain of meteorites. Riding the ‘Arsonist’, Li Yao performed the most thrilling and marvelous escape in his life.

Manipulated by him, the giant soldier weighing hundreds of tons was as light as a dry leaf, as agile as a butterfly, and as agile as a spark. It turned into a streak of redness and treated the meteorites as stepping stones. With the help of the energy from the annihilation of the meteorites, it accelerated, accelerated, and accelerated!

The thunder was roaring. The sharp claws behind him reached out time and time again, only to brush past them narrowly. The hundreds of meteoroids were merely crushed, vaporized, and obliterated into mist that surrounded the plasma, adding to the unparalleled power and horror of the thunder. Calculating the trajectories of the meteoroids that were coming at him crazily and the route where he would jump and run away after using them as stepping stones consumed 120% of Li Yao’s computational ability. After only three to five seconds of running, his brain was boiling and exploding like magma. In order to cool down his brain, he even asked ‘Little Black’ to extract some professional cooling liquids for him so that he could cool down his brain. Although it was effective, his entire face was smeared with black charcoal.

He did not dare jump out of the stone belt and run into space.

In the empty vacuum, there was nowhere to run to. There was a limit to the speed of the giant soldier. There was no way that he could outrun the torrents of pure energy.

Only in the extremely unstable ‘Ancient Thirteen’s’ halo, the material eruption belt that looked like a mudslide, could he borrow the momentum to accelerate or change his direction time and time again, or he could treat the gravitational field of ‘Ancient Thirteen’ as his own ‘gravity slingshot’, and he could kick large-scale meteoroids into the super plasma torrent to weaken the enemy’s energy.

Li Yao never believed in ghosts. He only believed in objective facts and natural laws.

Conservation of energy was a truth that every existence in the Pangu Universe had to abide by. Regardless of whether or not the super plasma torrent was a real life, it was bound to release a lot of energy when it was crushing and vaporizing the meteoroids. Then, it would not be long before it collapsed and perished. It was a race between life and death.

Li Yao was going to kill it with himself as the bait!

Hiu! Hiu Hiu Hiu Hiu Hiu Hiu!

Spiritual shields, magnetic fields, power units, and even the rune arrays to adjust the temperature inside the giant soldier were all turned off.

ere

However, he could not release his spiritual energy for a long time, either. If so, his small boat, which was riddled with holes, would be wrecked in the sea of lightning at any moment.

That was why Li Yao told Ding Lingdang that it was a mission that only he could carry out.

Li Yao was jumping and dancing among the meteorites, trying to attract the attention of the super plasma torrent so that the enemy would not have time to perceive the situation of the exploration fleet.

It was a protracted tug-of-war.

In only a few seconds, his spiritual energy had soared from the highly-concealed state to the burning state. Both the human body and the Giant God Soldier were in excruciating pain.

It was like heating a metal from -100°C to -2,000°C in the blink of an eye, almost reaching the melting point, and then dropping to -0,000°C in the blink of an eye. The process was repeated dozens of times. Although it was not obvious on the surface, the metal elements, the spiritual energy fuel, and every cell on Li Yao’s body were all covered in tiny cracks.

Li Yao felt that he had turned into a blue and white porcelain bottle. If he was hit by the meteoroid again, he would be completely shattered.

But the super plasma torrent was not any better.

Li Yao’s eyes almost pierced through the ‘Arsonist’s’ light beam. No. It was not an illusion. The probe magical equipment of the ‘Arsonist’ had recorded its parameters clearly. Its size, brightness, and central temperature were indeed much lower than before.

Right now, it was only seven to eight hundred kilometers in diameter. Its main color had changed from bright purple to deep blue. A lot of lightning had withered and perished during the bombardment. The hideous snake heads had been chopped off by Li Yao’s will!

Every second, its energy was flowing and dispersing at a visible speed. Its magnetic field could no longer restrain its regular body. Like an out-of-control cancer, it released fuzzy halos and spread out.

It was tired, hungry, hungry, exhausted, and on the verge of death.

At this moment, forty-five minutes had passed since the hunting and the escape began.

After such a long time of bombardment from the meteoroid showers, all the starships were on the verge of collapse and wouldn’t be able to hold on for another second. They had to activate the faltering spiritual shields and magnetic fields and drive out of the stone belts with the strongest impetus.

Then, this was the moment of the final battle. As he expected, as soon as the thought popped up in his head, the arsonist detected that almost a hundred light spots far away from him had suddenly exploded, shaking off the powder all over their bodies and opening their wings like fireflies.

Chapter 3019: Lightning Life!

One minute, five minutes, ten minutes.

Run, run. He had to run even if his blood was being vaporized. He had to run even if his muscles were being torn apart and his bones were burning. He had to run even if his bones were being ground into powder. He had to run even if his cells were withering. Even if he was the only one left, even if the sky and the earth turned black and white, even if there was no end to the road ahead, even if the finish line was just an even more rugged racetrack, he had to run, run, and run!

Because the journey of life was a never-ending and gratifying one!

Sharp buzzes and screams echoed in his ears, which were the moans of the pyromaniacs who were about to be paralyzed because of Li Yao’s fury.

Li Yao had no intention of slowing down at all. He was still sprinting at the highest speed with his back facing the enemy at a frequency of seventeen to eighteen times per second. The corners of his mouth were curled into a smile, too, except for the dense white smoke that was spurting out.

The super plasma torrent was no longer working

Li Yao had no time to calculate the precise time, but the life-and-death race with the stone belts as the racetrack had lasted at least two hours. During the long time, the super plasma torrent had not only had to control its own magnetic field to change its direction in a fixed pattern, but it had also crushed countless meteorites. Its ‘life force’ was draining away at a visible speed.

Right now, it was no longer as dazzling, enormous, and active as it had been in the beginning. It was as clumsy as a beached whale and as haggard as an old wolf that was marching forward in the cold wind, hungry. It turned from bright purple to deep purple, and from deep purple to deep blue. Occasionally, it would be mixed with orange ripples. In the end, all the colors faded away and turned into a lackluster gray.

Its diameter had shrunk from a thousand kilometers to dozens of kilometers, to the point that it could be cut in half by Li Yao’s saber. Of course, even if it was only a few kilometers away from self-destruction, Li Yao could not even lift his little finger to cut it.

It was the same. In the beginning of the chase, it had been releasing electric arcs extravagantly. As long as there was the slightest chance, it would release thousands of electric arcs to form an overwhelming electric net to capture Li Yao. But now, even at a distance within its reach, it was barely able to release even one miserable electric arc.

There was no telling who was the hunter and who was the prey between the pursuers and the fugitives. Maybe they had forgotten the significance of chasing and hunting and simply ran and ran habitually.

Suddenly, just before the super plasma torrent was about to be extinguished

Hum!

The main power unit on the left side of the ‘Arsonist’ crystal reactor made a low noise and spurted out pungent black smoke, which condensed into a series of black balloons in the vacuum.

Li Yao’s well-trained muscles and bones were not even enough to support the iron body of the giant soldier. They collapsed because of him!

His mind was in a mess, and his brain was almost blank. Li Yao, who was running for his life by instinct, did not have the time to foresee or deal with the paralysis of the left engine. The ‘arsonist’ staggered and lost control of his direction. The result of the unilateral output force was that he was spinning quickly in the torrent of meteorites, hit by hundreds of them in the blink of an eye, and pierced through by the dying super plasma in the next second.

Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi! Zi!

Every nerve in Li Yao’s body was pierced through by the super electric current and the indescribable pain. He was ‘crystal clear’ because of the electric shock. Every bone, every vein, and every internal organ was clearly illuminated by the electric current.

What was weirder than the super electric arcs was the information. A lot of information flooded into his body through the electric arcs and his brain. Some of it even tried to crawl into his brain cells and leave a deep impression on his soul.

It was a different feeling from possession.

Li Yao didn’t know if he was crazy or not, but he was well aware that the stranger had no intention of possessing him at all. He was merely ‘recording’ Li Yao’s brain and body as tools to record information, just like how human beings carved information on turtle shells, stone plates, and bamboo slips with sharp knives.

It made sense. If the ‘lightning creatures’ considered themselves to be the only natural life form in the universe, Li Yao’s body of flesh and blood and his carbon-based life form might be nothing but a lifeless slate in their eyes.

BOOM! BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM!

Countless thunders exploded inside Li Yao’s brain at the same time, as if ancient human beings were chiseling the slate with stone hammers and spears, carving out the brand of civilization.

He had ‘seen’ it. He had seen the origin of the super plasma torrent, a magnificent and soul-stirring epic, and an endless journey of struggling and exploring in darkness just like the civilization of mankind. His initial assumption was correct. The super plasma torrent, including the one that tried to attack Iron Fist earlier, was indeed a special life form born and raised in the atmosphere of the ancient giants.

The atmosphere of the ancient giants boasted thousands of times more gravity than that of Empyreal Terminus. They were full of fatal elements such as liquid hydrogen and methane. The super storms that swept over the planet over the years even changed the appearance of the planet permanently, turning the surface of the planet into parallel giant ribbons. In the places where the wind congregated, enormous cyclones more than ten thousand kilometers in diameter were formed, each of which could last hundreds of years! In the extremely harsh environment, no carbon-based life forms, not even the archaea that could survive in undersea craters, could be born and live in this place.

However, life would always find the most suitable form and stubbornly break out of the soil.

In the Thunder Ocean, where even the anaerobic fungi could not survive, a new life form entirely different from the carbon-based life form appeared with ‘lightning’ as the carrier!

Chapter 3020: Poem Of Life

No one knew exactly how the lightning life-forms appeared, just as no one knew how the organic molecules such as the original amino acids were born from the crazy collisions and intense reactions of inorganic objects. In short, in the atmosphere of the ancient giants over the past billions of years, intense energy waves had been released and detonated in the form of lightning countless times. By unbelievable coincidences, the lightning captured some unusual particles, which were rare elements from the depths of the ancient giants or from outer space, such as the helium element, etc.

When the powerful lightning pierced through the core of the thallium, it was immediately vaporized by the high temperature into translucent, immaculate plasma balls. The free electrons that carried the negative charge formed its outer layer, and the ions that carried the positive charge formed its inner layer. The inner and outer layers formed an obvious boundary, but they also allowed the energy to circulate and transform freely. It was like the membrane and wall of a biological cell, a great yin-yang that could communicate with the heaven and the earth.

In other words, countless lightning strikes had created a miracle of life, a gaseous, pure-energy, plasma-like ‘cell’.

Of course, such ‘plasma cells’ were different from cells in the sense of a carbon-based life. They did not have any ‘genetic material’.

However, the life form was colorful in the first place. Genetic information did not necessarily need genes to be transmitted. The five definitions of cells—the ability to self-replicate, the ability to transmit information, the ability to metabolize, the ability to grow on their own, and the obvious boundary separating them from the outside world-were all met by the ‘plasma cells’.

Although its ‘life’ could only be maintained for a short period of time in the beginning, during its short and glorious life, it could absorb energy from the outside world and expand or even divide. It could extend the tentacles of lightning and absorb the neutral elements nearby, such as lanthanum, lanthanum, and even methane and ethane. It could add its own energy reactions and emit colorful brilliance. It could even divide itself into two different plasma cells, each of which carried the same electromagnetic features.

It could even transmit information by radiating the energy of electromagnetic waves into other plasma cells so that the atoms inside the plasma cells would be at the same frequency.

If it was not a ‘cell’ and not a ‘life’, what was the significance of the definition of life?

Li Yao noticed that such a marvelous lightning elf had not been around for long, far less than the hundred-thousand-year history of the human civilization. It was even less impressive than the civilization of the Pangu civilization or even the ‘Black Wall Manufacturing’ billions of years ago.

Their ‘history’ was only two to three thousand years old.

Two to three thousand years ago, it was the time when the two high-mass stars near the ancient giants entered the last years of their life and started blossoming violently, showing signs of ‘supernova explosions’.

Maybe, it was the intense spurting of materials and energy before the death of a star that injected new vitality into the atmosphere of the ancient giants and led to the birth of the elves of lightning

They were out-and-out ‘Children of the Sun’.

Although it was only three thousand years ago, it was a journey that had taken billions of years to complete. Their lives were short and intense. Every lightning bolt that pierced through the sky of the ancient giants could only last several seconds to ten seconds at best. In those ten seconds, billions of plasma cells were born in the lightning. The lightning was their river of life and their eternal home. They had to absorb the various elements inside the atmosphere and try new discharge structures and electromagnetic force fields in order to prolong themselves and the lightning. If they failed to do so, they would divide and proliferate crazily, trying to ‘jump’ to another lightning that had just been born before the first one was annihilated.

“Survival, survival, survival!”

Li Yao seemed to be able to hear the newborn plasma cells roaring in endless desire amid the spluttering electric arcs.

It was true that the meaning of life was much more than survival. But if one could not even survive in the beginning, one would not be able to find any meaning no matter how hard one tried.

At the origin of any life, especially at such a harsh and cruel place, survival itself was the only meaning

Every one of Li Yao’s brain cells was wreathed in lightning He ‘appreciated the poem of life that had lasted for hundreds of years.

He ‘saw’ that countless plasma cells were absorbing the elements around them and trying to build the foundation of their life with the vibration frequencies of various elements and atoms.

99.99% of the attempts ended in failure. The plasma cells that absorbed the wrong elements and used the wrong vibration frequency were annihilated instantly. Not even a ripple was left. In exchange, 0.001% of the lucky ones were able to extend their life by 0.001 microseconds.

Before they were annihilated, the lucky ones would immediately transmit the new elements, vibration frequency, and other information to their ‘compatriots’ inside the lightning.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

In the meantime, the ‘compatriots’ were either splitting crazily and increasing the number of the lightning balls exponentially, or jumping out of the lightning like moths to a flame, trying to find another newborn lightning in the ocean of methane and helium so that the entire species could have a few more seconds.

It was in such a crazy attempt that tens of thousands of generations of ‘people’ struggled and sacrificed themselves in less than twenty seconds. Gradually, the survival time of the plasma balls was improved from a few seconds to almost one second. The lightning that they lived in was modified to be different from ordinary lightning and could be maintained for several minutes, dozens of minutes, or even longer. They were even able to attract a few lightning bolts together and weave them into a marvelous 3D geometry in unbelievable ways. Everything they did was for the purpose of improving the survival time of the lightning in the atmosphere and the long-distance transmission ability.

They were building their own ‘space warships and spaceships’. They were refining the lightning into their own starship! Li Yao ‘saw’ that the plasma cells that upgraded the lightning into ‘battleships’ gradually developed self-organization abilities in the intense battlefield of life. Their exploration of the entire planet was no longer limited to the places where the lightning was most intense. Instead, they drove the lightning around the magnetic field and spread to every corner of the ancient star.

Then, they discovered the place where life could leap forward and evolve into a ‘civilization’, which was the most obvious red spot on the ancient giants.

The Great Red Spot was a super cyclone more than a thousand kilometers in diameter that could last more than a hundred years.

Inside the super cyclone, the intense changes in the air pressure could make the electric reaction of the lightning more violent, faster, and more possible. The super cyclones were stirring the materials nearby. A lot of materials that had been absorbed by the ancient giants were spurting out, too. They brought abundant energy and materials from the core of the planet like a volcanic eruption in a primitive ocean and became the cradle of life. They could also bring more abundant elements to the plasma cells.

The ‘red phosphorus’, the special material that constituted the red spot, could even release glamorous, unpredictable flames in the high-speed friction of lightning. It was the carrier of the ‘civilization’.

It was true that every ‘fire of red phosphorescence’ could only last several minutes and a few hours at most. It was ‘in the blink of an eye’ when measured in the time scale of human beings. But for the plasma cells, which could pass on their knowledge to the next generation after only several seconds, the burning of a single flame was long enough. It could be used to make ‘books’ and pass on their wisdom like paper that could not decay for decades.

Just like that, the plasma cells, riding the lightning, looked for the Great Red Spot one after another and thrived, establishing the real ‘Lightning Civilization’. Of course, their civilization was completely different from the civilization established by the intelligent carbon-based creatures. At the very least, 99% of the information that Li Yao read was incomprehensible. He had no idea what the plasma cells were doing, what they were building, or what they were chasing after. On the other hand, if the ‘Lightning Civilization’ could observe the debauchery, love and hatred of human beings, the rise and fall of a nation, heroes and epics, and so on, they wouldn’t be able to understand what the highly self-organized carbon constituents were doing, would they?

However, there were some things that all civilizations shared.

Li Yao ‘saw’ that the plasma cells had ignited red phosphorous flames one after another in the depths of the red spot and recorded specific information with the bouncing flames.

Maybe, it was the discovery of their major research, their understanding of the universe and the universe, their secret arts to pass on their life, and the method to build a ‘lightning warship’ that was even stronger.

It was also possible that the poems in which they chanted ‘belief’ and ‘love’ were merely glamorous, useless show-offs, or even spells based on their origins that were based on the superstitions of the sun.

Just like the lightning that they had tried to modify in the past, they were now modifying the super tornadoes that they were in right now. They controlled the electric current, electric charge, and voltage precisely, the clouds made of helium and methane, and the speed and angle of the storm, just so that the big red spot they were in would be larger, more stable, and last longer. Also, they could absorb more elements from the depths of the ancient giants and carry the more glamorous fire of red phosphorus.

The natural Big Red Spot could only last a hundred years, but after their modification, it could last three to five hundred years. For the Lightning Civilization, it was almost eternal.

Then came the war.

Wars, especially civil wars, were an integral part of the growth of all civilizations.

The war between two large red spots, billions of lightning bolts, and immeasurable plasma cells had begun!

Chapter 3021: Last Struggle

It was a war that no human being—or any carbon-based intelligent life—could imagine.

In the cruel battlefield where the standard gravity of the ancient star was thousands of times higher, two super cyclones, both more than a thousand kilometers in diameter and even more furious than level-13 hurricanes on ordinary planets, collided brutally.

At the edge where the two swirls touched, billions of lightning bolts pierced into each other’s bodies like spears, building a bridge of death leading to doom.

Countless plasma cells lunged at the enemy through the bridge of lightning. They burnt their life force, which was only several seconds long, and released the most intense electromagnetic radiation, bombarding the enemy with their own vibration frequency.

If the plasma cells on the other side could not withstand the ‘explosive bombardment’ of the attacker and changed the frequency of their atom oscillations to the same frequency without them knowing, they would be infected and transformed into one of the attackers.

Just like that, the two clusters of plasma cells were engaged in a ‘short-range battle’. They swallowed and transformed each other or were evenly matched. Neither of them was able to transform the other. But after all the electromagnetic radiation was exhausted, they perished together in a flash.

It was a slaughterhouse of melee combat.

In the battlefield of a higher scale and a larger scale, there were still a lot of plasma cells that did not participate in the battle directly. Instead, they were trying their best to increase the wind pressure and wind speed of the Red Spot they were in. They released the arms of the storm, trying to shatter the enemy’s Red Spot and swallow the super cyclone.

Such ‘war’ could often last days and nights. On the time scale of the plasma cells, it was an epic war that lasted hundreds of years.

In such a protracted war, the fire of red phosphorus on both sides would be burning furiously, increasing the brightness of the two red spots by one level.

Maybe, they were the poets, singers, and historians of the Lightning Civilization who were singing, lamenting, and recording the brilliant, the cruel, the sad, the touching, the magnificent, the lamentable, and the meaningless war! In the end, one of them was defeated, and all the lightning was extinguished. The fire of red phosphorus dimmed, and the vibration frequency of all the plasma cells was changed to that of the winners. Even the red spot where they lived was shattered and swallowed by the red spot, turning into a super red spot more than five thousand kilometers in diameter.

The strong preyed on the weak. Survival of the fittest was a natural law even in the world of energy life.

The war between the Great Red Spot and the Great Red Spot lasted a thousand years.

Countless civilizations in the Great Red Spot had been wiped out. More civilizations were born in the brand-new Great Red Spot. Of course, the ‘Lightning Empire’, which had been flourishing for a long time, had developed to its limits, too. But the Great Red Spot, which had been thriving for a long time, had reached the limit of its life. It was on the verge of collapse, and the civilization that had lasted for billions of generations was destroyed.

All in all, at the moment when Lu Qingchen, Li Yao, and the exploration fleet of human beings reached the periphery of the ancient star, the civilization of lightning had developed into an unprecedentedly prosperous civilization.

Basically, the lightning creatures were already able to condense and control a lot of plasma cells and construct all kinds of delicate and complicated structures. They had also explored almost all the big red spots in the atmosphere of the ancient giants and occupied every fertile land that was suitable for the development of a civilization. They also boasted the preliminary ability of sailing in the sea of stars. They could build ‘Lightning Warships’ that were almost a thousand kilometers long, get rid of the attraction force of the ancient giants, and stop briefly near the ancient giants, which were the glorious and terrifying forms that Li Yao had seen.

However, that was the limit. The world they were born in played a cruel joke on them. Their civilization had only just been established. Two thousand years was not even a moment in the universe, and they were about to meet their doom.

Or rather, when the first plasma cell woke up from ignorance and struggled to survive, they had been sealed by the destiny of desperation.

It was the omen of a supernova explosion that brought a lot of rare elements and special radiation to the ancient giants. That was how such a miraculous life form was created and evolved into a unique civilization.

However, the complete explosion of a supernova would seriously affect the ancient giants and destroy the entire world that they relied on.

They were creatures living in lightning, and the birth of lightning could not be achieved without energy, magnetic field, and atmosphere. The loss of atmosphere and magnetic field was already a catastrophe that they could not bear, let alone the collapse of the ancient giants. The lightning life-form had keenly sensed the magnetic field changes of the ancient giants decades ago.

A few months ago, the appearance of the ‘Gold Saint, Heaven’s Horizon’ suddenly changed the gravity and magnetic field of the ancient giants and their satellite systems. The living environment of the lightning-type creatures was even worse.

ave

A few months were like thousands of generations of people’ for the lightning creatures. Of course, they were trying to save themselves, survive, and fight against the apocalypse. However, no matter how they changed themselves or the forms of the lightning and the Great Red Spot, they could not stop the collapse of the ancient giants and the annihilation of the magnetic field, much less the explosion of the two supernovas.

In a sense, the lightning creatures were creatures on the micro level. How could they stop the supernova explosions and the destruction of planets on the macro level?

Then, there was only one way.

Run away. Run away. Run away without caring about anything else. Run away from the ancient giants and turn from ‘planetary creatures’ into ‘cosmic creatures’, just like the carbon-based intelligent creatures such as human beings had tried before.

Planets were merely the cradles of life. The sea of stars was the home of life.

The lightning life-form spent about a month experimenting with various plans to ‘save the ancient planet’ and came to the conclusion that it could not. Then, it spent another month and countless generations of ‘people’ exploring various methods of sailing in the sea of stars.

It was a pity that their lives were too short. Countless generations of ‘people’ could not find a macroscopic answer.

It was almost impossible for him to cross the resource-scarce zones that were hundreds of lightyears away with pure energy alone. He would collapse, dissipate, and be obliterated within several hundred million kilometers.

The mass of the ancient giants was too large, and their gravity was too strong, which prevented them from refining all kinds of materials into solid and stable shells that could carry extremely active energy.

It was an unsolvable problem. However, the lightning creature had no other options except to try again and again, driven by its survival instincts.

The appearance of Lu Qingchen, Li Yao, and the exploration fleet of human beings gave them a slim chance of survival.

It was beyond Li Yao’s expectation that his initial assumption was wrong. The lightning creatures’ attack on the exploration fleet was not ‘hunting’, or rather, ‘exploration’ on a different level.

It did make sense now that he thought about it. Endless energy was still stored inside the ancient giants at this moment. If it were just for temporary survival, the lightning life-forms would not have had to leave the atmosphere and gravity and fly millions of kilometers away to swallow the exploration fleet.

If the ancient giants were in a stable state, no matter how close the exploration fleet was to them, the lightning creatures would not be interested in human beings at all, just like human beings would not be interested in cold rocks.

But right now, it was different. The ancient giants were about to be destroyed. The lightning-like creatures, who had nowhere else to run, were not willing to let go of any chance of escape. The starships, crystal suits, and giant soldiers of human beings represented a brand-new, stable, special magnetic field and spiritual energy circulation structure that could cross the sea of stars. The model of such a special magnetic field and the algorithm for the operation of spiritual energy were both unprecedented in the billions of generations of genetic mutations of the lightning creatures.

Therefore, the fleet of the lightning creatures sent out their own ‘exploration fleet’, namely, two super plasma torrents.

They were not malicious at all, nor did they feel that they were attacking or destroying, because they did not realize that the exploration fleet of human beings was a kind of ‘life’ at all. In fact, if they had a certain primitive and obscure religion, they might consider the exploration fleet of human beings as a blessing that the ‘god’ bestowed upon them. The ‘god’ would teach them a brand-new structure that could allow them to flee from their home and march into the sea of stars, and the ‘arky’ that could save them from the suffering.

Therefore, when the torrents of super plasma passed by dozens of starships after they crushed the two supreme fortresses, they would take the appearance of the supreme fortresses and the starships. They were indeed learning and mimicking, hoping to find a way to maintain or store the lightning for a longer time.

Li Yao and the exploration fleet, on the other hand, turned off the power source and the spiritual shield time and time again and triggered the most intense spiritual flames. Such a clumsy tactic would work, too, because in the eyes of the lightning life-form, it was perfectly justified and natural. Naturally, the powerful energy would be extinguished after a brief outburst, which meant that ‘death’ was no longer worth exploring.

As for how the energy of ‘death’ could be resurrected, it was far beyond their understanding and understanding.

Even now, when all the plasma cells inside the super lightning torrent were about to be drained and annihilated, it was still carrying out its mission dutifully, exploring and learning how the neural bioelectricity inside Li Yao’s body worked and condensing into a ‘human figure’.

It did not realize that the magnetic field and the energy were not enough for it to jump from the microscopic level to the macroscopic level.

It was just like a stupid but stubborn student who had put in a hundred times more effort and was constantly trying, struggling, and struggling in the wrong direction.

This was the story of the lightning life-forms born from ancient giants. Maybe it was the last words of a civilization, or at least a species, before it perished.

Chapter 3022: The Great Expedition Of A Thousand Generations!

Such a short and brilliant civilization made Li Yao sigh with mixed feelings.

Although he knew that a lightning life-form based on plasma cells could not have the social form, emotional waves, and thinking style of a carbon-based intelligent life-form, Li Yao could not help but imagine the attitude and resistance of the lightning life-form when it was faced with the ‘apocalyptic apocalypse’ in a childish way.

Did the lightning-type creatures have scholars, too? Did they have astronomers and energy scientists? Yes. Even if they could not understand the concepts of astronomy, they must have the concepts of energy science, too, and they must be a hundred times more proficient than the carbon-based intelligent creatures.

Then, decades or even a hundred years ago, before the billions of generations in the time scale of the lightning, had the scholars sensed that they were doomed by the subtle changes of radiation and magnetic field? Had they hesitated, despaired, and worked hard to calculate every route of escape and one billionth chance of survival?

Were there priests in the life of lightning, too? When all the scholars failed to rescue them, would the priests pray to the stars more sincerely, or would they discover in horror that their stars could barely protect themselves and that they might have been killed by the violent outbreaks long before they were born?

It was even possible that they would discover that they were the products of a supernova explosion, that they were the souls of a star, and that they were not the ‘Children of the Sun’, but the ‘Children of Demons who destroyed the Sun’. When the mission of destruction was completed, they would be destroyed, too.

With such a cruel understanding, could the priests still firmly believe in the beginning, or would they ‘transform’ and ‘degenerate’ into believers of darkness, just like the strongest human beings who could not accept the dark truth of the universe and walked into the abyss?

Will the lightning-type creatures have warriors, too? For the sake of their home and their compatriots, for the survival of their race and civilization, the warriors of the lightning-type creatures will sacrifice themselves without any hesitation. Yes, there will be. Their lives will only last a few seconds. They are destined to be a species that is more resolute, cruel, and desperate for a moment than the intelligent carbon-type creatures. In order to achieve their greatest goal-survival – the warriors of the lightning-type creatures will even fly to the failing neutron star in the enormous lightning-type starships, trying to ignite the sun of death with their lives as fuel and restore the stability of the entire galaxy.

Li Yao could not help but imagine such a scene.

Countless lightning bolts were launched from the ancient giants across the long distance and the chaotic radiation belts to the pale neutron stars. Even though the neutron stars were quickly collapsing and cooling down, they did not hesitate at all. They crashed, shattered, and perished time and time again, like moths darting into flames, but they could not give up.

Then, during the decades, or rather, billions of generations, of ‘people’, how were the lightning-like creatures going to face their doom? Were they divided into homers and spacers like the human beings of the Flying Star Sector in the old days? Had they tried countless times to condense a large-scale material starship out of the materials of the ancient giants and escape from the enormous attraction force? Did they have a clear understanding about the vastness, vastness, and barrenness of the sea of stars? Had they engaged in countless debates and explorations? How many heroic and touching stories had popped up in the debates and explorations?

Lastly, the super plasma torrent in front of Li Yao.

No. It was not ‘it’, but ‘them’. It was not ‘one’ lightning creature, not even ‘a bunch’, but a combination of countless generations of lightning creatures, an expedition team, and an expedition army!

Li Yao and the super plasma torrent spent at least two to three hours inside and outside the gravity field of ‘Ancient Thirteen’. If every plasma cell could survive ten seconds, it would be a thousand generations of ‘people’.

A great expedition across a thousand generations!

Li Yao could totally imagine the ecstasy and excitement of the lightning creatures when Lu Qingchen pushed the ‘Fortress of Withering Wood’ into the red spot of the ancient star and detonated the ‘Fortress of Sharp Gold’ and the ‘Fortress of Phantom Ocean’ in the sea of stars so that the lightning creatures could perceive everything clearly.

They must’ve thought that it was a sign from the gods.

However, the enlightenment required endless courage and sacrifice.

An ‘army’ made of the best warriors who were not scared of death immediately gathered together. The entire race produced a gargantuan warship of lightning that was thousands of kilometers long with the purest and most furious energy. The warriors did not have the chance to bid farewell to their family and the plasma cells that boasted the same frequency of atom oscillation before they embarked on a journey of no return.

For a single plasma cell, the ancient giants were too far away from the exploration fleet of human beings. The first generation, the second generation, and even the tenth generation of soldiers of the expedition army were annihilated in the few minutes when the lightning was rushing out. But in the few minutes, they split and created new warriors from the 11th generation to the 100th generation, passing on their ‘mission’ instantly through electromagnetic radiation.

Just like that, after hundreds of generations of plasma cells, they finally reached the exploration fleet, only to fall into Li Yao’s trap and be attracted to the wrong direction.

Locked in the observation blind spot at the microscopic level, they were doomed to fail to see through the traps and plans of human beings. They fell deeper and deeper into the mud.
