The speed of their annihilation was higher and higher, but the speed of their division was lower and lower. The ubiquitous radiation in the sea of stars had seriously interfered with the transmission of electromagnetic radiation, too. The vibration frequency of the atoms inside their bodies was becoming more and more inconsistent. Some of the plasma cells gradually forgot their mission and even lost their wisdom. They fell into ignorance again and turned into ordinary, lifeless sparks.

However,

Although thousands of generations had passed, even though countless warriors had perished, even though they had forgotten their mission and wisdom, even though they were getting further and further away from their home, even though they might lose the opportunity to return to their homeland and rescue their compatriots forever, even though the frequency of their atom oscillations was becoming more and more chaotic, even though more and more information was decaying and dispersing in the vast space, the last batch of warriors still remembered their mission and completed the impossible task. They poured all their energy into Li Yao’s life magnetic field.

At this moment, Li Yao’s vitality magnetic field and the frequency of the lightning were gradually converging and resonating.

Li Yao could completely understand their sad fate and was deeply immersed in the magnificent journey, unable to free himself.

His eyes were filled with tears, which were instantly vaporized by the electric current, making the lightning even more dazzling.

Countless plasma cells were revolving, groping, and simulating his limbs and nerves, which were the ‘coil’ of his life magnetic field. Soon, they crawled out of Li Yao’s pores and formed a small, haggard, shivering human figure in front of Li Yao, too, especially the brain. Billions of plasma cells rushed to the brain and divided and perished, condensing into a marvelous ‘energy brain’.

Li Yao was very clear that it was meaningless.

The brain and the body of flesh and blood of human beings were the most suitable carriers for the intelligent creatures that were based on carbon. But it did not mean that the energy creatures could have the same abilities as human beings just because they simulated the forms of human beings.

Besides, even the super experts among human beings, such as himself, had to rely on the protection of starships in order to travel through the four-dimensional space.

And as an enormous gas-liquid hybrid planet, the enormous attraction force and the overly thin materials were destined to be unable to refine a true warship on its surface—the vast majority of materials would be flattened when they reached the surface of the ancient giant, so how could it be refined and refined?

Li Yao did not know what the lightning creature was going to do to break its destiny and escape.

In his eyes, the poor imitation of the lightning creature was merely a waste of his precious energy and a step closer to the abyss of death.

Such a conclusion made the movement of the lightning creature even more tragic. Li Yao could not help but extend his hand toward the lightning creature. He did not know what he was going to do.

The lightning creature, on the other hand, sensed the subtle changes of the magnetic field inside his body and extended its arms that were wreathed in electric arcs.

The fingertips of the carbon-based life and the energy-based life touched each other softly, producing indescribable sparks. Complicated and intense feelings popped up inside Li Yao’s brain. They were not his own emotions, but the feelings that the lightning creature stimulated his braincells.

It was… sorrow, jealousy, anger, desperation, envy, confusion, and all kinds of other feelings.

Maybe ‘they’ had read it in a certain way, too. At the very least, they had vaguely perceived the information inside Li Yao’s brain, recognized the vastness and cruelty of the universe, and learned the truth of the supernova outbreak and the ending of the ancient planet.

Or maybe, it was the long journey across a thousand generations that made them realize their own insignificance and fragility, and that even the path of escape did not exist.

They were desperate about the end of their civilization. They were envious and jealous of the carbon-based intelligent creatures such as human beings, furious about the natural law that created them and was about to destroy them, and confused about the existence of the natural law.

What was the truth of the universe exactly? Did the Heavenly Dao really exist? Were they some kind of conscious products, at least slaves, toys, or test subjects, or were they just… insignificant coincidences?

They wanted to survive. They released the strongest malice to Li Yao, hoping to annihilate Li Yao, human beings, and all the other intelligent carbon-based creatures in order to survive. But in the very next second, they sent the most pitiful prayers and the purest kindness to Li Yao, praying that the intelligent carbon-based creatures such as Li Yao could help them survive, such as the way they were stored in solid iron cans. If that was not possible, they prayed that Li Yao could at least remember their life form and the frequency of their atom oscillations and their history, their species, and their civilization.

Chapter 3023: Rescue Or Destruction

Watching the lightning life-form struggling silently, Li Yao felt… almost ashamed.

He used to think that the universe was so unfair to human beings that, if there was a ‘heavenly law’, human beings would only be the slaves and toys of the heavenly law. The world in which human beings lived was so cruel that the resources of the Pangu Universe were always scarce, forcing human beings to walk the path of self-destruction or suicide.

But right now, from the lightning-like life of the ancient star, he finally realized what ‘cruel’

was.

Human beings, or most carbon-based intelligent creatures, were lucky.

They lived on two stable planets. Such planets were ‘everywhere’ on the scale of the universe. The natural advantage of carbon-based life meant that every one of them had a relatively long lifespan. They were also able to resist the storms that were passing through the four-dimensional space and the pain of separation and consolidation, which made sailing in the sea of stars possible.

When the advantages were combined, the fire of life on the carbon-based intelligent creatures spread across the entire universe and started burning furiously. It was very convenient for the intelligent creatures in various space zones and even in different universes to communicate with each other. Communication, even in the cruelest ways of war, slavery, and slaughter, would promote the advancement of a civilization.

Just like the civilization of mankind today, they used to have a lot of brothers and sisters, and they had been marching forward in the internal strife with their brothers and sisters. They had also found their ‘father’, the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, who were also carbon-based life forms, and even the makers of the black wall. They had also begun to create the next generation of information life forms that could carefully and gradually get rid of the carbon-based shells.

How happy and fortunate a civilization with parents, brothers, and children was, and how generous and extravagant the universe was about carbon-based life!

But what about the Lightning Civilization? The planets that they were born on were the fewest among the families of planets. Besides, not every planet had an atmosphere, and not every planet’s atmosphere could produce plasma cells.

It would require two neighboring supernovas to explode at almost the same time and sweep countless kinds of matter and radiation into the atmosphere of the planet. The intensity of the radiation would not be strong enough to swallow the planet, which was the only miracle that could be achieved.

With such harsh conditions, not just the Pangu Universe, even in countless multiverse, Li Yao believed that the lightning life-forms from ancient giants were unique.

It meant that they were destined to be alone. They were natural-born orphans. It was impossible for them to receive the help and enlightenment of their predecessors. It was almost impossible for them to communicate with the outside world. They could only grope in the darkness until they fell into the abyss.

Besides, the ‘heavenly path’ left them too little time. Too little time. They were like children who had been infected with a fatal virus when they were still in their mother’s womb. They were sentenced to death the moment they were born. Two to three thousand years later, even though their development and mutation speed far exceeded the limits of the carbon-based intelligent creatures, it was still not enough for the feeble creatures to defeat a giant planet, two supernovas, and the desolate, barren universe!

Therefore, the struggle of the lightning creatures was destined to be a hundred times more difficult, a hundred times more tragic, and a hundred times more splendid than the struggle of the civilization of mankind.

It-they were still struggling.

After only a few minutes, the lightning that constituted the shape of a human became dimmer and smaller.

They were still wandering around Li Yao, trying to crack the secrets of Li Yao’s vitality magnetic field and brainwaves.

However, human beings had been studying the secret for a hundred thousand years and failed to realize it. How could the lightning life-form crack it in only several minutes?

Sensing the electric currents passing through his braincells, Li Yao was caught in a dilemma.

Now that he had recognized the magnificent civilization of the lightning creatures and their magnificent fight against destiny, Li Yao’s first reaction was naturally to help them.

But his rationality also told him that, when he was in touch with an unknown civilization, it was necessary to assume that they were hostile.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Intentionally or unintentionally, the previous two attacks had paralyzed many starships of the human exploration fleet and even killed many crew members.

Just a moment ago, the lightning life-form had been releasing undisguised hostility toward him, trying to possess him clumsily. The lightning life-form was like an innocent child who did not hide his purpose at all. It was the purest, most ultimate ‘evil thought’.

Li Yao had no doubt that, if destroying human beings could save their civilization, the warriors of the Lightning Civilization would definitely do it without any hesitation.

He should annihilate them, shouldn’t he?

However, in the depths of the Pangu Universe, in the multiverse where there were endless possibilities, how many life forms were as wonderful and magnificent as the lightning creatures? Was the civilization of mankind going to sweep across the entire universe and turn the entire universe into a barren desert with a sentence that read ‘Those who are not of the same species will be disloyal’, just like the flood?

Was it possible that the so-called ‘flood’ was a group of super civilizations that believed that ‘those who are not of the same species must have a different mind and strike first to gain the upper hand’. All the unknown civilizations were hostile civilizations, and only the civilizations of destruction were friendly civilizations’.

Was the ultimate purpose of mankind’s march into the sea of stars to become a second flood, or was it to stand on the opposite side of the flood and fight against the flood together with countless brilliant species?

To save or not to save?

How could he save them if he had to? How could he destroy them if he did not?

The ripples under Li Yao’s eyes started trembling violently.

Right then, he suddenly felt that several streaks of lightning as thin as hair had crawled into the depths of his brain and tied up his soul, merging with it.

Some of the plasma cells crawled into the fuel tanks and the crystal reactors of the arsonists and vanished, too.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently.

He really did not know whether it was another failed attempt of the lightning creature, or whether the creatures had grasped secrets that even he did not know during the arduous exploration of dozens of generations and turned his body, soul, and even the giant soldier into a brand-new ‘starship’.

Li Yao was dazed for a long time. Then he smiled. The muscles that were cramping because of the electric arcs all relaxed. He took a long breath in relief and mumbled to himself, “Yes. I was too arrogant to think that I could be your ‘savior’ or ‘destroyer’.

“Compared to the supernova explosions and the collapse of galaxies that are going to destroy you, my strength is nothing. After fighting against such a cruel universe and fate for such a long time, you have become the strongest warriors. You don’t need the rescue of other people at all. You can save yourselves. “Also, how can I destroy you? Even the explosion of two supernovas, the super-giant storm, the super gravity, and even the natural laws of the universe failed to destroy you. How can I destroy you? “Then, let’s do it. Let’s continue fighting with everything we’ve got. Maybe this is the most efficient way of communication and mutual salvation between two different civilizations. Or maybe, this is the greatest respect that a warrior struggling on the road of survival and resistance can offer to another warrior in the same trade!

At this moment, Li Yao closed his eyes and burnt his life, trying to drive out the lightning-like creatures that had invaded his body.

But it was also possible that he was not expelling them but releasing all the information about human beings, the Pangu Clan, the Black Wall Manufacturing, and other carbon-based intelligent creatures. He was also sending his precious energy into the body of the lightning-like creature to prolong its life. It was hard to define whether it was destruction or salvation between different life forms.

By the same logic, the lightning life was shaking violently inside Li Yao’s brain, carving and imprinting It was perhaps malicious. It was trying to erase all the memories and consciousness in Li Yao’s mind in such a way, while it ‘moved its entire civilization inside.

It was also possible that he was trying to teach Li Yao, a fellow traveler on the endless journey, the delicate secret arts of manipulating energy when he sensed that his life was about to end.

It was also possible that it still did not recognize the full picture of a carbon-based intelligent life, nor did it have the concept of good and evil. It was merely trying its best to build a tomb and a monument for its premature civilization between Li Yao’s braincells and the electric current.

Li Yao fell into a trance again, not knowing how long the process lasted. In the face of two civilizations whose time scales were so different, ‘time’ itself was meaningless. In short, when he gradually woke up from the never-before-seen, exquisite, and marvelous lightning energy model, the first picture he saw was the lightning life-form in the shape of a human gradually drifting away from the spiritual residence of the giant soldier and sinking into the depths of the sea of stars.

It seemed to have completed its mission and was ready to welcome its doom. The bouncing electric arcs were dimming, rippling, and dispersing, but the lightning that should’ve been the most furious was as tranquil and clear as a stream.

It opened its ‘arms’ and floated further and further away, becoming smaller and smaller, as if it had really let go of its long hair and sunk into the ocean quietly.

No. Not the ‘undersea’, not the depths of the sea of stars, but in the direction of the ancient giants!

Even though it was so far away from its home, even though its minimal energy was not enough to support a plasma cell to return to the ancient planet, it was still deeply attracted by the magnetic field of its home. It was not falling, drifting, or sinking, but returning to its home.

Chapter 3024: Entrance To A Super Civilization

Li Yao’s nose and chest were blocked by something sour again. With the last bit of his strength, he extended his trembling hand, controlling the arm of the steel giant to bid farewell to the withering lightning “Goodbye, my friend. Although there is not even one billionth of a chance, I still hope that part of you, or at least one plasma cell, can return to the ancient planet and spread the story of our wonderful encounter.

“I should have saved you or destroyed you, but I was struggling for my own survival and destruction, too. I was going to a more mysterious and dangerous place to find my origin, my destiny, and my answer. “Therefore, let’s part ways here and look forward to meeting again in the future.

“After all, today’s encounter was too hasty and embarrassing. I really look forward to the day when we or our descendants can get in touch with each other in a more dignified, calm, and wise way. Or maybe, hehe, we can put on an even more spectacular and splendid war!

“Anyway… Goodbye… Goodbye…” The lightning life was getting smaller and smaller in Li Yao’s vision. It gradually melted into the enormous shadow of the ancient star and was no longer visible. Li Yao had run out of spiritual energy during the life-and-death battle and the communication later.

He didn’t even have the strength to shake his little finger.

Fatigue and sleepiness gradually flooded into his brain like a black, thick tide and consumed his every brain cell.

Before he passed out, he had only had time to turn on the automatic navigation system of the Giant God Soldier with the movement of his eyeballs and the release of his brainwaves so that the arsonists could search for the signs that Li Linghai, Ding Lingdang, and the rest of the team had left and catch up with the main team to the entrance of the tomb.

Li Yao did not know whether or not the broken, paralyzed giant soldier could complete the task. Just like the lightning creatures, he was faced with desperation and hope.

Li Yao slept for a long time. His dreams were filled with lightning, thunder, and endless energy models.

When he gradually woke up, his condition was no better than before. He was still tired, sleepy, and hungry. Every cell in his body was screaming. A lot of high-nutrition drugs had been stored inside the Giant God Soldier. Little Black should’ve injected and treated them in time.

However, the high-nutrition drugs had been electrolyzed and vaporized by the lightning-like life-form. There was barely any nutrition left.

Li Yao checked the navigation log of the mainframe crystal processor and was surprised to find that he had slept for… seven days!

Such a long period of loss of control and coma was rarely seen in his entire training career.

Thankfully, according to the log, the Giant God Soldier had carried out his instructions dutifully while he was in the coma. Besides, the signs left by Li Linghai and Ding Lingdang were clear enough, too, making him closer and closer to the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor.

Considering the damage of the Giant God Soldier and his remaining fuel, it was an out-and-out miracle. “Is it… you?”

Li Yao rubbed his aching head and felt that lightning was still dancing inside.

He did not know whether or not the lightning-like life had crawled into the fuel tank of the Giant God Soldier and changed the structure and constituents of the crystal fuel, squeezing out more energy in a way that… the carbon-based intelligent life could not understand.

He did not know whether or not the flashing nerves deep inside his brain carried the features of a lightning-like life.

However cruel the universe was, life would always find a way out, wouldn’t it?

Li Yao laughed.

Looking at the ancient giants that were drifting behind him for seven days and seven nights and the giant planet that was as brilliant as the sun, Li Yao suddenly felt that the desolate and lonely universe had become livelier and livelier.

Who said that human beings were lonely? Maybe there were countless wonderful and splendid creatures living in different levels of the universe. Human beings simply needed to find them by rubbing their eyes. It was like a lush forest. On different levels, there were towering trees, shrubs, and grass. There were wolves, tigers, leopards, antelopes, and deer. There were also reptiles and insects, and even fungi and bacteria.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

It was possible that the wolves, tigers, and leopards had never realized that countless well-organized ant nests and ants might exist in the soil below their feet. The wars between ants were as solemn and magnificent as the battles between ferocious beasts.

They were merely hunting and killing blindly.

But human beings were not predators.

Nor should it. Never.

Li Yao suddenly felt that the word ‘dark forest’ was contradictory and… groundless!

“The vast majority of plants require sunlight in order to achieve photosynthesis. Therefore, the existence of the forest itself means the existence of light. No matter how dark the forest appears to be, there will certainly be light outside the forest!

“Even if there is an ‘absolutely dark’ environment, the natural creatures born in the ‘absolutely dark’ will never evolve into light-sensitive organs such as eyes in the first place. Then, what’s the significance of darkness for them and what’s the trouble?

“Even if they haven’t evolved into ‘eyes’, they must have ways to perceive the information around them and communicate with their companions or even other species. Whether they are willing or not, they are the best, if not the only, motivation for their evolution! “As long as we can communicate with each other, light is destined to be born in darkness no matter how dark it is. The civilization of mankind is a product of light in the first place. Right now, we only need to spread the light and pass it on!

Li Yao extended one of his fingers.

“Crack! Crack!” An insignificant ball of lightning appeared on his fingertip. It was no more than five millimeters in diameter, but it was changing into all kinds of delicate forms and hundreds of complicated 3D structures. It was even blossoming like a flower and dancing like a butterfly, inadvertently displaying the energy structure and manipulation ability of Li Yao that surpassed all the intelligent carbon-based creatures such as human beings and the Pangu Clan.

It was a gift from the lightning creatures, just as he had given them the ability to smelt metals and manipulate materials.

“If the lightning life-forms, whose history is only two thousand years and whose individual life span is less than ten seconds, have never given up hope in the face of the supernova explosion and the catastrophe of the collapse of galaxies, what reason do we, human beings, have to give up hope when we are faced with a flood?

Li Yao smiled. He pulled the lightning back to his palm and charged forward on the Giant God Soldier.

An hour later, the pearl floating quietly in the sea of stars at the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor appeared in his vision again.

He had also received Ding Lingdang’s message from a crystal processor floating near the entrance of the tomb.

“Li Yao, it will be great if you can see the message!”

Inside the light beam, Ding Lingdang was exhausted. The cockpit behind her was burning with smoke. “The meteoroid storm and the electromagnetic interference were too severe. Our fleet suffered tremendous losses. Many starships lost the ability to sail or fight.

“What’s worse is that the navigation and communication of many starships have been completely wrecked and have drifted to God knows where. Right now, we have only found a dozen starships. The rest of the starships will not arrive until they repair themselves.

“Time is of the essence. We can’t wait any longer. Right now, we have to enter the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor to capture Lu Qingchen. Therefore, we have set up an information node here. If there are any abnormal changes inside the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor, we will send out the information as soon as possible. “If there are no anomalies, it means that we are still in the process of capture. Li Yao, or anyone else who has read the message, please enter the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor to reinforce us immediately!”

The message had been sent five days ago.

Then, there were scattered, messy messages that were squeaking and hissing. It seemed that Ding Lingdang and the rest of them had been seriously jammed in the tomb of the Supreme Emperor and that the messages they sent had been sabotaged.

Five days ago, Ding Lingdang and the rest of them had entered the tomb of the Supreme Emperor and still hadn’t come out. Had they failed to capture Lu Qingchen, or…

Li Yao did not dare to think any further. He accelerated and approached the entrance of the tomb.

The ball of mercury and pearls had entirely different features from the ancient planet.

If the ancient giants symbolized primitiveness, nature, wildness, and brutality, they were the symbols of absolute precision and control. They were man-made products of the super civilizations.

The ‘Arsonist’ was less than a hundred meters away from the entrance of the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor. But at such a close distance, Li Yao was still unable to detect any flaws or bumps on its surface. On the surface that was more than several kilometers in diameter, there was not even a fluctuation of 0.001 micrometers.

–With Li Yao’s knowledge of materials science and metal processing, even processing solid metal into such an accurate sphere was absolutely impossible.

Besides, it was not a solid at all, but an unknown material that looked like liquid metal but was hundreds of times more marvelous. It was an ‘entrance’ that could be passed through freely.

Just like that, it was quiet, indifferent, and aloof from the world. It was blossoming with its own brilliance, displaying its strength and making all the latecomers feel that they were looking up to a high mountain.

It was no longer appropriate to call it the entrance to the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor.

Li Yao was certain that even the Supreme Emperor of the human civilization did not have the ability to build such an entrance.

Moreover, judging from the relics of the Pangu Clan and the warships of the Nuwa Clan, their metal refinement and processing ability were far from that.

Li Linghai was right. The relic was neither the work of the Supreme Emperor nor the Pangu Civilization. It could only be a product of the ‘black wall’. It was the work of the super civilization that blocked billions of stars and sealed the Pangu Universe!

Chapter 3025: Ancient Tomb

Li Yao scanned the surface of the entrance of the ancient tomb carefully, but he found nothing except for his own clear reflection. Just like Li Linghai, he did not know what the so-called ‘entrance’ was. Was it a man-made celestial body, a super teleportation array, or was it because the ‘black wall maker’ captured one wormhole or a hundred wormholes and condensed them together? Li Yao’s attention was completely occupied by the entrance of the tomb. He could not help but manipulate the Giant God Soldier to touch the mercury-like surface.

Ripples immediately spread out on the surface of the entrance of the ancient tomb, as if it were a tunnel leading to the unknown. Before Li Yao was ready, an inexplicable attraction force pulled him and the arsonist in.

It was not a simple absorption. A lot of ‘mercury’ that seemed to be alive surged out and enveloped, melted, and melted the giant soldier god.

The feeling was similar to the space jump, which was a three-dimensional breakdown of the space jumpers.

wa

varm

But compared to the simple and crude technology of space jumps, which had tremendous side effects, the technology employed by the ‘Black Wall Makers’ was much more advanced. The entire process was very warm, gentle, and comfortable. The warm ‘mercury’ not only enveloped the Giant God Soldier, it also enveloped Li Yao’s skin and every cell in his body, giving him maximum protection. Without him knowing, he had passed through the barrier of time and space and arrived at –

Li Yao felt that he was falling rapidly.

am

He seemed to have appeared out of thin air among the blue sky and white clouds, falling towards the lush land.

Pu! Pu pu pu pu! The power spurts of the Giant God Soldier emitted an ugly noise. The intensity of the power unit, the metal fatigue of the overall structure, and the consumption of fuel had all reached the limit. Li Yao could not even activate the anti-gravity unit. He could only spurt out a few feeble flames. Together with his own gravity manipulation technique, he was able to help the Giant God Soldier decelerate. In the end, he fell to the ground with a “Bang Bang” and fell flat, creating a human-shaped crater.

Thankfully, it was not hard metal or concrete below but soft soil, which reduced the impact by more than 90%. “Chi…”

The protective cover of the spiritual residence opened automatically. Li Yao crawled out on all fours.

Little Black, who had followed him since childhood, had also condensed into a form suitable for high-speed movement and exploration. It was a fat ball that looked like a ball made of black sesame seeds and sticky rice. It flapped its short wings and circled around Li Yao’s head.

“Is this the legendary… Tomb of the Supreme Emperor, the Gold Throne, and the Firmament Sector?”

Li Yao scratched his messy hair and tried to open his swollen eyes. He sniffed the air and felt that… something was wrong.

This was a somewhat successful landing. Although the appearance of the Giant God Soldier was terrible, at least its main structure was not damaged. With the right materials and fuel, Li Yao could restore more than 70% of its fighter jet energy. The gravity inside the ancient tomb was somewhere between the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Empyrean Terminus Sector, which was the most suitable for the survival of the two-meter-long, carbon-based intelligent life forms. In the meantime, although the air was filled with an unpleasant smell of sulfur, the composition of the poisonous gases such as sulfur dioxide was still within the tolerance of ordinary people for a short period of time. There were no earthquakes, tsunamis, volcanic eruptions, or tornadoes. The humidity of the air made Li Yao believe that there should be lakes, oceans, or water systems nearby. Generally speaking, this place could be called a paradise for carbon-based intelligent life. It wouldn’t be strange to say that the first carbon-based intelligent life originated from this place.

And that was exactly what happened.

This place was full of vitality. No, the word ‘lively’ was not enough to describe the abundance of creatures here. A great explosion of creatures had just taken place!

As far as one’s eyes could see, there were primitive forests everywhere. The plants dozens of meters tall were tied up by vines and weaved into a complicated three-dimensional ecological network. From the depths of the forest came the howls of various animals and the cries of birds. Even in the transition zone between the forests, which was Li Yao’s landing point, there were large areas of grassland.

The grass here was different from any other plants that Li Yao had seen on any planet. Their moisture content was shockingly high. With a light step, they would spurt out thick and dense sap, filling the air with a fresh and sweet smell. The cracks would even grow at a visible speed and soon recover to their original state, becoming even more robust. Even though the Giant God Soldier had fallen from the sky and burnt a large area of the prairie to death five minutes ago, more grass was wriggling to cover the Giant God Soldier right now. Good heavens! They were even extending their roots from the wet soil, trying to climb onto the body of the Giant God Soldier and drown him!

“How did this happen?”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao could not figure it out at all.

He flew to the sky with Little Black and looked down at the land from a height of approximately 200 meters. As far as his eyes could reach, there was nothing but colorful, lush green, and covered by primitive jungles and boundless grasslands. As far as the eye could see, it was the same even to the end of the horizon.

But—but it was impossible! Li Linghai had told Li Yao and everyone else very clearly that, although the environment inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor was suitable for the survival of human beings, it was definitely not as vigorous, primitive, and natural as this. Instead, it was full of man-made traces.

According to Li Linghai’s speculation, ten thousand years ago, at the peak of the Star Ocean Imperium, the Supreme Emperor had wanted to gather the strength of the entire civilization to explore this ancient tomb. Therefore, the Supreme Emperor had spared no expense and transported a large number of warships, carriers, shield machines, mobile laboratories, military camps, and even metal smelting bases as well as fully-automatic puppet production lines, turning this place into a copper-cast, iron-cast, and armed forward base.

When the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God suffered heavy losses and he was ready to hibernate in the depths of the ancient tomb, he should’ve brought a lot of people with him. It was not like he had only one commander, was it?

Li Linghai should not have lied about the matter.

Even if she wanted to sabotage Li Yao, it was unlikely that she would sabotage her own son, Li Jialing, too.

Besides, Li Yao and Li Jialing had both inherited part of Wu Yingqi’s remaining soul, which had wandered in the tomb of the Supreme Emperor for hundreds of years. Therefore, Li Yao and Li Jialing both had vague memory pieces and flashbacks, which proved that Li Linghai was not lying.

Li Linghai had excavated a lot of treasures from the tomb of the Supreme Emperor, such as ‘Emperor Flame Beads’. In fact, they were all retrieved from the forward operating base. They were all pure gold and could not be faked.

But right now, right in front of Li Yao’s eyes, the forward base of the Supreme Emperor Era, as well as the forward base that Li Linghai established in order to explore the tomb of the Supreme Emperor, were all gone. Or rather, it had been swallowed by the primitive forest.

It was a clear blue sky with white clouds. Yet, Li Yao felt gloomy and miserable.

“Ding Lingdang, Li Linghai, Boss Bai, Long Yangjun!”

Li Yao screamed hysterically in the communication channel.

However, noises were echoing nonstop in the communication channel. The interference was too serious.

The magnetic field here was in an extremely disordered and disordered state. Even the electromagnetic frequency was fluctuating. It did not seem to be a natural magnetic field at all. Not only was it difficult to conduct electromagnetic or electromagnetic communication, it was also hundreds of times more difficult to manipulate spiritual energy and trigger spiritual flames. In other words, any expert who came to this place would be significantly weakened.

“It doesn’t make sense. It’s been less than thirty years since Li Linghai last visited the tomb. How could such drastic changes have taken place in the tomb in such a short time?”

Li Yao pondered for a long time before he turned around and asked Black Wing, “Little Black, if you were really brought out of this place by foster father a hundred years ago, then do you still remember what this place was like? It was also like today… lush, verdant, primitive, and boundless?”

Black Wing desperately flapped its wings as a crooked “8” flew out.

Li Yao and Black Wing had known each other for a long time. He immediately knew that Little Black was expressing ‘no’. “No. In other words, Li Linghai was not lying. There were indeed a lot of man-made traces inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor. There were not so many… animals and plants in the forward operating base left by the Supreme Emperor.”

This time, Little Black drew a giant circle to express its affirmation. “Then what’s going on? Where did Ding Lingdang, Li Linghai, Boss Bai, and Long Yangjun go? Those people are all seasoned warriors. They cannot have been swallowed by the forest without leaving anything behind. Also, what about their starships? Li Linghai said that the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor could accommodate warships several kilometers long. There were no traces of starships outside, either. Therefore, they must’ve driven dozens of starships in. Where are they hiding?”

Ten thousand question marks popped up in Li Yao’s head.

Naturally, his first thought was that Lu Qingchen had set up a trap inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor and killed Ding Lingdang and Li Linghai.

However, Lu Qingchen had left the five supreme battle fortresses outside. Li Yao did not think that he had the capability to annihilate Ding Lingdang, Li Linghai, Li Jialing, Yan Liren, Boss Bai, and Long Yangjun’s splendid lineup without alarming anyone.

Even if a fierce battle had taken place, the traces had already been swallowed by the crazily growing plants.

So

Li Yao sighed and looked at the world in confusion.

Then, cold sweat broke out on his forehead.

Li Yao discovered something. Something terrible, serious, and weird.

The entrance to the tomb was gone.

Chapter 3026: Twisting, Cutting, And Condensing Space

It was not very accurate to say that he did not see it, because Li Yao had been dizzy since he ‘fell from the sky’ and did not see the inside of the entrance clearly.

However, according to Li Linghai’s experience and the memory pieces of Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi, there should be an almost perfect ball of mercury inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor, which was floating in the sky like an eternal moon. The Supreme Emperor had built a forward base around the ‘entrance’, and so had Li Linghai. He had stationed his camp right below the entrance all the time. Therefore, Li Yao should have been able to see its coordinates when he entered the tomb.

Of course, the precise coordinates of the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor were in the depths of the four-dimensional universe. Amid the raging tides, its entrance appeared and disappeared in the three-dimensional universe. Chances were that it had already ‘disappeared’ in the three-dimensional universe.

But the possibility was slim. According to Li Linghai, the harsher the astronomical environment outside was, the larger the crack in the three-dimensional universe would be, and the more stable the entrance to the tomb would be.

This time, they had calculated the coordinates of the entrance and arrived before the tomb was opened. How could it be closed so quickly?

“What is going on exactly? Why is everything different from the exploration experience of Li Linghai and Wu Yingqi? If there is no exit, will I and the entire exploration fleet be locked in this godforsaken place for decades or even hundreds of years until the next ‘exit’ appears in the three-dimensional universe?”

Li Yao was soaked in sweat.

Even though the environment here was very suitable for the survival of human beings, considering the capability of the experts here, it would not be difficult for them to survive for decades. However, the idea that all the elites of human beings in the Pangu Universe would be sealed here for decades was still too absurd.

There must be a way out. Let’s see what this place is exactly!

Li Yao made up his mind and put the broken Giant God Soldier back into his Cosmos Ring before he leapt into the air.

“Chirp chirp, chirp chirp chirp!” Although Little Black had a round head and a cute appearance, it had been in the form of ‘Black Wing Sword’ for decades. It was one of the fastest flying swords in the Star Glory Federation. Naturally, flying in the air was not a problem for it. However, it turned into a streak of blackness and enveloped Li Yao, accelerating him to the maximum.

One kilometer, two kilometers, three kilometers, five kilometers!

Li Yao accelerated. The land below his feet gradually blurred. The green forest, the blue ocean, and the red mountains were as distinct as chessboards.

Li Yao was dying to know whether or not he would break out of the atmosphere or even break through the clouds if he kept accelerating toward the sky, so that he could get a clear view of the tomb.

Or maybe, he would touch an invisible barrier, like most world fragments.

But what happened next was beyond his expectation.

When the earth and the ocean below his feet were covered by clouds that looked like a thick fog, and a brilliant starry sky was about to appear above his head, he suddenly felt that the magnetic field of the universe had flipped over. He felt that his head was heavy, and his feet were light. He was jumping upward, but he was falling at an accelerated speed.

What the hell!

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. The clouds above his head parted, revealing a land that was exactly the same as the one below his feet a moment ago! Yes. He remembered the demarcation line of the seashore, the wilderness, the jungle, and the mountains very clearly. Judging from the shape of the demarcation line, it was exactly the land that he had just set off from!

How? How was it possible? He was supposed to be jumping upward. How did he end up falling down? How did he end up in this place when he had started from this place?

Floating in midair, Li Yao stared at the ground more than a thousand meters below his feet in a trance.

Taking a deep breath and gritting his teeth, he changed his direction and flew toward the sky again, back the way he had come.
