As it turned out, after flying for about half a minute, he experienced another reversal of the magnetic field. The attraction force below his feet suddenly turned into a force above his head. He went from ‘rising into the sky’ to ‘falling from the sky’, and ‘should be below! was still the familiar land.

IIII

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He flew to the horizon at the highest speed, while he observed the land and drew a map of the place.

If the tomb of the Supreme Emperor was really a planet, judging from the gravity and the natural environment, its mass and radius should be similar to those of Empyrean Terminus and Heaven’s Origin. It should be no larger than twice the size of Empyrean Terminus and Heaven’s Origin.

With Li Yao’s current Cultivation, it was not difficult for him to circle around such a planet.

However, weird things happened again.

Li Yao had noticed that the land and the ocean here were both slightly curved, like the surface of a planet. But when he set off from the east coast of the continent, crossed the continent and the west coast, and crossed a vast ocean, he had returned to the starting point without even circling the planet once or even a quarter time!

The magnetic field here was too chaotic for him to distinguish between east, west, south, and north. However, assuming that the journey was in the east-west direction, the situation in the north-south direction was the same. Li Yao had planned to look for the ‘Antarctic’ and the ‘Arctic’, but after a moment of dizziness, he returned to the starting point.

“Alright. If the interference of the magnetic field was not so intense that it jammed my brain, my crystal suit, and Little Black’s navigation system at the same time, it would’ve been the legendary ‘Ghost Hits Wall’.

Li Yao pondered for a moment and said, “Or, let’s put it in a more sophisticated way. The concepts such as ‘space folding’ and ‘space distortion’ were intentionally disrupted by somebody who set up a maze that nobody could get out of. Well, it’s not unusual that the maze was used as a shield for the ‘black wall control hub’.

The problem was, the last time Li Linghai and Wu Yingqi explored this place, it was not like this.

At that time, the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor was no different from ordinary world fragments. It had a fuzzy boundary, and its structure was very stable. It looked harmless and honest.

Could it be that something, something, or even… someone had activated a certain system or defense measure deep inside the tomb?

Whatever it was, it gave Li Yao an ominous feeling

“No wonder Ding Lingdang and the rest didn’t return to the outside to look for me after setting up the forward operating base. It’s because the space here has been distorted, and they can’t find a way out.”

Li Yao continued pondering. “However, is space the only thing that has been twisted?”

While exploring north, south, east, and west, Li Yao took a lot of pictures with high resolution and collected a lot of climate and magnetic field data.

The gift he had received from the ‘lightning life-form’ not long ago gave him a sensitivity thousands of times higher than that of a pigeon. His perception and understanding of the magnetic field could infer a lot of things from the traces of the anomaly.

Li Yao noticed that, in this small, enclosed world, there was not just one climate system, but dozens of entirely different environments.

The place where he landed was indeed peaceful and pleasant.

But some other places were covered in snow and ice.

In other places, including the depths of the ocean, volcanoes were erupting. The plate movement was extremely intense. What was weird was that, no matter how intense the climate and the geological changes were, they did not affect the neighboring areas nearby at all. Even though they were only a hundred meters away from each other, with the volcano eruption on one side, the earth was shaking, and the mountains were shaking, the other side was covered in snow, and the icebergs were absolutely still.

“That’s impossible.”

Li Yao inputted all the climate data he collected into his brain and made a complicated calculation. Then he came to a very absurd conclusion. “The environment of this world is simply like the different stages after the birth of a planet. The most obvious is the content of various constituents in the air. Some areas are like the stages when a planet has just been born, which are filled with fatal venomous gas. Some places are too high in oxygen, like the early years of the primeval era when plants were just born. Some places have relatively normal air, which is the most perfect stage for a habitable planet.

“However, isn’t the flow of air the strongest? The atmosphere here has obvious convection. How can the air on one side belong to a primitive planet, the air on the other side belong to a desolate planet, and the air on the other side belongs to a modern planet? It-it doesn’t make any sense!”

Li Yao could not figure it out at all.

He made bold assumptions and verified them carefully. At first, he imagined that there was an invisible barrier in the air that blocked the air in different areas and created different ‘labs’.

But what about the air, the abrupt changes of the magnetic field, and the discontinuity of the crustal movements? What kind of barrier could make the earth shake a hundred meters away and everything calm down?

Besides, such an assumption was too simple and crude. It seemed to be completely inconsistent with the exquisiteness, perfection, and shock that the ‘black wall maker’ had established at the entrance.

It was the approach of human beings, not a super civilization that could build a ‘black wall’.

“What kind of place is this exactly? I feel like I’m on a different planet every minute!”

Li Yao mumbled to himself.

Then, his body stiffened, and he shivered coldly.

Yes. Different planets. The immaculate world in front of his eyes seemed to be made of hundreds of different planets!

“Is—is it possible?”

Li Yao asked himself softly, “Cut the ‘space’ from hundreds of different planets as if it were a cake and put it back together without leaving any traces of joints?”

“Let’s put it in a simpler way. The so-called Tomb of the Supreme Emperor, ‘Gold Holy See, Heaven’s Horizon’, is not a world fragment but a collection of hundreds of other world fragments?

Chapter 3027: The Starting Point Of Chaos

Li Yao was shocked by his crazy speculation.

Splitting a piece of space from hundreds of worlds, combining them seamlessly, maintaining the stability of the space and the circulation of air, and producing normal animals and plants… It sounded like a story from legends.

However, since the super civilizations had developed to the peak, the techniques that could move mountains and seas were the source of the myths.

Wasn’t the Pangu civilization and the Nuwa civilization worshipped by human beings in the future, too?

The Pangu civilization was already able to utilize the energy of stars and neutron stars efficiently. The ‘Black Wall Manufacturing’ would only be more marvelous and unbelievable than them.

Then, Li Yao examined the animals and plants. The abundant evidence supported his speculation that he was not in a world fragment but had passed through hundreds of worlds and entered ‘parts’ of the habitable planets. Although Li Yao was not a zoologist or a botanist, he could still tell that the plants in many places seemed to have been born in the primeval era. They were the earliest spore plants and ancient ferns. In some other places, the form of the plants and the way they photosynthesized were closer to modern times. Often, dozens of plants that were hundreds of millions of years old were growing less than a hundred meters away from each other. Some of the ferns should’ve gone extinct a long time ago, but they grew together with the ‘later species’ in perfect harmony.

No. It was wrong to call it ‘harmonious and orderly’. When a plant took root and continued expanding its territory, it was inevitable that it would step out of its native space and invade a completely different environment. It was an environment that it could never adapt to. Often, it would wither, wither, and rot at a visible speed. Under the influence of fungi and bacteria, it would turn into a humus and provide endless nutrients for the growth of new plants.

“Therefore, this is not a ‘primordial forest’, but a museum of a certain plant where all the plants that appeared during the billions of years of evolution of a habitable planet were displayed and annihilated.

Li Yao mumbled to himself.

From the rising and collapsing of the plants, he sensed an abundant, even ‘intimidating’, vitality. The flow of time here seemed to have been accelerated by thousands of times. Everything was growing and dying crazily.

Plants and animals were the same.

Li Yao discovered a very ancient fish in a swamp. They were the pioneers of the march of animals from the ocean to the land and had appeared billions of years ago.

In the depths of the forest, he also discovered spiders the size of basins and centipedes that were almost as large as boas. In the distant past, almost every habitable planet had a plant explosion. As a result, the air was filled with too much oxygen. The higher the oxygen content, the larger the size of the living creatures. It was quite common for arthropods and crustaceans to grow to two to three meters.

As for all kinds of ancient animals such as saber-toothed tigers, marsupials, giant boars, and even mammoths that had gone extinct a long time ago, they were everywhere. It was not unusual at all.

The appearance of the ancient creatures was not strange enough. If the biochemical technology of human beings was enough to achieve such a feat, the creatures that appeared in front of Li Yao’s eyes could almost be called legends.

It was a tall, muscular elk, or rather, a creature that looked like an elk. It had dazzling bright gold hair and radioactive sun stripes on its body. On the two sides of its head were helical horns that looked like the horns of a lamb, on which colorful flowers were blossoming!

Li Yao could see very clearly that the flowers were not climbing on its horns like creepers, but growing out of the horns directly, as if it were a combination of… plants and animals!

Li Yao stared at the golden moose for a long time.

The beautiful creature seemed to have never seen a human being before. It stared at Li Yao curiously for a long time, too, until countless butterflies were dancing on its horn. Then it jumped into the forest agilely.

If Li Yao hadn’t gone through the training of “Three Thousand Mortal Dusts, External Incarnation’ and was still in the Divinity Transformation Stage, he would’ve doubted whether or not he had fallen into a dream again and everything around him was imaginary. However, right now, he had reached the Soul Division Stage. Not only had his soul experienced three thousand reincarnations in the world of mortals, it had also been tempered by the lightning-like life form and was sharper than before. He should be able to distinguish the difference between ‘virtual’ and ‘reality’.

Or rather

If there was a certain existence who could create such a vivid illusion that even the division experts were completely fooled, the technology and resources behind it would absolutely not be much less than building such an ancient tomb.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

At Li Yao’s level, the boundary between reality and illusion was gradually blurred. If an illusion could fool him, it would be ‘real’ in a sense.

For the rest of the day, Li Yao explored almost every different space and analyzed the plants and animals there, including the undersea volcanoes thousands of meters deep.

As it turned out, as more and more data was collected, the seemingly random, twisted maze gradually revealed its full appearance.

Li Yao discovered that there was a pattern to the arrangement of most spaces. They seemed to be arranged in the order of one ring after another, as if there was a certain ‘center’ or ‘origin’.

The further away from the center of the circle or the origin, the more ancient the air constituents, crustal features, and animal and plant forms would be. The closer to the center of the circle or the origin, the higher the level of evolution would be, and the more colorful and explosive life would be.

The ‘center’ or ‘origin’ was at the center of the continent below his feet.

Li Yao retrieved all the strengthening drugs and nutrition drugs inside his Cosmos Ring. Whether or not they were affected by the lightning life-force or not, he simply swallowed them all.

Then, he captured a few giant octopuses that were more than twenty meters long and animals that looked like whales. After examining them and making sure that they were harmless, he wolfed them down and started chewing.

He found an open beach with no obstacles and set up a cordon. With the help of deep meditation, he absorbed all the nutrition and nourished his dry cells, nerves, veins, and nerves.

Three hours later, the pores all over his body were emitting hot streams again. After countless simulations and calculations, the vitality magnetic field had been adjusted to be more suitable for the chaotic space. Li Yao suddenly rose up and marched toward the ‘origin’ of the chaotic space. The closer he got to the ‘origin’, the more chaotic the magnetic field became. Flying in the sky was too dangerous. Besides, he wanted to observe the world at a closer distance, too. Therefore, he chose to walk in the dense forest.

The closer they got to the ‘origin’, the more colorful the life forms were. Countless weeds, bushes, vines, and trees were growing rapidly. The fungi were wriggling, expanding, and rotting at a visible speed, as if they were living carpets.

There were also animals, all kinds of weird animals that seemed to have escaped from a nightmare. They were fighting hard in the depths of the forest that was growing and dying crazily. The instincts carved deep inside their genes were burning, bringing a strong scent of blood to the forest.

There was no road in front of him at all. Li Yao had to pave the way with his saber of flames. Occasionally, when he entered an area where the oxygen content was too high, a small spark would burn furiously and beyond his control, burning down the entire forest. But he didn’t care too much about it, because new plants would be born in a few minutes, half an hour tops, and cover the rotten wood that had yet to be burnt up.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Heavy footsteps suddenly echoed in front of him, as if thunder had risen from the underground For the first time, Li Yao had a premonition of danger. Something big seemed to be coming.

Li Yao immediately withdrew all his spiritual energy and aura. He rolled on the ground, covered himself in mud and humus, and crouched below a giant tree. He even allowed the vines on the trunk to hang down and wrap around his body.

CRACK! CRACK! BOOM!

The moment he finished his disguise, the giant tree in front of him collapsed. A figure that looked like a grey iron tower appeared in front of him. It was solemn, magnificent, majestic, barbaric, and a symbol of ferocity and violence. It was a giant reptile that looked like a tyrannosaur. “So… beautiful!”

However, with Li Yao’s combat ability, he was not scared of any giant reptiles. It did not matter even if thirty to fifty real tyrannosaurs attacked him together.

But the ancestral memories hidden deep inside his cells still made him exclaim in admiration in front of such an ancient tyrant.

In Li Yao’s era, almost all the habitable planets in the three thousand Sectors had been occupied by human beings and modified to be suitable for the survival of primates. Ancient reptiles such as dinosaurs could not exist naturally.

It was true that the biochemical experts of the Blood Demon Sector had developed some enormous, brutal biochemical beasts with the genes of dinosaurs. But no matter how enormous and brutal the biochemical beasts were, they could not have been more intimidating than the natural tyrant in front of them.

The body of the tyrannosaur-like beast was full of lumps, as if it were wearing a solid armor. There were bright purple stripes on its body, either natural colors or feathers.

But it seemed to have reached the end of the road and was on the verge of death. After a few painful roars, its heavy feet staggered, and it lost its balance all of a sudden, crashing into a giant rock that was full of vines and moss. After an earsplitting explosion, the rock was dented, and it fell to the ground.

Li Yao blinked and immediately understood.

Due to the enormous size and the relatively primitive structure, dinosaurs had a high demand on the oxygen content in the air. They could only live in a certain oxygen saturation state, and it would not do if their oxygen content was even a few percentage points lower. It was also one of the reasons why many giant beasts that looked like dinosaurs had gone extinct on countless habitable planets.

The ferocious beast that looked like a tyrannosaur must’ve been born in the periphery of the space far away from the ‘origin’ and in an even more ancient geological era and ecological environment. But it had left its home by accident and trespassed into a space where the oxygen content was lower. It must’ve been suffocated to death.

Chapter 3028: Long Yangjun’S Message

It was lying on the ground like a hill that had collapsed in an earthquake. The rise and fall of its stomach had not calmed down yet. Its red eyes were now covered in a layer of grey-brown haze. The scent of life was pouring out like a broken hourglass.

Li Yao slowly stood up from his hiding place and quietly walked to the front. He observed this untimely prehistoric ferocious beast… Perhaps even in death, it would not understand what had happened and why the surrounding world had become so “terrifying”.

Before it was completely dead, a lot of vines and creepers were already crawling on its body like hungry insects. The lumps on the surface of its body were shivering weirdly, too, like plump fruits. They exploded and flew out, producing clusters of bright purple and dark gold butterflies!

Butterflies flew into the sky. Gold and purple smoke rose up from the corpses of the prehistoric beasts.

Li Yao was dumbfounded by the scene and could not calm himself down for a long time. He extended his fingers subconsciously, trying to catch a butterfly that flew out of the dead body of a dinosaur. But he felt that his fingertips were numb, and his every cell was itching as if the gene strands deep inside his cells had been broken, minced, and reassembled. The scraps were embedded into different forms.

Li Yao felt that countless butterflies were hidden inside his fingers, too, which were about to explode and soar into the sky.

Moreover, his entire body seemed to be made of billions of butterflies and could collapse at any moment.

Li Yao was greatly shocked. He hurried to suppress the feeling with his spiritual energy and managed to stop it from spreading out.

“Is this… some kind of virus?”

“No, it’s not a virus. Rather, it’s a kind of special radiation. It’s a radiation that is rarely seen in nature and even in the entire universe. It can trigger the division, collapse, reorganization, and mutation of genes, allowing the vast majority of cells to break through their limits. In a short span of a few days, a few hours, or even a few minutes, they become completely unrecognizable. They can even combine the genes of different species and create all kinds of strange new species.

“No wonder the life in this world is growing and changing so crazily. It’s like the brakes have been lost. They are rushing forward and evolving toward the abyss of destruction!

“What should I call the special radiation

—’Light of Life’? Maybe it was the intense radiation that accelerated the growth of creatures and turned the tomb of the Supreme Emperor into a boundless forest in only several days. But the ‘acceleration’ is too fast.”

While Li Yao was deep in thought, he suddenly noticed a reflection from the corner of his eyes. It did not seem natural but more like the reflection of the plate armor.

Slightly dazed, Li Yao was overjoyed. He stepped over the rapidly decomposing body of the tyrannosaur and looked at what he thought was a giant rock. As he expected, under the cover of moss, vines, and creepers, he discovered a large plate armor of a starship. As it turned out, what was standing on his left side was not ‘Mountain Rock’ at all but the remains of a starship. The starship had crashed not long ago. It had been covered by all kinds of plants and fungi. There was not even a gap the size of the tip of a needle. Also, the interference of the magnetic field here was too intense for even the metal detectors to work. No wonder Li Yao did not notice it just now.

The tyrannosaur staggered close and crashed into the debris of the starship, denting the broken shell and wiping off a large part of the creep and moss before it was able to see the light of day again.

In his crystal suit, Li Yao waved the heated axe and the flaming saber and burnt all the weeds, vines, and creepers that covered the debris of the starship. Finally, it was revealed that the starship had been crushed into a heap.

Judging from the number and emblem on the two sides of the starship, the fast assault starship was named ‘Crystal Light’. It was Long Yangjun’s ride.

Its surface was covered in shocking, enormous tears, and its turret had been blown off. There were also traces of the blast that had collapsed inward. The damage was so severe that it seemed to have been savaged by an ancient star.

Li Yao found the constituents of the meteorite fragments in some of the giant cracks, but he found traces of blood and biological tissue in other cracks. It meant that ‘Crystal at the Speed of Light’ had not only been assaulted by the meteoroid shower while it was escaping from the lightning creatures but also engaged in a battle after it entered the tomb of the Supreme Emperor.

Besides, the latter battle was the main reason why it crashed in this place.

Were they giant reptiles similar to tyrannosaurs?

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao shook his head. What a joke. If the strongest warship of the human civilization could not even deal with dinosaurs, what was the point of exploring the universe? Then, was it Lu Qingchen?

It did not seem to be the case, either. The damage to the shell of the starship did not seem to indicate that it had been attacked by a giant soldier. Instead, it seemed to be something more primitive, brutal, and furious. Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

On Li Yao’s retina, all the aggressive magical equipment inside his crystal suit and Cosmos Ring rolled down in the form of lists, making sure that every piece of magical equipment could be retrieved within 0.01 seconds and activated in the ‘Obliteration’ state.

“Long Yangjun? Long Yangjun?” Li Yao activated the communication system again, but there was still no response. He entered the starship carefully along a giant tear that almost cut ‘Crystal at the Speed of Light’ in half.

The interior of the starship was packed with all kinds of vines, moss, and creepers, as well as a lot of weirdly-shaped, colorful snakes, insects, and rats. One of the rodents looked like a hybrid of mice and sandworms. It had a circle of mouthparts that looked like a rock crusher and could spurt out corrosive acids. No metal could escape its biting. Such animals had gnawed on the debris of the starship and left nothing of value behind.

Thankfully, Li Yao did not find the crystal suits and bodies of the crew members, especially those of the important figures such as Long Yangjun.

It seemed that they had escaped before the starship crashed.

He hoped that Ding Lingdang’s starship was the same.

Although he did not have much hope, Li Yao still groped his way to the cockpit.

As it turned out, the mainframe crystal processor was beyond recognition, too. Nest after nest of super mice was crawling on it. Li Yao searched for a long time before he finally found a few memory chips with burnt surfaces.

Holding his breath, Li Yao tried his best to polish the lines of the storage chips with the super micro-grinding and engraving techniques.

After connecting them to his crystal processor and browsing through the garbage data for a long time, Li Yao took a long breath in relief and finally found a message from Long Yangjun.

Fortunately, it was a message for him.

Unfortunately, the memory chip was too corrupted to recover two thirds of the information. The remaining one third was mottled and intermittent.

“The legends… The legends are true. There is indeed a certain method to freeze time deep inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor!” In the picture, Long Yangjun was in a trance and shouted, “Time and space are like a frozen river. This place has been frozen for too long. Right now, the ‘upstream’ is gradually thawed, and the ‘downstream’ is still blocked. It is like a flood that is unstoppable!

Li Yao did not understand what he meant. Time freeze? Time thaw? What kind of nonsense was that?

The critical explanation that followed was swallowed by the unrecoverable garbage. When the picture was clear again, Long Yangjun’s face was even weirder than before. The camera was shaking violently, too, with the sounds of cannon fire and fighting echoing far away.

“It is alive.”

There was no telling whether Long Yangjun was ‘mad’ or ‘excited’. She repeated mysteriously, “It is alive. It has woken up from the hundreds of thousands of years, no, billions of years of hibernation!”

What was the meaning of this?

Li Yao could not wait to punch the light beam into pieces.

Then, the picture changed and became even more blurred and gloomy. Long Yangjun was more anxious than ever. “Listen, Li Yao. We were all wrong. We were all wrong!

“About my hometown, the Ancient Sacred World, and the Nuwa Battleship and the Pangu Underground Palace that have been bothering me for half of my life… We have always believed that a cutting-edge biochemical research center is hidden inside the Pangu Underground Palace. It is a place where the Pangu Clan studies how to pass on their civilization, isn’t it? Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun, two experts in the Divinity Transformation Stage, were attacked by the monsters inside the underground palace while they were exploring the Pangu Underground Palace. Their bodies of flesh and blood kept expanding, and they almost turned into the new Pangu Clan, didn’t they?

“Hehe. No. That’s not right at all. We have completely mistaken the purpose of this cutting-edge biochemical research institute!

“Do you remember how Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun described how they felt when they were injected with the biochemical drugs by the monsters? They felt that their minds were melting and disintegrating, their emotions stripped away layer by layer. In the end, they were almost turned into killing machines!

“If this is the way that the Pangu Clan preserved their civilization, what can the biochemical drugs do even if they can produce countless killing machines that look like the Pangu Clan in hundreds of thousands of years? The Pangu Clan is not a natural-born homicidal maniac. They are ‘civilized people’ with a high level of rationality and socialism! Their purpose is to pass on their spirit, will, wisdom, and way of thinking. They are definitely not creating a bunch of ghosts that have nothing but skin and no soul. “No. What is being carried out in the underground palace of the Pangu Clan is not an ‘inheritance experiment’ at all, but an ‘experiment of destruction’. It is something that can destroy the primeval civilization. It is too dangerous and can only be tested in the Ancient Saint World, which is enshrouded in dark nebulae!

“Unfortunately, or rather, fortunately, the experiment of the Pangu Clan succeeded in the end. The terrible biochemical drugs or genetic viruses destroyed the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and all the prehistoric species. Only human beings survived and opened a whole new page. “But right now, hehe, right now…”

Chapter 3029: The Living Pangu Clan!

Long Yangjun was going to continue, when an intense fire exploded behind her. The heatwave and blast destroyed the entire picture. But deep inside the fire, a raging black shadow was dancing crazily.

It was the only useful information that Long Yangjun had left for Li Yao.

Li Yao took a deep breath and calmed himself down. He combined all the information he collected in the past into a glittering necklace.

Firstly, the legends were true. There was indeed a certain technique or magical equipment deep inside the tomb that could freeze time.

Maybe it was something that was a hundred times more delicate than the hibernation system of the Pangu Clan that they had seen in the Kunlun Mysterious Realm, a certain… super hibernation system.

It was possible. Very possible.

It was because the hibernation system in the Kunlun Mysterious Realm had only been built by the Pangu Civilization. The ‘hibernation system’ deep inside the tomb of the Supreme Emperor was most likely built by the ‘black wall maker’, a super civilization from the primeval era. It was even possible that the living ‘black wall maker’ had been frozen!

“That’s right. Assuming that this is really the ‘Black Wall Hub’ that controls the brightness of billions of stars, it is quite reasonable to assign a few of our compatriots to control the switches. What better way to monitor the functioning of the Black Wall than prolonged hibernation?”

Li Yao pondered for a moment. There was nothing wrong with the logic.

According to the legends, the Supreme Emperor entered the depths of the ancient tomb when he was heavily wounded and on the verge of death in an attempt to find a time freeze so that he would be in a “non-living, non-death superimposed state” to forcibly save his life. This could also indirectly prove Li Yao’s speculation.

Then, there was the ‘Light of Life’, the weird radiation that could accelerate the cell activity and gene division.

Li Yao estimated that it was meant for ‘freezing time’.

The reason was simple. If a piece of meat was frozen into a hard rock for a long time, how could he thaw it quickly? Naturally, he would have to set it on fire!

The hibernation technology employed by the Pangu civilization and the human civilization, when a person needed to be woken up after sleeping in the hibernation chamber for too long, would inject some stimulating constituents into the hibernation drugs to accelerate the metabolism, circulation, and brain oxygenation so that the hibernator could be woken up slowly.

If something had been ‘frozen’ in the depths of the ancient tomb for billions of years, what could provide better ‘stimulation’ and ‘freezing’ than the weird radiation such as “Light of Life?

However, once the radiation spread out, it was not as friendly to creatures that were not hibernating for a long time. As far as Li Yao could see, all the creatures were growing, splitting, mutating, and perishing crazily on the genetic level, which was the reason for the terrifying forest.

In the end, Long Yangjun mentioned the virus. The Pangu Underground Palace in the north pole of the ancient sacred world was the place where the Pangu Civilization developed the most advanced biochemical weapons.

It did make sense. Li Yao had thought that it was too simple and crude to pass on such a terrible virus. Since human beings were the brand-new, perfect carbon-based carriers that the Pangu Clan had created for the purpose of passing on their civilization, there was no need to transform human beings into tall, clumsy giants with high energy consumption.

It would make sense if he was studying a biochemical weapon that could attack the genes directly.

When Li Yao was fighting against Fuxi on ‘Ultimate Rescue’, the latter had mentioned that the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and all the other intelligent species of the primeval era had all been destroyed by biochemical weapons. The virus that was meant to attack the enemy had undergone unexpected mutations after countless releases and dissemination. It had evolved into an apocalyptic weapon that could annihilate most creatures and the entire primeval era. Only a few human beings were immune to the virus and survived the disaster.

Right now, there were only two questions.

Firstly, if human beings were immune to the virus, how did Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun contract it? Was the virus unstable in the beginning, or had human beings not been immune yet?

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

It was an important question because it would determine whether or not Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Li Jialing, Li Linghai, and all the exploration teammates who entered the ancient tomb would be infected by the same virus.

Secondly, and more importantly, shouldn’t the depths of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor be a shelter for the Pangu Civilization? Why was there a virus in this place that was guarded by the other half of the Fuxi civilization?

Li Yao was confused, but he finally saw the light. He could only hope that he could find more evidence and traces to piece together the truth.

After leaving the cockpit, he searched the debris of the starship from top to bottom and found some super-compressed high-nutrition drugs and components that were not corrupted At the rear of the starship, there was a giant hole near the engine compartment. It seemed that the power unit had been fully functioning and exploded, blowing up the ‘ass’ of the starship.

Li Yao walked out of the hole, trying to search for traces of the crew members.

Then, he saw three dark shadows in the forest outside.

The blood all over Li Yao’s body was about to be frozen, but it boiled in the next second and rushed into his brain like magma. He crouched, unsheathed his saber, and released Little Black, which flew to the top of the three figures with a few monitoring crystal cameras.

The three figures drew close from above, but they were still absolutely still. Upon closer inspection, Li Yao discovered that they were not human beings but three broken crystal suits. There was no flesh and blood inside the crystal suits, but they had been filled up by the wriggling weeds, vines, and fungi. Supported by the vitality of a hundred times, the objects were like the internal organs that had been exposed to the air. They were hissing and trying to grow colorful flowers through the gaps of the crystal suits.

Such a weird appearance gave Li Yao a sour feeling that was neither disgusting nor mushy.

Compared to the real horror, he hated the inexplicable strangeness more.

Right then, heavy footsteps echoed in front of the starship again.

All the animals in the forest were fleeing. Even the plants inside the crystal suits were wriggling faster, trying to drag the heavy crystal suits away from the place.

“Another dinosaur is not coming, is it?”

Li Yao slightly frowned.

But it was quite possible.

It was because, if there weren’t even more ferocious beasts chasing after it, how could the first dinosaur flee from its territory in panic?

Besides, if it had not been chasing and fleeing for a long time, the first dinosaur would not have run out of oxygen so quickly and suffocated itself.

Li Yao entered the deep slumber state again. He crept around the debris of the starship and looked ahead.

He saw someone.

A giant was holding the neck of the dinosaur corpse with his knee and digging out its internal organs.

The giant was about fifteen meters tall. His skin was gray and brown, with wrinkles and stripes all over it. If it were not for the clear facial features on his round head, he would have looked no different from the dinosaurs below his knees.

His figure was slightly hunched, and there was not an inch of clothing on his body.

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

He did not use any tools, but he broke the bones of the dinosaurs one by one with his amazing brute force and dug out their abundant internal organs. He wolfed down the food without doing anything.

“Pangu Clan?”

Li Yao could not believe his eyes. The blood that was boiling just now was frozen again. He had never expected that he would run into a ‘living’ Pangu Clan member under such circumstances.

Although the appearance of the Pangu Clan changed subtly according to the living environment after they occupied the three thousand Sectors…

Although the Pangu Clan seemed to be feasting on raw meat and blood and did not seem to be a highly-civilized intelligent species at all,

But Li Yao was still sure of his identity. The secret was the organ in his lower body.

The Pangu Clan was the same as human beings. They were creatures that reproduced both sexes. There was a difference between men and women.

However, since a long time ago, they had awakened the ability of brainwaves to communicate directly. They were adept at large-scale ‘spiritual communication’ and could get great pleasure from the collective telepathic thoughts. Therefore, they had been training their brain cells and the strength of their mind all day. They were not very interested in the affairs between men and women.

After the expedition army of the Pangu Clan returned from the multiverse in failure and discovered the horror of the ‘black wall makers’ or even the ‘flooding tides’, they had also listened to the advice of Fuxi and sealed their own feelings. Naturally, they could not allow the so-called romantic feelings to disturb the survival of the entire civilization.

Therefore, in the later years of the Pangu civilization, the organs below the Pangu Clan gradually shrank and degenerated. Only the most basic functions were retained, and it was fine as long as they could output the life seeds.

Li Yao had seen a lot of corpses of the Pangu Clan in the Kunlun Mysterious Land and the Pangu Underground Palace. Although most of them would be obliterated the moment they met the air, some precious pictures and files could still be kept. Therefore, he knew the body structure of the Pangu Clan very clearly.

The giant’s body shape, the wrinkles on his skin, and the disproportionately small organ below all proved that he was a member of the Pangu Clan, or from the era when emotions were completely sealed under the suggestion of the Fuxi Clan.

“How did this happen?”

Li Yao felt that time and space were in a mess.

What was worse was that his ‘Incendiary Soldier’ had not been repaired yet. Now that it was riddled with holes and running out of fuel, it could not be activated at all.

How could a crystal suit deal with a living member of the Pangu Clan?

Li Yao’s heart skipped a beat.

Although the Pangu Clan member was naked and looked like a barbarian, his keen senses did not diminish at all. He immediately sensed the changes in Li Yao’s heartbeat and brainwaves. Holding the internal organs of a tyrannosaur, he turned around and stared at Li Yao warily.

Chapter 3030: Self-Destruction

Li Yao immediately felt that an invisible chisel had pierced through his brain and turned his neural network into a interweaving torrent of magma.

He had been discovered and locked onto by the Pangu Clan!

The indifference on the Pangu Clan member’s face immediately collapsed, replaced by wariness, excitement, and brutality, as if he were a human-shaped tyrannosaur. Li Yao thought of something and noticed subtle changes.

The Pangu Clan should not have such obvious and intense expressions on their faces. Were their emotions not sealed?

It was just like the Pangu Clan member who had fought against him in the Kunlun Mysterious Realm. Even at the most intense moment of the battle, the ‘anger’ on his face was only fleeting, as if he was scared that he might be triggered.

But the Pangu Clan member in front of him was completely unrestrained, releasing primitive, primitive, and animal-like emotions to his heart’s content.

Had his seal been broken? Had he regained his emotions? Before Li Yao figured out the question, he already felt that a brutal, stinky wind was blowing at him. Good heavens! The powerful Pangu Clan expert had grabbed the hind leg of the tyrannosaur, picked up the enormous beast that weighed dozens of tons, and smashed it at Li Yao!

Li Yao’s pupils constricted to the size of a needle tip. He turned into a streak of brightness and dashed almost a hundred meters away.

BOOM!

The body of the tyrannosaurus rex was smashed to the place where he had been standing a moment ago, resulting in a blast that seemed to be real. The mud that was splashing was more than ten meters high.

“Hooooo!”

The Pangu Clan roared in fury when their attack failed. They jumped up and down like gorillas and bashed their chests in fury.

“What—what the hell? So barbaric?”

Li Yao’s head was in a mess.

Since his opponent had apparently unlocked the shackles that bound his emotions and will, Li Yao did not want to kill him brutally.

He had no ill intentions toward the Pangu civilization as a whole. They were the continuity of a father-son civilization and even different stages of the same civilization. Was there no chance for them to work side by side in front of the desolate universe and the flood that could destroy everything? Couldn’t they at least sit down and talk peacefully?

“I don’t want to fight you!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth and opened his hands, gesturing to the Pangu Clan. “Can you understand me? At least look at my posture. I mean you no harm. Let’s talk. Talk!”

For a moment, there was confusion on the Pangu Clan’s face.

But his eyes were soon filled with the redness of killing. He picked up a giant rock and threw it at Li Yao.

“Idiot!”

Li Yao cursed in his heart. He did not know whether he was cursing the Pangu Clan that he could not communicate with or himself for his childishness, but he turned into a phantom and dodged the giant rock agilely. Thinking for a moment, he sealed a ‘brainwave’ with the new energy structure that the lightning creature had just taught him and sent it to the Pangu Clan.

“Let’s talk. If we really have to fight, it doesn’t stop us from talking in one minute. At least, we can talk while we fight!

Li Yao sealed such thoughts in his brainwaves.

The Pangu Clan was best at telepathy. If human beings were their creations and their slaves, they would certainly have the ability to command and receive feedback from human beings. The two parties would definitely be able to communicate.

But Li Yao’s brainwaves were completely unresponsive.

No. There was not no reaction. A moment later, an invisible lightning hit Li Yao’s head brutally like a burning giant axe, almost splitting his brain in half.

The Pangu Clan had launched a mental attack on Li Yao!

Li Yao was in so much pain that he could barely contain his fury. He was deeply confused.

Why was there such a deep hatred between the two parties that the Pangu Clan wanted him dead the moment they met?

Or rather…

The Pangu Clan member had been infected by the ‘virus’ that Long Yangjun described. The price to be paid for unsealing his emotions and will was to unleash his instincts of killing and destruction to his heart’s content. He was not targeting Li Yao, but all the creatures in his vision.

There was no time to think. The enemy attacked again like a tyrannosaur that was hundreds of times more agile.

If it were Li Yao in the Nascent Soul Stage in the Kunlun Mysterious Land, he would not have been able to resist such a dual attack without the help of the giant soldier gods.

But right now, Li Yao, who had reached the Soul Splitting Stage, could manipulate his body to dodge the attacks of the Pangu Clan while he established a ‘brainwave shield’ to resist the mental attacks with the energy structure that the lightning life-form had just taught him. He could even spare part of his computational ability to analyze everything in the battlefield calmly.

The combat ability of the Pangu Clan member was far weaker than that of the one in the Kunlun Mysterious Land.

It was not because of his Cultivation or his absolute strength, but because the Pangu Clan member in the Kunlun Mysterious Land was apparently a rational, civilized man who had grasped unbelievable techniques such as ‘Drawing Runes in the Void’ and ‘Dictation of Law’. He could perform powerful killing moves with his bare hands.

The Pangu Clan member in front of him, on the other hand, had been fighting recklessly with his amazing physical strength from the beginning to the end. Even the mental attacks that he performed were not organized at all. His brainwaves were triggered to the maximum and bombarded Li Yao brutally.

Barbarian. He was simply a barbarian wearing the skin of the Pangu Clan!

Besides…

Li Yao noticed that, less than three minutes into the fierce battle, the Pangu Clan member’s chest was already fluctuating violently. His skin turned from grey to deep purple, and steam was popping up from the wrinkles on his skin.

Several obvious red spots were floating on his bare head, as if countless scorching flames were about to burst out of his brain. The ‘magical axes’ that he was slashing at Li Yao with became disordered, too. Many of his attacks were not aimed at the target at all, and his precious mental power was wasted.

Li Yao thought quickly and immediately understood.

Although the conclusion was quite absurd, the reason should be the same as the cause of death of the previous dinosaur. The Pangu Clan member was not adapted to the environment. The gravity here was very comfortable for a human being around two meters tall, and the oxygen content in the air was just right. On the other hand, for a giant carbon-based life form more than ten meters tall, the gravity was too heavy, and the oxygen was too thin. Maybe the air even contained ingredients that were highly toxic to the Pangu Clan.

The Pangu Clan had always been picky about the living environment and was relatively ‘delicate’.

Otherwise, there wouldn’t have been a need to produce the “universal, all-terrain, versatile tools” in place of the three thousand Sectors. When the Pangu Clan member was chasing after the dinosaurs, he had crossed countless different ecological environments and absorbed a lot of ‘lethal impurities’. Like the dinosaurs, he was already exhausted and on the verge of collapse.

When he saw Li Yao, he launched a high-intensity mental attack without bothering about anything else. However, although he had brute force, he did not have the ability to control brainwaves and mental power. He was about to be burnt to death when his braincells were triggered!

“Hey hey hey hey, you—you must be joking!”

Li Yao didn’t know what to say. He jumped from branch to branch agilely, dodging the increasingly furious and chaotic attacks of his opponent, while he continuously released the brainwaves that he had sealed. “Are you really in a conscious state? Do you know what you are doing? This is not your battlefield. Moreover, your spiritual flames are not surging. You are not even wearing a strengthened exoskeleton to protect yourself. You are resisting the super high gravity with your flesh and blood! Stop it! Stop it now!”

“If it goes on like this, you will play yourself to death. Stop stimulating your brainwaves and mental power without restraint! You are completely overdrafting your life and burning your entire brain at the fastest speed. You are committing suicide. Do you understand or not?”

Obviously, the stranger did not understand at all.

The giant turned a deaf ear to Li Yao’s exhortations and continued attacking futilely. He knew that his skin had turned deep purple, and the white mist spurting out of his wrinkles was intermittent. His entire skull had been heated to the point that it was translucent, and he could vaguely see his brain-it was as if his skull had turned into a piece of transparent glass.

Li Yao had no idea what to do, but he understood the reason why the seemingly insignificant human beings were the ultimate winners in the primeval war.

In the competition of survival, the winners and the losers were never distinguished by their size and strength. Even though the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan were both giants who could summon wind and rain, they still had to abide by the law of conservation of energy.

The larger the body size and the higher the mass, the more serious the gravity restriction would be. The metabolism and energy circulation system would be more precise, and the more likely it would be damaged. More energy would be consumed, and more waste would be excreted.

In order to release intense spiritual lightning, his brain had to be burnt. One moment of carelessness, and his entire brain would be burnt.

If he performed techniques that were too complicated in high-mass, high-gravity, low-oxygen, and relatively short of resources on a high-intensity battlefield, it was very likely that he would be crushed just like what was happening right now.

BOOM!

After unleashing an earthshaking mental attack that seemed to be real and brushed past Li Yao, the Pangu Clan member finally collapsed.

From beginning to end, Li Yao had never taken the initiative to attack him. It was purely because he had over-exhausted his brain and physical strength in the extremely harsh environment. He had drained himself, burned himself dry, and played himself to death.

“What the hell is this? This guy doesn’t even have the slightest bit of intelligence. He completely doesn’t know how to control his own enormous strength. He is literally an idiot and a barbarian.” Li Yao observed from afar and pondered calmly. But judging from the organs in his lower body, he is clearly from the later stages of the Pangu civilization. He should know the basic knowledge about how to control the body and the brain!

Chapter 3031: The Structure Of The Pangu Clan

The Pangu Clan member was still struggling.

There was not the slightest hint of enlightenment and wisdom that a ‘civilized man’ should have in him. Instead, he was filled with the simplicity, recklessness, and barbarism of a prehistoric beast. He was not scared of death at all. Instead, he squandered the last bit of his vitality and tried to stand up on all fours, only to fail again and again.

In the end, he completely collapsed. His chest rose and fell rapidly as he roared like a bull.

The voice gradually weakened. He was dead. No more brainwaves were sent out. The arrogance, brutality, and brutality on his face were frozen into confusion and loss.

Li Yao waited patiently for a long time. Then, he asked Little Black to check and make sure that the body functions of the Pangu Clan member had been completely cut off.

Standing in front of the hill-like body, Li Yao pondered for a moment and decided to perform an ‘autopsy’.

There was no need to really cut open his stomach. He could simply simulate the ultrasonic waves with the high-frequency vibration of his hands and check whether or not something had been implanted into the body of the Pangu Clan member, which made him go crazy. Li Yao circled around the Pangu Clan and returned to his head.

The head of the Pangu Clan was as thick as Li Yao’s. Li Yao tried his best to press his hands against his temples. His hands immediately turned into two streaks of grey mist. Weak bioelectricity spurted out of his palms and flowed into his body through the temples of the Pangu Clan, while he sensed the brightness of his brain, which was gradually dying down, and every meridian inside his body.

It was a typical member of the Pangu Clan, no different from the corpses that the Star Glory Federation had found in the Kunlun Mysterious Land and the Pangu Underground Palace.

What was special about them compared to human beings was that ‘third brain’ was embedded between the two halves of their brain, which was similar to the ‘pineal gland’ of human beings. Or rather, they were born with spiritual roots that were visible to the naked eye.

The ‘third brain’ was the material foundation for the Pangu Clan to release powerful brainwaves and realize super-long-range telepathy and a large-scale mental network.

It was also the natural gift that allowed the Pangu Clan to communicate with the intelligent creatures on different carbon bases and become the leader of the Pangu Civilization Alliance.

It was a pity that there were pros and cons to everything. Any advantage of evolution had a high price. The Pangu Clan was on the wrong path of evolution. Or rather, their brain evolution was too extreme.

dve

It was true that the ‘third brain’ could release brainwaves and achieve high-speed telepathic thoughts, but the heat, oxygen, and energy consumption were all astronomical.

Maybe, the Pangu Clan in the beginning accidentally discovered the mysteries of mind communication and mental attacks. The Pangu Clan, which was best at mind communication and mental attacks, took the advantage in the fierce competition for survival and evolved in such a way

Over the long years, in order to carry the ‘third brain’, which was getting larger and larger, and to process the tremendous data that was transmitted and transmitted telepathically, they had to evolve the left half of the brain and the right half of the skull that were getting larger and larger, including a ‘cooling system’ that the human brain did not have, as well as a special energy transmission and storage system that supplied the super brain. In order to accommodate the system, the Pangu Clan grew larger and larger. In the end, they were all more than twenty meters tall on average, almost as tall as the largest reptiles.

In other words, the Pangu Clan did not need such a huge body. It was only because they had a super-large head that they had to match such an enormous and clumsy body.

Then, was it possible to change the way of evolution and only grow an enormous head while keeping the body exactly the same, or even completely regress?

Such bizarre creatures had appeared in many fantasy novels, including Lu Qingchen’s Phantom Plan.

However, the body of a carbon-based intelligent life form was a very delicate and compact system. The so-called body was not just meant to support the movement of the brain. It was also responsible for the absorption and digestion of the nutrition that the brain required. The brain of the Pangu Clan, in particular, had to be constantly supplied with burning oxygen. The brain of the Pangu Clan often required high-intensity operation in order to achieve mental communication and mental attacks. The temperature was often raised to almost a hundred degrees. In order to lower the temperature, the Pangu Clan also had a kind of body fluid that was different from blood and similar to ‘cooling liquid’. The body fluid had to circulate at a high speed throughout the body and be cooled by the hot stream emitted by the wrinkles on the skin. Only with a huge body and a large enough area of wrinkled skin could the cooling liquid be restored to an extremely low temperature after one round of circulation and protect the heated brain again.

The brain, the reliance of emotions, the carrier of thoughts, and the residence of the soul, although less than one twentieth of the weight of a human body, consumed more than one third of the oxygen and nutrition. For a Cultivator such as Li Yao, his brain might consume almost all the oxygen and nutrition. How could he live without a strong body?
