Therefore, the freaks who only had brains but no bodies could never evolve in nature unless they were supported by a full set of machines and biochemical circulation systems.

Just like life in nature could never evolve into seemingly efficient organs such as wheels. Li Yao estimated that the enormous, heavy, and clumsy body was not a big problem on the original planet of the Pangu Clan.

Many biologists, cosmologists, and archeologists believed that the original planet of the Pangu Clan was likely to be a planet with low density and mass, high oxygen content, and low temperature. The lower the mass, the lower the gravity. Together with the high oxygen content, creatures could grow unscrupulously. Even if they weighed dozens of tons, they could still move as fast as swallows.

The low temperature was naturally conducive to the cooling of the brain, which made the Pangu Clan less worried about the brain overheating. Just like that, the Pangu Clan thrived on their native planet, developed a brilliant civilization, and flew out of their mother planet.

After they flew out of their mother planet and explored countless habitable planets, they discovered that their mother planet—the ‘low-mass, low-gravity, high-oxygen, and cold-air’ planets—was a rare exception. Most of the habitable planets were ‘standard’ planets from the perspective of human beings such as Empyrean Terminus and Heaven’s Origin, which meant high-mass, high-gravity, low-oxygen, and relatively high temperature. It was already too late.

They had walked so far on the road of evolution. It was safe to say that their entire civilization was based on telepathic thoughts and mental attacks. How could they chop off their most powerful techniques and restart them, thereby changing their life form completely?

Oh. It was possible. It was also the reason why the Pangu Clan wanted to ‘create human beings’.

Compared with the bodies of the Pangu Clan, the bodies of human beings might have a hundred shortcomings, but the three advantages of ‘low energy consumption, high adaptability, and great potential’ were enough to win the competition of survival with a deadline of a million years.

Li Yao sensed every organ of the Pangu Clan carefully, especially the marvelous ‘cooling system’.

It was a fluid circulation system that was hundreds of times thinner and more complicated than the veins and nerves, including the heat dissipation pores that were hidden inside the wrinkles of the skin. The heat dissipation efficiency of the power unit was comparable to that of a starship. It was indeed an unbelievable direction of evolution.

“As I expected, my braincells have been completely burnt. My entire brain is a mess. Even the gods can’t save me.

“It was not easy for the Pangu Clan to survive, build, and fight on countless planets with such a clumsy and redundant body!

Li Yao sighed. The more he learned about the Pangu Clan, the more he felt that the Pangu Clan was not killed by the Nuwa Clan, and certainly not by human beings, but by the shortcomings of their own evolution, or rather, by the ‘heavenly law’ that restricted the intelligent life of the carbon-based creatures from exceeding the limits and the universe!

While he was pondering, Li Yao suddenly sensed that there were uneven stripes on the inside of the left wrist of the Pangu Clan member.

It was a very delicate rune hidden among the wrinkles, which looked like the overlapping of countless complicated patterns. The standard language of the Pangu Clan looked like a human tattoo.

The ‘3D tattoo’ stunned Li Yao.

Although he did not know the specific content of the tattoos, he had seen many similar tattoos in his research files, including the ones on the wrists of many elite warriors of the Pangu Clan in the Kunlun Mysterious Land and the Pangu Underground Palace.

It seemed to be a symbol of the bravery of the Pangu Clan, which had been passed on after the earthshaking civil war between the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan.

In other words…

The Pangu Clan member was indeed from the prehistoric era and a well-trained elite warrior. He was definitely not a primitive barbarian.

How was it possible!?

The elite warriors of the Pangu Clan were adept at all kinds of mysterious techniques. They could use large-scale armors similar to crystal suits and terrifying magical equipment. Their mental attacks should be more organized and targeted. They should be able to respond to Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts. At the very least, they should have a high level of understanding about the status of their brain and know when to cool it down instead of launching mental attacks recklessly and burning themselves to death!

Looking at the dirty eyes of the Pangu Clan, Li Yao was deeply confused.

He had thought that the Pangu Clan member was a ‘barbarian’ who had been living on his own in the natural environment, just like the human beings who grew up in the jungle.

But the tattoo on his wrist irrefutably indicated that he had once been a member of the tribe and possessed great wisdom.

“Could it be…”

Deep in thought, Li Yao mumbled to himself, “It’s true that the Pangu Clan regained their emotions and their will, but the price was the complete loss of rationality and wisdom. “It’s like… it’s like… emotions and will are a river that is too violent. They were originally sealed and strangled, but when the seal is completely broken, the torrent is unstoppable, and even rationality and wisdom are washed away?”

It was the only reasonable explanation.

But it was definitely not the answer that Li Yao had hoped for.

Of course, he was not the only member of the Pangu Clan who went mad after his resurrection. Long Yangjun, Ding Lingdang, and the rest of them had probably fought against the infuriated Pangu Clan a long time ago. The crash of Long Yangjun’s starship was proof of that.

Then, where were the remaining Pangu Clan, Long Yangjun, Ding Lingdang, and the rest of them?

Chapter 3032: Summoning Of The Ancients

With the crash of ‘Crystal Light’ and the location of the Pangu Clan as the center, Li Yao launched a search with a radius of more than a hundred kilometers.

As it turned out, when he discovered the mysteries hidden under the vines, weeds, and creepers and set them on fire, he found a lot of debris of starships under the lush, living plants.

‘Azure Dragon’, ‘Tiger of the Sky’, ‘Gold Hammer’, ‘Nine-Headed Dragon’… They were all all all-terrestrial warships that could move freely in gravity-free vacuum and high-gravity atmosphere.

The warships, like ‘Lightspeed Crystal’, turned into twisted debris and were swallowed by the crazily growing plants. There were not many valuable discoveries inside.

However, Li Yao did find a lot of scattered bones near the debris of the warships. They were several meters long and dark gold, indicating that they belonged to the Pangu Clan instead of human beings.

Maybe, there had been a lot of human corpses in this place, too, but they had been swallowed by plants and fungi just like the flesh and blood of the Pangu Clan. Only the thickest, most solid golden femurs that could carry dozens of tons of weight were hard to destroy.

So, when Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, and Li Linghai’s leading human exploration fleet entered the tomb of the Supreme Emperor, the first thing they encountered was not Lu Qingchen’s ambush but a bunch of crazy Pangu Clan members?

Li Yao examined the damage of every starship carefully, leaving no traces behind, and inferred the cause of the wounds with his abundant experience in refining.

He discovered that most of the shocking wounds were caused by very low-level personal magical equipment, such as oversized chainswords and vibration sabers.

There were even traces of dark gold blood around the irregular cracks, which seemed to have been torn apart by the Pangu Clan barehanded. It did fit the description of the Pangu Clan’s brute force.

If it were an established battle squad of the Pangu Clan, they should not have been carrying only individual magical equipment such as chainswords and vibration sabers. They should not have been fighting human beings’ warships with their fists without any magical equipment.

Therefore, Li Yao suspected that the Pangu Clan encountered by the exploration fleet of human beings were the same as the previous Pangu Clan. They had lost their memories and rationality and degenerated into ignorant barbarians. Was it the effect of the ‘virus’ that Long Yangjun mentioned, or the ‘light of life’ that could thaw time and accelerate the growth of plants, or was it the result of billions of mutations triggered by the ‘light of life’?

Li Yao had no idea.

Although he was anxious that the answer would be revealed in the next second, he managed to hold back his anxiety and found all the crashed starships in the two to three hundred square kilometers nearby. He also collected enough components, high-nutrition drugs, and crystal fuel from them.

He was quite lucky. The maintenance workshop of a multipurpose support ship was mostly intact, and the maintenance platform was still usable.

Although it was impossible for the Giant God Soldier to be reborn, it was fortunate that the main structure of the ‘Arsonist’ was not damaged. Its shell had merely been torn apart by the meteoroid storm.

After replacing the crystal suit with hundreds of colorful metal patches and filling it with crystal fuel, it ignited a dark and stubborn flame.

Before marching toward the ‘origin’, Li Yao took a night’s rest.

There was no real day or night in the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor. After the blue sky and white clouds lasted for almost fifteen hours, the sky would be enshrouded in dense fog, which would last almost ten hours before it was replaced by a new blue sky that was exactly the same as yesterday.

Li Yao had a vague feeling.

This… primeval relic, whether it was named ‘Emperor Tomb’, ‘Divine Tomb’, or ‘Black Tomb’, was not prepared for the Pangu Clan but for human beings.

The reason was obvious.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The gravity here was the most suitable standard for the survival and combat of human beings, as were the oxygen and the impurities in the air, including temperature, humidity, and even plants, animals, and fungi.

Li Yao tested the toxicity of many kinds of plants, animals, and fungi and found that most of the creatures in this place were not poisonous at all. On the contrary, they were very beneficial for the body’s replenishment and recovery. It was truly a paradise where Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures could be found everywhere.

Even if an ordinary person lived a life of raw meat and blood in this place, ate a lot of Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures, and breathed the freshest air, they would slowly strengthen their flesh and blood, awaken their spiritual root, and become a cultivator.

Also, ‘one day and one night’, which was close to twenty-four hours, was also the ‘standard planet’ most suitable for the survival of human beings, with the most average rotation time.

For the giants that required cold air and low gravity such as the Pangu Clan, their ‘day and night’ were much longer than twenty-four hours because of the smaller density of the planets.

It was definitely not a comfortable place for the Pangu Clan.

“Why?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “If this is really the ‘control hub’ of the makers of the black wall, why is it so suitable for the survival and training of human beings? What is the so-called ‘black wall maker’ exactly?”

Just like the previous questions, the answer to the question could not be revealed until Li Yao reached the real entrance of the ancient tomb.

While he was resting, Li Yao released Little Black and scanned the five hundred square kilometers nearby with the monitoring crystal camera. After eliminating the interference of the trees and the fungi, he compared it with the map that Li Linghai had drawn in his previous explorations.

As he expected, the ‘origin’ where the ‘light of life’ was the strongest and where the plants were densest was the real entrance to the ancient tomb.

When the sky was painted with an intense blue color again, Li Yao led the way with his flaming saber and marched toward the deepest part of the forest.

A lot of plants and fungi were squeaking and shivering in the burning flames. But more branches and vines lunged at the ash behind Li Yao and swallowed him instantly.

The nightmare-like picture did not stop Li Yao from exploring the truth at all. He trudged forward in the depths of the forest. Three hours later, after more wrecked starships were discovered, he finally found the remains of a camp left by Li Linghai during his previous exploration!

It was a piece of a metal anti-radiation tent with ‘Deep Sea’ on one corner.

Such tents were meant to be used to build mobile laboratories. They could block all kinds of tiny radiation and ensure that the experiment results were accurate. Li Linghai told Li Yao that she had tried to carry and unfold a lot of tents in the tomb of the Supreme Emperor.

The two words ‘Deep Sea’ naturally meant the Deep Sea Fleet. They were Li Linghai’s personal weapons.

Having said that, Li Linghai did not want too many people to know such an important secret as the tomb of the Supreme Emperor. Therefore, she only brought a lot of spiritual puppets as her assistants during every exploration.

When Li Yao dug out three multipurpose puppets that had run out of energy and a lot of scraps of engineering magical equipment from the Star Ocean Imperium from the depths of the soil, he was even more certain that this was Li Linghai’s camp, the forward base of the Supreme Emperor, and the real entrance to the Supreme Emperor’s Tomb.

Then, what about the ‘door’?

Li Linghai told Li Yao that there was a literal ‘gate’ at the entrance of the tomb.

It was a black stele nine meters high, four meters wide, and one meter thick.

Nine to four to one. The square ratio of three to two to one was very solemn and perfect.

The gate, or rather, the black stele, was as precise as the first entrance. No matter how precise Li Linghai’s measurement equipment was, he could not calculate the slightest error. He had reached the peak in mechanics, materials science, and refinement.

However, the first entrance was always open to all. Any living creature that touched it would immediately be assimilated, melded, and absorbed. The second door was an extremely solid black stele. No matter how hard Li Linghai tried to stimulate or ram it, he could not open it at all.

Had he not received the information from the Supreme Emperor’s forward operating base and Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi’s firm belief that it was the real entrance, Li Linghai would not have been able to connect a solid stele to the gate to the ancient mysteries.

The black stele further solidified Li Yao’s speculation that the primeval relics were prepared for human beings.

A gate that was nine meters tall and four meters wide was too cramped for the Pangu Clan, or rather, a behemoth more than ten meters tall like the Pangu Clan.

But for human beings, the two contradictory concepts of solemnity and exquisiteness were perfectly combined.

Besides, the unit of length named ‘meter’ was most suitable for human beings. Any intelligent human being would think of the length of their arms first when they set the unit of length. Therefore, the ‘one meter’ of the Pangu Clan was much longer than that of human beings. “In the primeval era billions of years ago, did a certain super civilization leave a gate to the future for human beings billions of years later in units of ‘rice’?

Li Yao thought to himself.

But now, the gate was gone.

Li Linghai told Li Yao that her camp and the forward base of the Supreme Emperor were right in front of the black stele. The distance between the three was no more than a hundred meters. Li Yao wouldn’t overlook a nine-meter-tall black stele no matter how tall the weeds and bushes were.

But…

Li Yao extended his telepathic thoughts and scanned in the direction where the black stele should’ve been. His heart was suddenly beating fast beyond his control. Humming noises were echoing inside his brain, as if they were the most sacred and mysterious chants in the ancient times.

“What the hell is this?”

Looking at the latest form of the black stele right in front of him, Li Yao was dumbfounded and exclaimed.

Chapter 3033: Black Crevice

The place where the black stele should’ve been was empty, but there were a lot of… black gaps floating in the air.

Li Yao did not know how to describe such things. They were like the branches of a tree that had been dug out and burnt into carbon powder before they were consolidated into the weirdest and most twisted form. They were also like the burnt corpses of a lunatic artist who had been inspired by a nightmare.

Hundreds of real black gaps in the three-dimensional world were not affected by gravity at all. They sent out spider-net-like ripples as if they were embedded in the air. The smallest gap was about three to five meters long and sent out multiple waves and tentacles.

The biggest gap had dozens of roots, with a length and width of more than a hundred meters.

“Is this… a wormhole?”

Li Yao found it hard to believe.

When he was performing a space jump, he had seen or scanned wormholes with his magical equipment more than once. The so-called ‘wormholes’ were not really round holes. Most of the wormholes were in the form of extremely twisted cracks, which were very similar to the black cracks in front of him.

However, the wormhole was a gap between the three-dimensional universe and the four-dimensional space. It shouldn’t have a real body. It shouldn’t even be visible to the naked eye and could only be scanned by telepathic thoughts and spiritual waves. How could it be like this? Visible? Touching?

Li Yao even felt that the ‘solid wormholes’ were the marks of some bizarre-shaped alien species that had been burnt by the flames in the depths of hell.

Li Yao swallowed hard. Manipulating the muscles that he had trained countless times, he activated the magical equipment mechanically and performed the scanning and exploration procedures.

Yes, the scene in front of him was not a 3D illusion. It was indeed some kind of material that was neither gold nor stone but extremely hard. It was embedded in the air, and from the depths of the material, intense radiation was being released continuously, which could accelerate the ultimate division, mutation, and failure of cells.

The ‘light of life’ was released from the black gaps. The community of black crevices was almost five thousand meters in diameter. Li Yao circled around the community of black crevices and found more traces of human exploration squads, including magical equipment, crystal suits, used strengthening drugs and cans of high-nutrition drugs, bloodstained medical gel, broken shells, and so on.

Besides, judging from the distribution of the traces, they were gradually shrinking and eventually disappearing below the black gaps.

“What do you mean?”

Looking at the twisting black gaps floating in midair, Li Yao mumbled to himself, “Did Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, and Li Linghai arrive at the black stele after they took care of the rampant wild Pangu Clan, only to find that the black stele had ‘split’ into thousands of cracks and that they had crawled into the gaps?”

Li Yao was not sure whether or not the black gaps in the air were the broken pieces of the black stele, or whether or not they were the real entrance to the tomb and the correct way to open it.

After scanning again and again and making sure that there were no other fatal radiations other than the ‘Light of Life’, Li Yao held his breath and bound the smallest black gap with his telepathic thoughts, trying to move it with the technique of controlling objects remotely.

After he reached the Divinity Transformation Stage, Li Yao’s life magnetic field had become stronger than ever. He could even manipulate the magnetic field of a planet and absorb the gravity for his own use.

When his telekinesis ability was activated to the maximum, he could move objects weighing more than five hundred tons. Even the Giant God Soldiers and starships could be lifted and pushed easily.

But no matter how he pushed and pulled, the black gap remained firmly fixed in the air, not moving in the slightest.

Li Yao switched to three clusters of black gaps and exerted all his strength, but he failed to move them at all.

“This—this is impossible!”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao was breathing hard and sweating hard. He observed the black gaps with wide-opened eyes, not knowing how they were fixed in the air. They were like the brand of the three-dimensional universe.

Shua!

Li Yao unsheathed his chainsword and held it high with both hands. The aura of the sword soared into the sky and turned into a crimson dragon more than ten meters long.

Li Yao’s face was extremely solemn. It had been a long time since he had poured all his mental power, will, and strength into one sword aura and slashed it so seriously.

Hiu!

When the sonic boom echoed, the crimson dragon had already turned into a streak of redness and slashed at a black gap.

It was a perfect attack. Even if ‘Sword Addict’ Yan Liren came close, his control over speed, strength, and rhythm would not be any better than Li Yao’s. Whatever was blocking the attack -crystal suit, giant soldier, starship, or even a star fortress-Li Yao was confident that he could cut them in half.

However…

“Clang!”

The crisp sound of something breaking did not come from the black gap but from Li Yao’s chainsword. The moment the aura of the sword passed through the black gap, a ‘Blade of Space’ seemed to have dashed out of the gap and shattered the chainsword easily.

Since Li Yao had poured too much spiritual energy into the attack, the broken chainsword did not stop reacting. It continued splitting into hundreds of pieces and exploded. Li Yao held his sword and breathed heavily. He was dazed for a long time before he raised the broken sword to his eyes.

The chainswords that were qualified to be used by him were, of course, the ultimate weapons that boasted the most cutting-edge technology of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance and were made of the most solid super alloys that would not break even if dozens of crystal suits were cut apart. But such a marvelous weapon was shattered by the black gap the moment it was unsheathed.

And on the black gap…

There was not even a tiny gap.

Li Yao’s eyelids twitched violently. His pupils constricted to the size of two glittering needles.

He retreated hundreds of meters without saying a word. Taking a deep breath, he opened the cover of all the remote magical equipment on his crystal suit one after another. Hundreds of beams of light interweaved and bombarded a cluster of black gaps like a storm.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The cluster of black crevices was immediately wreathed in flames, blast, and dust. A giant fireball hundreds of meters in diameter gradually rose up, causing the vines and creepers nearby to shriek. In Li Yao’s most terrifying attack, the extreme temperature in the deepest part of the fireball was close to the burning of a star!

However, when the fireball was extinguished and the smoke dispersed, all the black gaps were still intact. There were no traces of them being burnt at all.

The products of the ancient super civilizations talked about their strength and the ignorance of their latecomers quietly.

“Damn. Impenetrable!”

Li Yao spat. After the fury and frustration, he calmed down quickly and sat cross-legged, pondering

He found that he had made a mistake.

The black gaps could not be cracked by brute force.

In terms of violence, Ding Lingdang might be on par with him, but Li Linghai and the other experts in the exploration team were definitely no match for him.

However, since all of them had crawled in successfully, it meant that the black gap was in a certain… ‘open’ state.

“Right, I’ve been making the mistake of thinking that we have the key to the real entrance of the ancient tomb. I have half of it, and Li Linghai has half of it. “But the two ‘half-keys’ are supposed to be combined. If they were the key to the real entrance, Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi would’ve been able to open them eight hundred years ago. There’s no reason that Wu Yingqi’s remaining soul could not find what Li Linghai and his foster father found.

“At that time, Wu Yingqi’s explanation was that his remaining soul was too weak, and he was afraid that the depths of the ancient tomb were too dangerous, so he did not dare to take the risk easily. However, no matter how you looked at it, Wu Yingqi did not seem to be such a rational and patient person. “But is it possible that the key is not meant to open the ‘black stele’ but for other purposes?

“The real entrance to the ancient tomb, be it in the form of a ‘black stele’ or a ‘black gap’, is not entered by a key but by something else.

“Wait. What did I just say-drill?”

Li Yao stared at the black gap.

The black gap gazed at him, too.

Li Yao’s soul was deeply attracted by the bizarre-shaped, raging, dancing black gaps. The drumming that originated from his heart and the whispers that originated from his eardrums seemed to be calling from the depths of his brain again.

He suddenly had a very strange feeling that this was not an exploration but a return home.

The depths of the black crevice were his hometown, the starting point for all human beings in the Pangu Universe.

Li Yao’s eyes locked onto a cluster of black crevices more than ten meters in diameter and two to three meters wide. There was no reason other than his intuition.

“This is mine.”

Li Yao walked to the bottom of the cluster of black gaps and pressed them with his hands, applying pressure evenly. He was not pushing the gaps but trying to squeeze himself through them.

At first, the black gap did not move at all.

The material could not be separated by Li Yao’s bare hands even though it was made of the hardest alloy and the most furious spiritual energy.

However,

When Li Yao’s impetus reached its limit and every cell in his body was activated, the extremely hard material turned soft and thick like super-dense glue. First, his ten fingers, then his two hands, then his two arms-Li Yao was squeezed into the black gap!

arms

Watching his arms disappear as if they had been swallowed by the black gaps was a very weird feeling.

It was like the ‘wall-piercing technique’ in the ancient legends, where one solid object pierced through the other.

Then again, even the hardest super alloy material, when observed from the micro level, was empty except for the tiny nuclei and electrons.

As long as the internal and external balance could be changed, why couldn’t the solid pass through the solid?

Chapter 3034: The Fall Of The City Of Silver

“The so-called ‘entities’ simply don’t exist. Even if 99.99% of the space inside an inseparable atom is filled with empty force fields and energy, and investigating the essence of the 0.001% nucleus and the electrons is just a collection of countless ‘energy strings’.

“Matter is energy. The stars and the brilliant universe have nothing except the endless strings of energy-waves.

“The black gaps are ripples, the crystal suits are ripples, and my body and soul are ripples. Then, as long as the ripples are adjusted to a special frequency…” Li Yao gritted his teeth. Countless marvelous curves burst out in his brain. His every cell and even every gene strand rippled and dispersed on the curves, gradually melting into the black gaps.

It was an indescribable feeling.

Every atom that constituted his body was squeezed into the dense material that constituted the black gaps.

As his head sank in, the world outside suddenly disappeared. In front of his eyes was a world of absolute darkness.

His sight and hearing had been swallowed by the darkness. Only his sense of touch and perception had become particularly sharp. Li Yao felt that he was a four-legged snake that had crawled into a crevice. The deeper he went, the narrower and more twisted the crevice became. He was forced to change the shape of his limbs, to the point that he almost broke his bones into pieces. Only then could he adapt to the wrinkles deep inside the crevice.

For a moment, Li Yao was hesitating, not sure whether it was the gate to the primeval relics, a fatal trap, or even the throat of an enormous beast.

He wanted to retreat, but it was too late.

As his entire body sank in, he felt a gentle but firm impetus behind him. The ‘rock’ around him was full of invisible thorns, too, making him unable to turn back.

Besides, Ding Lingdang, Li Jialing, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Li Linghai, and most of the other experts had been involved, too. What was the point of him retreating alone?

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao gritted his teeth and squeezed deeper into the darkness.

“Crack! Crack! Crack!” All kinds of noises and alerts of faults came from the crystal processor, as if millions of tons of pressure were crushing him from all directions. The lighting system had been wrecked a long time ago. The heat-resistant and oxygen-circulating systems were struggling, too. The twisted readings on the mottled light beams were making Li Yao hallucinate. He was not sure whether he was walking in the center of the planet or on the surface of the neutron star.

“Ah!”

The heavy pressure was haunting him and savaging his limbs. First, his limbs, then his torso, and finally his head. Li Yao felt that he had been pressed into a deformed monster whose limbs were dozens of meters long and whose head was oval in shape. He couldn’t help but scream.

But this was far from the end. The pressure permeated into his flesh and blood like black venom and directly compressed and reshaped his cells. Every cell was like a plump grape that had been stepped on and burst forth with “BoBo Bo Bo Bo Bo” sounds. At first, they turned into a pile of mud, and then they grew without restraint like cancer cells.

“What—what have I become?”

Although Li Yao had experienced hundreds of battles and countless unpredictable dangers, he still felt cold to the bones.

If he was given a mirror right now, he would definitely not dare to look at himself right now.

The omnipresent, irresistible ‘compression’ lasted about ten minutes, or ten hours, or even ten days and ten nights. Finally, Li Yao couldn’t bear it any longer. Colorful fountains appeared in front of his eyes, and a curtain of rainbows made of billions of rays of light unfolded slowly. All kinds of hallucinations and auditory hallucinations flooded his mind. His life, the laughter of his family and friends, and the appearance of his home. Naturally, the mysterious Earth was no exception.

This was an incomparably clear near-death experience.

Then, like a roller-coaster that was slowly reaching its peak, everything suddenly plummeted and accelerated to the maximum.

Li Yao felt that he had reached the speed of light.

All the illusions and glamorous rainbows were dragged into infinitely extending rays of light that interweaved and formed an endless maze without entrances and exits.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

On every ‘wall’ of the labyrinth, dazzling white brightness was shining at first, before black ripples spread out and formed vivid 3D pictures that turned from black and white to glamorous.

All the pictures reflected a splendid city that seemed to be made of silver and mirrors.

The shape and the style of the city were similar to that of the city of the Pangu Clan that Li Yao had discovered in the mysterious realm of Kunlun. The skyscrapers were all square-shaped without any redundant lines or unnecessary decorations.

However, the city of the Pangu Clan in the Kunlun Mysterious Land was a lifeless black. Other than the solemnness, it gave a very depressing feeling. Although the ‘City of Silver’ adopted the same architectural and planning style, the buildings were generally taller, slender, and slender. Together with the glittering silver brilliance, it gave a feeling of lightness, sharpness, and future.

Li Yao had never seen such a beautiful city.

He had never sensed such intense ‘research, exploration, and decryption’ in any city, either. It was truly a city that belonged to the sea of stars and the future!

It was a pity that it was just what the City of Silver used to look like in several 3D pictures.

In most 3D pictures, the City of Silver had become a battlefield where explosions, collapses, and screams were everywhere.

In some of the other 3D pictures, the City of Silver had even been reduced to a lifeless graveyard. The glittering future had been corrupted by dried blood and brains.

“This is…”

Li Yao didn’t understand why so many shocking and shocking scenes had suddenly appeared in front of his eyes. It was as if some kind of force had directly interfered with his optic nerves and even his braincells and transmitted valuable information to him, allowing him to observe the fall and destruction of Silver City from various different perspectives.

However, both the guards and the attackers rendered Li Yao speechless for a long time.

He had thought that it would be a great war between the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan.

But the guards behind the city wall were the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Kuafu Clan, and the Houyi Clan… The thirteen species of the Pangu Civilization Alliance had all arrived.

They were wearing the most advanced prehistoric armor-a powerful magical equipment flying platform that was comparable to the Giant God Soldier. Even Li Yao could not see through the various magical equipment in their hands. Behind them stood floating fortresses more than a hundred meters in diameter. The electric arcs rippling on the surface were like dragons that were alive.

But none of that scared their enemies, because the attackers who were about to destroy the entire Silver City were not intelligent creatures at all, but ferocious beasts more than twenty meters tall.

It was like the product of combining hundreds of the ugliest and ugliest reptiles and arthropods and then magnifying them hundreds of times with radiation mutations.

Their ugly skin was full of wrinkles. They were naturally able to release powerful electric arcs, interference fields, and spiritual shields, which granted the hill-like beasts the ability to be invulnerable.

In the meantime, they could destroy a city wall with high-voltage electricity or a skyscraper hundreds of meters high with a simple collision.

It was perhaps the most ferocious ‘beast tide’ in the history of the Pangu Universe. As long as there were three to five such beasts in the Blood Demon Sector, it would be enough to break into the capital city of the Star Glory Federation.

It was an out-and-out epic battle. Although the ferocious beasts were enormous, brutal, and hideous, the defenders were still guarding the last defense line with unbelievable magical equipment and dazzling attacks. Their sabers could trigger a temperature of almost ten thousand degrees, their shoulder-mounted crystal cannons could fire a barrage that was even more powerful than the super arsenals of human beings, and there was even a special Thunder-Palms that could swallow all the spiritual energy reactions within several square kilometers instantly and collapse the interference force fields and the anti-gravity fields that supported the body of all the beasts. As a result, the hundred-meter-tall beasts collapsed because of their own weight, and the pressure of the atmosphere pushed their internal organs out along their throats. –According to this fighting style, no matter how large the ferocious beasts were, no matter how berserk they were, they would not be able to break through the defensive line. Eventually, civilization would prevail over barbarism.

However, soon after every beast collapsed, intense reactions seemed to be taking place in their internal organs, brains, and blood, making their stomachs and shells bulge up and explode into clusters of bright purple, crimson, and gray mist.

The defenders were extremely wary of the strange mist and did not dare to get close at all.

However, the two parties were now engaged in a head-on clash. No matter how powerful the magical equipment of the defending side was, their bodies were inevitably stained with the brains and blood of ferocious beasts, and their heads were inevitably enshrouded in mist.

Therefore, not long after, a shocking scene unfolded before Li Yao’s eyes.

A soldier of the Pangu Clan, who was on the defense team and wearing a helmet and plate armor, had his skin covered by the armor perfectly. Besides, it was obvious that there was compressed oxygen behind the armor. He had been dwelling in the mist for too long, and his armor had been crushed by the ferocious beasts, leaving tiny cracks that were invisible to the naked eye. The mist crawled in through the gaps like invisible bugs. He was about to slash a ferocious beast with his saber when he suddenly twitched violently and started dancing

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! The indestructible armor was torn apart from the inside, revealing the gray skin that was full of lumps and wrinkles. At this moment, countless bright red sprouts grew out of the wrinkles and expanded into thick tentacles at a visible speed, which tied up the warrior of the Pangu Clan!

Chapter 3035: The Person In The Crevice

“This—this is impossible!”

Li Yao opened his eyes and watched the tentacles surging out of the body of the soldier of the Pangu Clan entangling his limbs and torso. Then, after a series of disgusting wriggling, they expanded into new muscles, nerves, and veins, making his body expand by almost ten times until he was almost fifty meters tall!

As it turned out, all the ferocious beasts attacking the City of Silver were mutations of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, or the other carbon-based intelligent species of the Pangu Civilization.

However, such a mutation was too appalling. From a height of more than ten meters to more than a hundred meters, its mass had increased by thousands of tons or even ten thousand tons. Where did such an enormous mass come from in such a short time?

It was true that energy could be transformed into mass, but there was a limit and a source. Were there tiny wormholes inside the bodies of the ferocious beasts that allowed them to retrieve energy from the unknown space?

No matter how Li Yao questioned them, the facts were right in front of his eyes. The ferocious beasts must’ve grasped some incredible ‘mass-energy conversion technique’ in order to complete the fall from civilization to barbarism within several seconds!

“Hooooo!”

The mutated beasts roared earsplittingly and waved their tails, which were full of thorns and spurs, at their comrades a moment ago. Several warriors of the Pangu Clan were blown away and smashed into the skyscrapers.

More ferocious beasts crowded in through the breach. The silver guards were immediately routed.

Dragging such a heavy and clumsy body would consume a lot of energy, which would be a great burden for the body. Therefore, many ferocious beasts often collapsed when they were fighting, just like the dinosaurs and wild Pangu Clan that Li Yao had seen outside.

But more guards were corrupted by the mist and turned into new beasts.

It was destined to be a hopeless defense. The only thing that the guards could do was stall for time so that the City of Silver could resist the beast tide for a while longer, only to be savaged even more miserably.

The skyscrapers that were thousands of meters tall collapsed one after another. Dust and smoke mixed together, making them look like the surging tides in a tsunami. The spiritual energy transmission tubes below the street all broke apart. The super-compressed spiritual energy that was spurting out was released rapidly, turning into shocking eccentric flames. The ferocious beasts looked even more hideous and enormous against the mist and the eccentric flames. Their roars and every stomp seemed to be hitting Li Yao’s brain cells brutally, making him feel that he was in a nightmare that he would never wake up from. He did not know how to get rid of them.

“The skyscrapers in the City of Silver are generally more than a thousand meters tall. Judging from the size of the doors and windows, they are not designed for human beings, but for giants more than ten meters tall. Therefore, this is not a city of human beings or the ‘black wall makers’, but one of the Pangu Civilization.

“Why did the Pangu Civilization build such a splendid city in the depths of the ancient tomb? I can’t see the edge of the city at all. It is simply a city of the future. How many people can it accommodate, or can it accommodate all the elites of the Pangu Civilization Alliance?

“What happened to the ferocious beasts that attacked the City of Silver? Fuxi and Long Yangjun both said that a terrifying genetic virus went out of control and mutated, which destroyed the Pangu civilization and the entire primeval era. Is this the effect of the virus?”

“But that can’t be right. If the City of Silver is the laboratory that the Pangu Civilization established in the depths of the ancient tomb, and a certain virus is being tested in this place has gone out of control, chaos should be breaking out from the inside. But the ferocious beasts are clearly pushing inward from the outside. They are enemies from the outside. Who exactly is attacking the cutting-edge laboratory of the Pangu Civilization?”

Li Yao was greatly confused.

Suddenly, his heart twisted violently, and he could not help but shout.

“Ding Lingdang!”

That’s right, it was not his eyes playing tricks on him, nor was it an illusion, nor was it a mental disorder. Li Yao found his wife, Ding Lingdang, in a vivid, immersive picture!

Ding Lingdang was trapped in the City of Silver, which was full of fire and beasts!

She was almost twenty meters tall on the big Fire Dragon Sparrow. But in front of the enormous beasts that were almost a hundred meters tall, she was as small and feeble as a doll. She could only crawl and crawl among the broken walls under the cover of smoke and dust to avoid the death rays of the Pangu Clan and the trampling of the ferocious beasts.

With Ding Lingdangs appearance, more experts of human beings appeared in other pictures, too. Li Yao found Li Jialing, Li Linghai, Long Yangjun, and Boss Bai. They were all struggling in the surging flames of war caused by the collision of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and the ferocious beasts, like animals that were fleeing in fear when two dinosaurs collided.

Li Yao had thought that the siege of the City of Silver had taken place at the end of the primeval era hundreds of thousands of years ago. The war had been settled a long time ago. The ruins of the City of Silver might have decayed a long time ago, too, except that they had been recorded by special magical equipment and played for the latecomers. In many relics of the primeval era, such holograms were quite common.

However, after witnessing a human expert and his crystal suit being stomped into minced meat by a hundred-meter-tall beast that looked like a hybrid of a giant turtle and a gorilla, Li Yao had to change his opinion.

Everything in front of him was real.

The siege of the City of Silver was still on. Right now, right here, and most of the experts of human beings had been involved in it for no reason. They were on the brink of death!

“Ding Lingdang! Ding Lingdang! Ding Lingdang!”

Li Yao’s eyes were bloodshot, and he was roaring crazily, as if he were about to throw the communication magical equipment at the picture.

In the picture, Ding Lingdang was not aware of his arrival at all. She was still moving among the debris and the death rays, trying to find a chance of survival.

“I’m here. I’m here to save you!”

Li Yao activated the power unit of his crystal suit without caring about anything else and tried to crawl into the picture where Ding Lingdang was. But although he accelerated to the maximum speed, the distance between him and the picture was not shortened at all. No matter how hard he tried to touch Ding Lingdang, he could not touch her shadow at all.

It was as if Ding Lingdang and everyone else had been ‘sealed’ in a 3D painting. Even if Li Yao could see and touch them, he would never be able to crawl in.

Was this what the so-called ‘different people and ghosts’ meant?

Where were Ding Lingdang and the rest of them? What were they exactly?

Li Yao’s anxiety and fury almost made his crystal suit explode.

However, the pictures around him were spinning fast, making him lose Ding Lingdang’s coordinates. He was like a small boat that had fallen into a giant space swirl and could only be carried into a random space wrinkle.

No. Maybe it was not random.

“Jiji, Jiji!”

Little Black cried out.

Considering that Little Black came from the depths of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor and that it might have a natural sense of the environment, Li Yao did not put Little Black back into the Cosmos Ring when he entered the black gap. Instead, he asked it to change its shape and hide inside the Cosmos Ring.

Little Black had been silent the whole time. It was resisting the tremendous pressure like Li Yao did.

At this moment, Little Black suddenly exclaimed in excitement. It even leaked out of the gaps of the crystal suit and covered the crystal suit quickly, forming four helical wings on the back of the crystal suit.

When the last gap of the crystal suit was filled by Little Black, Li Yao suddenly felt that he could move freely in the wrinkles of space!

“Little Black…”

Before Li Yao was surprised by Little Black’s new technique, he was deeply attracted by the giant soldier in the picture.

It was—

“Rescuers!”

Li Yao gnashed his teeth, his eyes bloodshot.

Yes. He would recognize it even if it was burnt to ashes. The golden giant soldier in the picture in front of him was exactly the ‘savior’ without a cockpit, the giant soldier specially designed for the super artificial intelligence that Lu Qingchen had stolen from him!

When enemies met, their eyes were particularly red.

All the cause and effect had been caused by Lu Qingchen. If he had not been insane enough to risk universal condemnation, Li Yao and the rest of them would not have been so hasty to carry out the exploration of the tomb of the primeval deities, which even led to Ding Lingdang’s failure.

If Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Li Linghai, and the rest of the human experts perished in this place, and the terrifying ferocious beasts broke through the tomb of the Supreme Emperor and spread to the entire Pangu Universe, Lu Qingchen would be the culprit who destroyed the civilization of mankind!

Li Yao felt that it was his own fault that he failed to capture Lu Qingchen in the battle of ‘Ultimate Rescue’.

But this time, he would not make the same mistake again!

Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts flashed across countless pictures, but he could not find Ding Lingdang at all.

Then, let’S finish the mission first and capture Lu Qingchen for questioning, in case the lawless guy caused more trouble.
