Some of the acids even pierced through the spiritual shield and the interference force field and hit the spurts of the ‘arsonist’, corroding three to five power units. The ‘arsonist’ staggered and almost fell over.

Judging from the shaking of the earth and the rippling of the air, a large batch of ferocious beasts were still rushing toward the place from various directions. They must’ve been attracted by the noises caused by the ‘Rampaging Armored Dragons’ and the ‘Metal Knights of Starry Skies’, because all the ferocious beasts wanted a share of the loot.

This caused Li Yao’s scalp to go numb. He did not dare to stop for even a moment, for fear that he would not be able to extricate himself from the beast tide. He could only run, run, and run in a panic!

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

BOOM!

The arsonist passed through a silver skyscraper, leaving a clear human-shaped hole.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

In for a penny, in for a pound. After passing through the building, Li Yao concentrated all his firepower and cut the building in half from the bottom. Thousands of tons of rebar, concrete, and metal collapsed and buried a lot of ferocious beasts below, turning them into minced meat.

However, there were still countless ferocious beasts with amazing jumping ability. They jumped twenty to thirty meters high and hundreds of meters away across the debris toward Li Yao.

“Stop, stop stop stop stop!” Lu Qingchen suddenly shouted desperately, “Brake! Don’t move! Stop moving forward!”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment.

In front of him was a smooth and unobstructed road. Neither the visual nor the life detection system had detected the existence of any ferocious beasts. Moreover, the ground in front of him had collapsed. It seemed to be a wide and deep drainage system where Li Yao wanted to get rid of all the ferocious beasts.

When he observed carefully, he found that there was nothing wrong except for the flickering threads floating in the air, which twisted the picture.

But Li Yao was very clear that Lu Qingchen would not lie to him in such a situation.

Although the guy was shameless and ambitious, his soul had been sealed in a lump of iron that was lacking both computational ability and fuel. Without Li Yao, he wouldn’t be able to do anything, not to mention that he would be caught in a tide of ferocious beasts. The only choice left for them was to perish together.

Li Yao stopped on reflex.

Before he had the chance to adjust his posture, he was pushed to the ground by an enormous, spider-like beast.

Chi! Chi! Chi! Chi!

A large amount of mucus spurted out of the abdomen of the ferocious beast, which was emitting green fluorescence, seriously hindering the functioning of the joints of the ‘Arsonist’. The damn mucus was also extremely corrosive, causing the every movement of the ‘Arsonist’ to emit creaking sounds. The metal fatigue level reached its limit all of a sudden.

Li Yao was both shocked and angry, not knowing why Lu Qingchen suddenly asked him to stop.

But he soon found out.

Other than the spider beast that had pushed him to the ground and stopped him, there were a lot of other beasts that were so fast that they jumped hundreds of meters to the seemingly empty area in front of Li Yao.

A weird thing happened!

There was nothing in sight, but the air seemed to be filled with invisible blades and iron hammers. A few ferocious beasts vanished into thin air after jumping in and reappeared more than ten meters away half a second later, only to be cut in half from head to tail.

It was more precise than a scalpel. He slashed so fast that there was no blood at all when the two halves of his body fell to the ground.

There were also some ferocious beasts that directly disintegrated in the air—in the literal sense, they disintegrated in the air. A large ferocious beast was evenly divided into fist-sized pieces and fell to the ground with a “crackling” sound. Not even half a drop of blood flowed out.

Some of the ferocious beasts, after vanishing into thin air for a few seconds, reappeared dozens of meters away in the form of ‘averaged slices’. It was as if an invisible million-ton water pressure machine was hiding in the air and crushing their every cell into a two-dimensional plane!

BAM!

Riding the ‘Arsonist’, Li Yao kicked the spider beast that was hissing on his body into the terrifying area ahead.

In the beginning, the ferocious beast was jumping up and down, baring its fangs and brandishing its claws, trying to attack him again.

But when it crawled forward and stepped into an invisible trap, it vanished just like the victims before it.

One second later, a blood-red ball no more than ten centimeters in diameter appeared on the spot. All the flesh and blood of the ferocious beast had been condensed inside.

Li Yao swallowed hard. His spine felt as cold as if it had been soaked in acid.

“This is the discontinuous zone that I told you about just now.

Lu Qingchen said, “You can simply imagine that the stable three-dimensional space here has been shattered. The storms of the four-dimensional space have rushed out through invisible gaps, filling this place with fatal traps. When we, the creatures of the three-dimensional space, step inside, the space we are in will either be divided into two, or the space will suddenly be compressed from three-dimensional to two-dimensional, or the space will suddenly shrink by a hundredfold. Believe me, flesh, blood, crystal suit, and giant soldier gods will not be able to resist the attacks launched from space. Even I, whose existence depends on the magnetic field of life, do not dare to take risks easily. It is very likely that my soul will be sealed in a space ripple forever. Even if I can see everything that is right in front of me, I will not be able to move or escape. It is a fate a hundred times more miserable than death!

“Do you see the dangerous area marked on my map? Invisible traps are everywhere around us. I sacrificed countless subordinates to mark them one by one, only to discover that the area we are in has been completely blocked. Look, I’m not lying. You can’t go out and find Ding Lingdang or anybody else directly. You can only find the control hub where the other half of the ‘Fuxi’ is located and the tower!

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao burnt his life and soul to explore the discontinuous area.

As it turned out, on a level that was invisible to the naked eye and beyond the senses of most magical equipment and low-level Cultivators, he ‘saw’ countless raging gaps and chaotic four-dimensional storms blowing out of them.

The four-dimensional storm and the broken space constituted an endless high wall that blocked him from the nearby area. If he was not careful, his telepathic thoughts would be swept into the four-dimensional storm, which would be like his brain being blown away by the storm. Then, it would crash into his skull at a speed hundreds of times higher than normal. He grunted and felt that thousands of thunderbolts had exploded inside his brain. His eyes were bloodshot, and he was bleeding from all his orifices. He was about to pass out. There was no road ahead.

A large batch of ferocious beasts were still besieging them from the rear. They realized the formidability of the discontinuous zone and did not dare to jump. Instead, they crouched and eyed Li Yao covetously.

Chapter 3046: Skysplit Tower

Li Yao took a deep breath and licked the blood flowing out of his throat.

The continuous fierce battles had pushed the vitality of every cell to the limit. He felt that his internal organs were burning and his stomach was rumbling

The feeling made the blood that he swallowed back to his throat sweeter than ever. It stimulated his every nerve like fireworks. Li Yao controlled the ‘Arsonist’ to squat down.

The deep black shell turned orange due to the high-speed friction between the spiritual flames and the air. All the spurts on the back of the crystal suit were ripped apart, as if countless bright eyes had opened at the same time. The earth below their feet was cracking, and the spider-net-like cracks even extended to hundreds of meters away!

“You’re not thinking of escaping through the air, are you? Absolutely not. There’s no cover in the air. You will be shot down!”

Lu Qingchen sensed Li Yao’s determination and shouted.

However, it was too late.

When more than ten ferocious beasts lunged at Li Yao at the same time, the dozens of power units around the ‘Arsonist’ spurted out torrents that looked like volcanic eruptions at the same time. The blast was so powerful that the ground within a hundred square meters was blown away. It was like a messy crater after a meteor shower.

And with the counterforce, the arsonist leapt high into the sky!

The earsplitting sonic boom shattered the strengthened glass windows of all the skyscrapers dozens of kilometers nearby. It even stimulated the brains of many ferocious beasts and made them scream.

Many ferocious beasts were quick to react. They followed the footsteps of the arsonist and jumped high into the air. Some of them even extended their membranes and wings from their arms, trying to fly above the arsonist.

Little did he know that everything had been within Li Yao’s calculations.

In the seemingly miserable escape just now, Li Yao had scanned the physiological structure and methods of attack of every kind of ferocious beast clearly. He had even deduced hundreds of times in his mind in order to come up with an adventure that seemed crazy but was at least fifty percent certain.

Pa! Pa! Pa!

The arsonist suddenly moved sideways and fell down abruptly.

As it turned out, he had appeared right above the few ferocious beasts. The iron feet that were wreathed in spiritual flames and electric arcs smashed down heavily. The few ferocious beasts were blown into smithereens instantly, like crimson fireworks blossoming in midair. The ‘incendiary’ took the opportunity to accelerate and shoot to a higher altitude.

At this moment, several ferocious beasts were still flapping their membranes and wings and biting Li Yao’s back.

Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!

The steel tentacles that were dancing crazily like vipers suddenly darted out and pierced through several ferocious beasts, before they were pulled to his side brutally.

At this moment, the arsonist was already two to three hundred meters in the air.

It was the height that was most likely to be discovered by the firepower on the ground.

As far as one’s eyes could reach, a large area of fireballs, electric arcs, and colorful mist suddenly appeared among the broken walls and attacked Li Yao in midair like a raging storm.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The few ferocious beasts that had been captured by the arsonists, on the other hand, had been stomped on by Li Yao and served as the best meat shields. Before they had the chance to scream, they were already riddled with holes by the fireballs, electric arcs, and acids. Then, they were burnt into ash and blown to smithereens.

In the blink of an eye, with the help of the few ‘shields’, not only did Li Yao get a precious distance to run away, he also saw through the dense distribution of firepower between the ground and the high buildings and the seemingly impenetrable web of fire. The gaps were as thin as gossamer. Under his perfect manipulation, the giant soldier was as agile as a flying sword made of lightning, leaving dazzling blurred shadows behind it. It was about to dash out of the last few gaps of the dense web of fire.

Right then, Li Yao crashed into the ‘wall’.

There was nothing above Li Yao’s head except for all kinds of eyes, mystic rays, and fog that neither visible nor invisible light could penetrate. But he felt that he had entered a deep ocean before he even entered the fog.

Pushed by billions of tons of force from all directions, his every cell was bursting. It was the feeling when he first arrived in the City of Silver through the black gaps!

The Giant God Soldier was also sending out all kinds of blood-freezing alarms, sounds of metal fracturing, and explosions of magical equipment. On the light beam that was twisted to the extreme, all the performance parameters were dropping crazily like falling off a cliff.

Li Yao, Lu Qingchen, and the ‘arsonist’ were all blown back.

Then, it was wreathed in hundreds of wires, acids, and electric arcs.

Even the best super giant soldier in the Pangu Universe had been riddled with holes and shattered. All the strengthened plate armor on its body had almost exploded.

Li Yao, on the other hand, felt that he had been soaked in magma after being cut into pieces.

He could not help but feel light-headed and fell from the sky. The entire world was overturned.

Right then, he saw the tower.

The magnificent, magnificent, magnificent, sacred, ancient, and primitive tower that could not be described in any language in the human world.

The high tower rose up from the ground like a silver tree. Its high and interconnected roots were like continuous mountains that occupied more than ten blocks. Hundreds of skyscrapers and bizarre-shaped buildings had been built based on it. They were connected to each other through thousands of pathways, like soldiers guarding it.

It was glittering and emitting flawless silver brightness. Li Yao maximized his vision and failed to find any window or gap on its surface.

It seemed to be made of the stars of the entire universe.

Around it, three hundred meters away from the ground, there were three enormous silver balls that looked like floating fortresses. However, even the most magnificent fortress paled in comparison to the high tower.

The skyscrapers of Silver City, in order to accommodate the size of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, were much taller than the buildings of ordinary human beings and could easily reach thousands of meters high.

However, in front of the high tower, the skyscrapers that were unreachable a moment ago were as low and insignificant as the weeds below the trees.

The tower was growing upwards. It even broke through the mist that blocked the sky and created a giant swirl that was swirling slowly. Looking through the swirl, Li Yao saw… the universe.

At first, Li Yao thought that he was hallucinating

However, no matter how deeply he breathed and how much he soothed his nerves with his spiritual energy, he could see a splendid universe where endless stars were blossoming and floating in the clouds above the high tower.

It was as if the high tower was some sort of… bridge, some sort of cross-border bridge from the Pangu Universe to the even more brilliant and resplendent multiverse!

“Supreme Tower!”

Lu Qingchen released extremely excited soul waves.

“What did you say?” Li Yao snapped. “The Skysplit Tower is the name of the pivot of the City of Silver and the location of the other half of the ‘Fuxi’.

Lu Qingchen continued quickly. “However, although the City of Silver was built by the Pangu Civilization, the ‘Pantheon Tower’ was not built by them. It must’ve been hundreds of millions of years old and was most likely built by the ‘Black Wall Maker’. All the secrets about the primeval era are stored at the top of the ‘Pantheon Tower’!

“Is that so?”

Li Yao’s mind was deeply attracted by the ‘heavenly tower’. He felt that he had seen similar facilities a long, long time ago.

Moreover, his soul had been launched into the ‘Pangu Universe’ at the edge of the multiverse through similar facilities!

“How is that possible?”

Li Yao mumbled to himself. He felt that he was a moth that was about to break out of the cocoon and see the last ray of light. However, there was an insurmountable barrier between him and the final answer. A beast tide. An overwhelming beast tide. It was surging from all directions of the City of Silver, surrounding the ‘heavenly tower’ and launching the fiercest attack.

There were so many ferocious beasts that there was not the slightest gap from the ground to the skyscrapers. Even the weakest silver-white color had been swallowed, to the point that Li Yao had mistaken them for a black carpet of fungi or endless ants.

However, if one were to observe carefully, they would discover that they were the main force of the ferocious beasts. There were already more than ten giant beasts that were almost a hundred meters tall such as the ‘Armadillo Dragon’ in the army that was attacking the ‘Tower Tower’. There were also countless hideous and bizarre-shaped ferocious beasts.

It was a soul-stirring epic battle. Although it was just a confrontation on the ground inside the atmosphere, it gave the feeling that thousands of starships in the vacuum of the universe were colliding with each other. With the stimulation of the soundwaves, the blood-boiling and shocking effect was hundreds of times more intense.

The surface of the ‘heavenly tower’ was as smooth and flawless as jade. There were no gaps at all. Naturally, there were no facilities such as firing points, hangars, crystal suits, or ports for the Giant Soldiers.

However, the architects of the Pangu Civilization had built fortresses and heavy turrets that were as complicated as mazes around the lower half of the tower, as if a hundred thousand volcanoes were erupting at the same time. The destructive magma could swallow the lives of hundreds of ferocious beasts every second.

Although he was dozens of kilometers away, Li Yao could almost hear the crazy and desperate roars of the guards inside the fortress.

Just as Li Yao had said, they were engaged in an impossible war.

Their crazy firepower could temporarily resist the Vicious Beast Tide, but it could not resist the invasion of the virus.

As more and more guardians of the fortress were infected by the virus and turned into ferocious beasts, the small fortress seemed to be unable to accommodate his deformed and swollen body. Bloody tentacles extended out of the holes and declared a ‘mute fire’.

The Vicious Beast Tide moved forward slowly but firmly.

The army of viruses that looked like black spots was about to corrupt the ‘heavenly towers!!

Chapter 3047: Little Black Guides The Way!

Silver ripples were released from the surface of the ‘heavenly tower’, too. Invisible energy fields were condensing in midair, like the calm before a storm. The air was filled with magma, electric arcs, and the smell of destruction.

Li Yao had an ominous feeling.

When the black spots formed by the Vicious Beast Tide stained the entire ‘Heavenly Tower’, the Heavenly Tower would release a destructive force that would destroy the Vicious Beast Tide, the guardians, the tomb of the Supreme Emperor, and even the Pangu Universe outside. Everything would be destroyed.

Li Yao did not know why there was such an omen, either. He could only try his best to fly toward the ‘heavenly tower’, hoping to stop the beast tide before it consumed everything.

He didn’t know what he could stop, even if it was just talking to the guards in the ‘Tower Tower’.

He had to communicate with the rational and calm Pangu Clan or the Nuwa Clan so that they could find the answers together and solve the ultimate problem of the Pangu Universe!

However, before he flew hundreds of meters away, the dense bombardment had blown up the power unit and the anti-gravity unit of the ‘Arsonist’. The acid that could even corrode diamonds stained the wings that he condensed with his spiritual energy. The ‘Arsonist’ fell from the sky like a bird with broken wings and lost control.

Black Hair screamed beside Li Yao’s ear.

In front of Li Yao’s eyes, countless complicated primeval runes popped up on the operation menu, only to collapse in the blink of an eye. The blinking signs that represented the units of magical equipment dimmed down. He lost control over every power rune array. He could only slightly adjust the posture of the Giant God Soldier with his remote manipulation ability, but he could not dodge the fierce bombardment on the ground. Therefore, the Giant God Soldier was heavily wounded and even worse. Like a broken meteorite, it cut across the sky and pierced through almost five skyscrapers before it crashed into the broken buildings.

BOOM!

For a moment, Li Yao felt that every bone and every joint of the ‘arsonist’ had been shattered.

He felt that he had been sealed in an impenetrable coffin. He could neither see nor hear anything. It was not until a long time later that his senses gradually returned to his body. The backup energy source of the ‘Arsonist’ was activated, and the operation menu was restarted. The units that were still intact were quickly connected to each other and assembled into a temporary driving mode that was barely functional.

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

Li Yao heard an earsplitting sound of metal being torn apart.

The stench of the ferocious beasts crashed into the cockpit through the cracks without any disguise.

As it turned out, a ferocious beast that looked like a hyena had discovered the ‘Arsonist’ and was biting the remaining breastplate of the ‘Arsonist’, trying to extend its hooked tongue into Li Yao’s body and lick his flesh.

Li Yao pulled himself together and picked up a chainsword.

Taking advantage of the moment when the beast tore open the breastplate of the ‘Arsonist’ and exposed himself to the air, Li Yao roared and lunged forward. He slashed his sword as fast as lightning and chopped off the tongue of the beast. Then, he opened his hands, and the light balls glittered. Spiritual energy flooded into the beast’s throat and blew up its brain. It collapsed to the ground before it had the chance to scream.

Li Yao sat down and breathed heavily, trying to restore the combat ability of the arsonist.

The breastplate was gone, and the spirit house was completely opened. The guardianship of the Giant God Soldier on the user had hit rock bottom.

But as a result, Li Yao could see everything in front of him clearly without any scanning or probe magical equipment.

The good news was that he controlled the direction when he was falling and dived toward the ‘heavenly tower’. He was not far away from the super building of the primeval era.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Even though it was blocked by skyscrapers, the enormous object still occupied half of the sky. It was like a giant silver tree that supported the heaven and the earth, leading to the highest heaven and the palaces of the gods.

The bad news was that thin, glittering space ripples appeared between him and the ‘heavenly tower’. They were the fatal traps that could cut through iron as if it were mud and would not accept any defense.

Li Yao could imagine that, if he stepped into the place recklessly on the back of the ‘Arsonist’, he would certainly be split into pieces like the ferocious beasts just now, or be condensed into fist-sized crystals of metal and flesh.

It was the most miserable way to die, even more miserable than jumping into a star during a space jump.

Besides, even if he could cross the dangerous space discontinuities, how was he going to drag the faltering giant soldier through the main force of the army of ferocious beasts?

Li Yao brought up the map drawn by Lu Qingchen again.

However, they found that the area was beyond the range of Lu Qingchen’s activity a few days ago. After a while of recklessness, they had reached an unknown area.

“We are lost.”

Lu Qingchen smiled bitterly. “I never tried to approach the ‘Tower Tower’ from the sky like you did a few days ago. Therefore, I don’t know where we are exactly, nor do I know how to get to the ‘Tower Tower’. Let me remind you, the closer you get to the ‘Tower Tower’, the more seriously the wrinkles and disorder in the space will be. It is like a defense mechanism of the “Tower Tower’. You must not rush into it directly!”

Li Yao was silent. He extended his telepathic thoughts to search the surroundings.

The skyscrapers nearby were like silver-white mirrors that reflected and refracted his telepathic thoughts, forming a complicated maze.

There were traps, ferocious beasts, and endless forks and spirals everywhere. The complexity of the space was far beyond Li Yao’s imagination.

The roars of the ferocious beasts were getting closer and closer. Li Yao could already see with his naked eye that several skyscrapers were shivering under the vibrations of the ferocious beasts.

But he still could not find a way out. He could not help but break out in cold sweat.

Right then, Little Black, who had been silent all the time, suddenly reacted.

It used to be a cluster of gel, or liquid metal, that was wrapped around Li Yao like a black jelly.

But this time, a small arrowhead suddenly protruded out of his soft body and pointed to the left.

“This is—”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. He was dazed for a long time before he stammered, “Little Black, are you showing us the way?”

Little Black rippled in gentle waves and seemed to be nodding at Li Yao.

“What is this?”

Lu Qingchen was greatly surprised. “Liquid alloys from the Star Ocean Imperium? They are intelligent? You believe that they can guide the way?”

Li Yao didn’t know how many secrets were hidden inside Little Black’s body. He didn’t know whether or not Little Black could really see the light of day in such chaotic space ripples.

But it did come from the depths of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor and was even closely related to the primeval relics. If there was anyone who could guide Li Yao, it would be it.

After all, neither he nor Lu Qingchen knew the direction. He might as well try his best!

“Little Black, do you really know the way to the Tower Tower’?”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao asked, “Or rather, did you stay in the ‘Tower Tower’ a long, long time ago? Are you from the ‘Tower Tower’?”

Little Black wriggled slowly.

He seemed to be thinking, recalling, recalling the data from thousands of years ago.

Then, it pushed Li Yao’s head softly and made a gesture that seemed to be a nod.

“Then… let’s go!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth. The heavily wounded ‘Arsonist’ rose up again and rushed toward the direction that Little Black had pointed.

The closer they got to the ‘Tower Tower’, the more intense the flames of war were. Ferocious beasts and battle puppets could be seen everywhere. Every minute, a skyscraper would collapse, and billions of tons of metal and stones would fall from the sky.

In the short ten minutes of the journey, Li Yao had broken four super-heavy chainswords and three high-frequency vibration sabers that were specially designed for the Giant God Soldiers. The ‘arsonist’ had added dozens of shocking wounds. The closest one was only a palm’s length away from Li Yao. If there had been a slight error, the thick horn would have pierced a hole in Li Yao’s chest. The spiritual shield and interference force field had long been gone. The fuel and ammunition reserves had dropped from 40% to 20%, too, reaching the critical warning line. But their route of retreat had been blocked by the Vicious Beast Tide. Li Yao had no other options except to march forward.

Huala!

Under Little Black’s guidance, the arsonist crashed through another skyscraper. The ‘heavenly tower’ seemed to be right in front of him. Li Yao could already see the tip of the tower that pierced through the sky and the universe around it.

However,

When the arsonist jumped out of the human-shaped hole, he happened to step into the nest, or rather, the rendezvous point, of a ferocious beast. Hundreds of giant reptiles that seemed to have mutated because of radiation turned around and looked at him. The corrosive liquid that was spraying out of the bloody mouth zigzagged to his feet.

Li Yao took a deep breath and forgot to swallow his saliva.

“I knew that I would not have a good ending if I teamed up with you. I was 100% sure that I would be struck by lightning!”

Lu Qingchen exclaimed, “What kind of ancillary system is this? What-Ahhh!”

He was only halfway through his complaint when the hammer that stored his precious soul was ruthlessly thrown out by Li Yao, smashing the head of the first ferocious beast into pieces. Of course, it also nearly shattered Lu Qingchen’s soul.

“We will definitely reach the ‘Tower Tower’.”

Li Yao roared, twisted his hips, stepped forward, and charged. He waved the hammer in his right hand as if it were a storm of destruction, and the chainsword in his left hand slashed through the air as if it were cutting through space. A path of blood was paved among the hundreds of ferocious beasts. “Nobody can stop me!”

The ferocious beasts that had no rationality and only had the instinct for killing were all shocked by Li Yao’s toughness and craziness. In their 100% instinct for killing, they were born with 1% fear.

Some of the rarely-seen beasts even ran away, only to be beheaded by Li Yao.

Li Yao seemed to have turned into a ferocious beast. The most ferocious beast, a beast king that was even stronger than the Armored Storm Dragon, crashed into the path of flesh and blood!

Chapter 3048: Mysterious Underground Man

“Chi!”

The broken chainsword was deeply embedded into the skull of a ferocious beast that looked like a tyrannosaur. Under the high-speed rotation and cutting of the sharp teeth, the skull, which was harder than alloy, was as soft as tofu. Li Yao slashed it open and extended it to his chest.

After a ‘huala’ sound, the super high pressure inside his abdomen pushed out a lot of his stinky internal organs, which were burnt to ash by the flames of the arsonist. They surrounded the Giant God Soldier and added to Li Yao’s evilness.

Before he could withdraw his broken sword, another ferocious beast attacked the ‘arsonist’ from the flank. The left arm that Li Yao had withdrawn was stabbed into its throat. After a round of bombardment, the stomach of the ferocious beast bulged as if it were pregnant. Very soon, steam thousands of degrees high was released from the wrinkles, indicating that its internal organs had been cooked by Li Yao.

Li Yao stimulated the Cosmos Ring crazily until the teeth of the last piece of melee magical equipment were broken. Then, he simply picked up the dead bodies of the ferocious beasts and smashed them into pieces like a giant hammer.

After moving forward a hundred meters, no ferocious beasts dared to block his way anymore.

Broken bones and minced meat of ferocious beasts were embedded between the feet of the giant soldier.

Of course, such crazy consumption was costly, too.

The ‘Arsonists’ had less than 10% of their fuel and ammunition. After a volley or a sprint, they would run out of supplies.

After a series of fierce battles, the result of the broken plate armor was that the ‘Arsonist’ turned into a skeleton.

Because the structure of the framework far exceeded the limits of metal fatigue, even the skeleton was on the verge of collapse. It was all supported by the soaring flames of Li Yao’s over-exhausted life force. At this moment, the “Arsonist” had almost lost the original appearance of the Giant God Soldier. He had completely turned into the amalgamation of Li Yao’s spirit, will, and his entire life, a burning fire!

Ao! Ao! Ao!

The ferocious beasts nearby all craned their necks and screamed, summoning stronger helpers.

III

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! The two skyscrapers in front of them collapsed one after another. The airwaves rose up like a tsunami and blocked the sky and the sun. Even half of the ‘heavenly towers’ were blocked.

A moment later, the tide of dust split apart, revealing another Armored Storm Dragon that was almost a hundred meters tall.

It was a pity that Li Yao didn’t have a second ‘Iron Knight’ with him.

Looking at the enormous, invincible enemy, Li Yao’s belief was as clear as water, as hard as iron, as cold as ice, as hot as fire, and without the slightest impurity.

At this point, there was no way out. Even if the enemy was a hundred times stronger, there was only one way out for him.

And that was… to summon the courage to raise the battle saber high and ruthlessly chop off the head of the hideous and ugly ferocious beast!

Taking a deep breath, Li Yao opened his arms on the back of the ‘Arsonist’. Spiritual flames spread out like tentacles. The broken sabers, broken swords, and battle axes, as well as the rebar of the skyscrapers, the remains of the battle puppets, and the most solid bones among the dead bodies of the ferocious beasts, were all summoned by him. They flew to the sky and gathered toward the ‘Arsonist’. Resonance of Li Yao’s fighting will gathered into two enormous sabers.

The saber, which used to be colorful and full of patches, was now a pure crimson after being cleansed by Li Yao’s crimson flames, as if it had been forged on the surface of a star. “Come on!”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao crossed his saber in front of his chest and roared at the ‘Armadillo Dragon’.

“Hooooo!”

In the blink of an eye, the Armored Storm Dragon’s neck was three to five times thicker than before. Its originally rough and ugly gray-brown skin had become almost transparent, as if an enormous ball of lightning was bursting forth from its abdomen.

Before the ball of lightning was spurted out of the bloody mouth, Li Yao had already melded with the ‘Arsonist’. He jumped high into the sky and charged at the Armored Storm Dragon’s thunderous mouth. BOOM!

Before the ball of lightning spurted out a mouthful of blood, it was already hit by Li Yao’s crossed sabers. The two clusters of energy that contained the power of destruction broke the restrictive magnetic field and blew up in the mouth of the Armored Storm Dragon.

The ‘Arsonist’ was blown away by the blast and fell to the ground again, leaving a deep pit.

The Armored Storm Dragon’s jaw was blown to pieces. Blood was flowing out of its eyes, ears, nose, and mouth. Its brain was seriously shaken, and it was unable to stand steadily like a mountain. It continuously retreated, causing countless buildings to suffer unexpected disasters.

It was obvious that Li Yao’s last-ditch attack had seriously wounded the Armored Storm Dragon. Now that the door was wide open, he could hit the brain directly through its softest throat!

It was a pity that Li Yao had run out of ammunition. He couldn’t even control a flying sword remotely. He could do nothing but watch the Armored Storm Dragon regain balance. Ruthlessness and fear were beaming out of his eyes again.

Li Yao was breathing heavily on one of his knees.

His eyes seemed to be blurred by blood, and he could not see his surroundings clearly. He could only grope around, hoping to find a saber that could still be waved.

The earthquake told him that the Armored Storm Dragon was getting closer and closer.

He finally found a twisted chainsword and shook it. The chains and gears of the chainsword all fell off.

But he did not think too much of it. He condensed new sharp teeth with his own blood and spiritual flames. Then, he raised his giant bloody sword high and aimed at the Armored Storm Dragon’s head, which was riddled with holes.

Then, Li Yao was dazed.

He saw an unbelievable scene.

Gold. The gold that was more brilliant than the sun and more dazzling than a supernova explosion was burning his eyes, shocking his brain, and boiling his blood.

It was a human being, not a giant soldier more than twenty meters tall. A human being more than two meters tall in a crystal suit was standing on the head of the Armored Storm Dragon!

He was on one of his knees. His left hand grasped one of the wrinkles on the head of the Armored Storm Dragon, as if a giant tree had been dug deep into the soil. No matter how hard the Armored Storm Dragon shook its head and struggled, he did not move at all and even dug deeper and deeper.

In the meantime, he opened his right hand and pressed one of the eyes of the Armored Storm Dragon.

Other than the crystal suit, Li Yao did not notice that he had retrieved any magical equipment. Even his movements were extremely gentle, as if he were petting a pet at home, while he pressed the eyes of the Armored Storm Dragon softly.

Sensing Li Yao’s gaze, he even had the leisure to raise his head and look at Li Yao. Then

Then, ten thousand suns burst out of his palm at the same time and flooded into the Armored Storm Dragon’s head along his eyeballs.

The Armored Storm Dragon’s head expanded like a balloon that had been filled with air. Dozens of malformed lumps even grew out of its head and protruded out one after another.

Its head had suffered the counterforce of its ball of lightning in the first place, not to mention the fierce attack of the mysterious golden man. Very soon, almost a hundred of its eyeballs were burnt into holes, through which raging golden flames darted out. It seemed that all the organs inside the skull, from the brain to the central nerves, had been burnt to ash.

When the golden flames of destruction soared into the sky together with all its brain tissues, the Armored Storm Dragon’s head finally returned to its original size, but everybody could tell that its skull was empty. It had lost all its physiological functions and turned into an empty corpse.

BOOM!

His body, which weighed more than ten thousand tons, collapsed heavily. The gargantuan beast had been killed by the mysterious golden man!

Before Li Yao had the chance to be stunned, the gold brightness around the mysterious man suddenly expanded by a hundred times, turning the entire world into bright, glittering colors. In the blink of an eye, Li Yao and the rest of the ferocious beasts could only see gold in various degrees. They could no longer see or feel anything!

Little Black, on the other hand, was overjoyed. It pushed Li Yao hard and gestured for him to move forward in the golden torrent.

“Little Black, you mean…”

Li Yao finally realized what was going on. “You are not bringing us to the ‘heavenly tower’, but to meet the mysterious golden man?”

Little Black shivered crazily again, indicating that Li Yao was right.

Li Yao no longer hesitated. He gritted his teeth and dragged the broken giant soldier forward, guided by Little Black.

He couldn’t see anything, nor could the Giant God Soldier scan anything. But the mysterious golden man and Little Black seemed to have had a wonderful connection. The detailed 3D map was inputted into Little Black’s brain. Li Yao allowed Little Black to guide him, but he didn’t meet any ferocious beasts, nor did he step into the terrifying discontinuous zone. Very soon, Li Yao felt that the ‘arsonists’ had sunk into the ground. The gold brightness gradually dispersed, and Li Yao regained his vision and senses. As he expected, the mysterious golden man was jumping forward so fast that even the Giant God Soldier could not catch up with him. Very soon, they had gone down along the helical pathway and were many meters below the ground.

In front of them was an iron gate weighing tens of thousands of tons.

The mysterious man made a gesture with his hands and drew runes in midair. Gold runes quickly crawled into the rune arrays on the gate. After dull noises, the gate, which was more than ten meters thick, was slowly opened. However, there was still a long, dark, damp pathway below the gate that was full of branches. At the end of the branches was a new gate.

Judging from the style of the gate and the password, the gate did not seem to belong to the same civilization. It was like the fossils buried in the faults had been accumulated over billions of years. The deeper one went, the more ancient the relics were.

Judging from the rune arrays and the refining materials, the gate at the beginning was indeed the style of the Pangu Civilization.

But then, a gate that seemed to be thick and black was filled with mechanical structures that were as precise as nerves and veins. It was exactly the same as the Star Knights.

Chapter 3049: Museum Of Prehistoric Civilization

Passing through the gate, Li Yao found that the walls of the pathway were full of interconnected tubes, gears that had been bitten precisely, and overlapping mechanical structures. Steam was even spurting out. He felt that he had entered the internal organs of a mechanical beast.

After passing through the ‘gut’ of the mechanical beast for a long time, Li Yao discovered that the gate below was actually made of translucent crystals. He could vaguely see that countless lightning bolts that had been sealed were flowing slowly among the crystals, as if they were vipers guarding treasures. However, under the command of the mysterious golden man, the lightning vipers all retreated. The crystal gate gradually turned transparent and disappeared.

Further down, they passed by another gate that was purely made of plants. There was another gate that was wreathed in vague mist and in a half-solid state. There were also several other gates that were of different styles and had complicated technology that even Li Yao had never seen before.

Every gate was opened in a different way. Some of the methods were beyond the limits of human biology and imagination.

But the mysterious man seemed to have been through the place hundreds of times. He was familiar with every step and opened all the gate smoothly without any hesitation.

After passing through a gate, going a hundred meters underground was like entering a whole new world. Dazzling clusters of crystals were growing in the pathway behind the gate, emitting dazzling brilliance that was not entirely gone after thousands of years.

Behind the gate of plants was what seemed to be a withered primitive forest. The vines and bushes had dried up and turned into human-shaped shadows.

Behind the gate of mist were traces that looked like frozen smoke. They seemed to belong to the explorers a long time ago. Their bodies of flesh and blood had long been burnt to ash, but their souls had been preserved in an unbelievable way, guiding the way for the latecomers.

Li Yao even saw countless… corpses of creatures on the four walls of a pathway.

The ‘things’ looked like gorillas with four arms. There was a giant eye on their forehead, but it had a bronze gloss and texture. After billions of years of erosion, they gradually melded with the pathway and turned into hideous, twisted paintings. The only thing left were tiny black holes, which were their eyes. The unbelievable scene made Li Yao shudder. He thought quickly and realized that it must be a laboratory, research center, or a forward operating base that countless civilizations had tried to explore over the billions of years.

When the ancient carbon-based intelligent creatures, who used to be the rulers of the Pangu Universe, discovered the primeval relics, they naturally gathered the most cutting-edge talents and technologies just like the Pangu civilization and the human civilization did and established the most magnificent laboratories and forward bases in the place, trying to unveil the mysteries of the primeval era.

However, a certain mysterious force had erased the traces of dozens of civilizations with ancient carbon-based wisdom, and only the ruins deep below the ground told of their past glory. Hundreds of thousands or even millions of years later, a new generation of intelligent carbon-based creatures developed a developed civilization and found the primeval relics. They established their own laboratories and forward bases on the ruins of the previous civilization, only to be destroyed again.

It was a never-ending cycle. Today, billions of years later, the underground of the City of Silver had dozens of collapsed buildings that were hundreds of times larger and more complicated than those on the surface.

If the City of Silver was destroyed by the tides of the ferocious beasts today, and the tides of the ferocious beasts were destroyed by the terrifying power released by the ‘heavenly towers’, it would not take hundreds of thousands of years for the ruins of the City of Silver to be corroded by time and become the topmost layer of ruins.

“Hundreds of civilizations stopped here, as if this was the end of their civilization!

Li Yao was deeply shocked.

He did not believe that when a civilization arrived in this place, they would not see the traces of the destruction of dozens of other civilizations.

Dozens of civilizations were recorded clearly in the database of the other half of the Fuxi civilization.

Knowing the dangers of the primeval relics, the civilizations were still rushing forward like moths to a flame. Why?

Li Yao thought quickly and smiled bitterly, laughing at his own stupidity. Curiosity and thirst for knowledge were necessary conditions for the development of a civilization. Human beings were human beings because of their endless desire for “unknown’ and ‘future’.

No civilization could leave such a huge ancient relic untouched.

There were always ambitious people, adventurers, and lunatics. Righteousness or evil, they were the first motivation for the advancement of civilization.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Besides, the more developed a civilization was, the more likely it would destroy itself.

Even without the primeval relics, could a civilization not develop ‘universal magical equipment’ that could destroy itself?

A civilization that could not destroy itself did not deserve to be called a civilization.

Maybe, the so-called civilization was such a crazy ‘cosmic virus’?

Li Yao was deep in thought.

At first, he tried to communicate with the mysterious golden man, too, including shouting and telepathic thoughts transmission. But the mysterious golden man turned a deaf ear to him and simply ran ahead of him.

Li Yao knew that he was being guided. He could only suppress his impatience and follow closely behind while he scanned every detail around him.

The last gate rumbled like an underground thunder and retreated to the crags nearby. It was no longer a narrow pathway or collapsed debris in front of him but a giant cave more than five hundred meters in diameter. Faint mist of light was swirling around him. The mist was like a living spirit that would condense as Li Yao’s gaze moved. When the mist of light condensed, its brightness would increase by a few degrees, allowing Li Yao to clearly see everything around him.

It was like… a museum of a prehistoric civilization.

In the hazy mist of light, Li Yao saw the remains of a ‘Iron Knight’ first. It had been carefully dissected into almost a hundred units. Even the most delicate core mechanical structure had been dismantled, as if a mechanical surgery had been performed.

Then, there was a structure in the shape of a crystal pyramid. It was transparent on the surface, but it was full of tiny holes inside. Li Yao observed for a long time before he came to the conclusion that the holes that looked like a network of nerves were indeed ‘nerves’ in a certain sense. It was a very efficient approach to transmit and process information through the transmission, refraction, and diffraction of the photoelectric signals inside the holes.

Li Yao did not know whether such a structure was a creation of the ancient civilization or a corpse of the ancient civilization. Although it did not look like a carbon-based intelligent life, it was possible that it was the crystallization of a certain coral-like creature after death.

There were many weird things inside the mist that looked like the structure of a crystal pyramid. But before Li Yao examined them carefully, the mysterious golden man had already walked into the depths of the mist.

The mist dispersed, revealing several enormous human figures.

Li Yao’s pupils constricted violently. He had discovered several other members of the Pangu Clan.

With a closer look, he discovered that the Pangu Clan members were not moving at all. They were soaked in the enormous strengthening crystal towers and surrounded by preservatives.

The Pangu Clan members had been killed a long time ago and turned into ‘specimens’.

No. It was not just the Pangu Clan. Hundreds of crystal towers were standing in the depths of the mist, each of which stored the corpse of a prehistoric, carbon-based, intelligent creature. Not just the Pangu Clan, whose skin was gray and whose head was full of heat-dissipating wrinkles, there were also the Nuwa Clan, which had human heads and snake bodies, the Kuafu Clan, which looked no different from plants at first glance, the Houyi Clan, which was made of black beetles and evolved from sponges, and the Gonggong Clan, which looked like transparent gel… The corpses of thirteen prehistoric species had been collected in this place. Many of them had been cut off precisely from their skin and muscles, leaving only veins and nerves.

The master of the place seemed to have a profound understanding about the biological structure of the ancient, intelligent species.

There were also ferocious beasts. Passing through the crystal towers where the corpses of the prehistoric species were stored, Li Yao saw more than ten operation tables that had been modified from maintenance platforms. The bloody corpses of the ferocious beasts were placed on the tables. On the biggest ‘surgery table’, there was even the head of a Armored Storm Dragon. Its skull had been cut open in a ring. The entire skull had been removed, revealing a deformed brain that had been sealed by a certain bright green gel so that it would not wither or rot.

Hundreds of crystal wires were inserted into the brain of the Armored Storm Dragon and led to the super crystal processors of the Star Ocean Imperium nearby. The crystal processors were emitting dim fluorescence and performing high-speed computations. The virtual brain of the Armored Storm Dragon was created on a 3D light beam.

Following the instructions of the crystal processor, the Armored Storm Dragon, which only had its head left, seemed to still be alive. Occasionally, it would blink.

It seemed that the master of the place had a very precise understanding about the brain structure of the Armored Storm Dragon, as well as its fighting pattern and habits. That was why he could reach its head and kill it without alarming anyone!

Li Yao was more and more curious about the identity of the mysterious golden man. In the museum of the prehistoric civilization, there were so many remains of the ‘Iron Knights of Starry Skies’ and the ‘Rampaging Armored Dragon’, including almost a hundred corpses of the intelligent creatures of the primeval erain the eyes of most human beings, they were ‘specimens of gods!!

Who was he exactly?

An obvious answer popped up in Li Yao’s brain.

The name that contained infinite power and almost represented ‘invincible’ made Li Yao feel short of breath just thinking about it. His heart was beating fast, his blood was boiling, and his pores were bulging uncontrollably, ready to spurt crimson steam.

Led by the mysterious gold man, they passed through the crystal towers and the operation tables. In front of them was the end of the mist and the maintenance and modification workshop of heavy magical equipment.

Chapter 3050: Little Black’S Complete Form

Li Yao’s heart was beating faster and faster, as if something mysterious was calling him.

On a large maintenance rack, the first thing that caught his attention was the remains of a crimson giant soldier.

The upper body of the giant soldier was enormous, with short limbs. It was in the shape of an upside-down triangle. Sharp tusks were embedded from its shoulders to the back of its hands, as if they were synonymous with ‘destruction’ and ‘apocalypse’. Right now, the limbs of the giant soldier had all been broken and placed to one side. The core unit that constituted its torso had been shattered, too. The most critical mainframe crystal processor had been riddled with holes and could not move at all.

But it was still raging in silence, as if it were an eternal fire that was burning the world in the depths of hell.

Li Yao had never seen such a brutal giant soldier before. Every streak of its fury turned into a saber of fear and slashed into his brain.

His rationality told him that the giant soldier had been paralyzed and dissected thousands of years ago. It was impossible for it to be reorganized.

However, his instincts and intuition sent out a sharp warning, as if the mental imprint of the master of the Giant God Soldier was too intense to be erased even after ten thousand years.

Even ‘Heart of Black Hole’, the personal coach of Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi, had never given Li Yao so much pressure before.

The stranger was apparently motionless and paralyzed, but Li Yao felt that his back was soaked in sweat.

Li Yao thought quickly and searched for the most famous Giant God Soldiers in the history of human civilization.

Almost without thinking, a soul-stirring name popped up in his head one tenth of a second later, while he grinned hideously.

It was an out-and-out ‘apocalyptic’ and ‘apocalyptic’ that had brought great suffering to the civilization of mankind and almost cast the Pangu Universe into eternal darkness. It was the ride of the Mad Blood God, the Mad Armageddon! It was true that only ‘apocalyptic apocalypse’, the legendary super giant soldier with the strongest attack ability in the Pangu Universe, could bring so much pressure to Li Yao when he was faced with the ‘incendent’ formed by the combination of Mystic Skeleton of Hell and the heart of the black hole.

Only the apocalypse, after the earthshaking final battle ten thousand years ago, could still be as ferocious as before after ten thousand years!

It was said that Blood God, the Mad Armageddon, had gathered the rebels of the imperium, the remnants of the demon race, and the extraterrestrial devils and marched into the Empyrean Terminus Sector with a magnificent coalition fleet, allowing the fire of destruction to engulf the entire Pangu Universe.

Even the ‘Supreme Emperor’, the strongest expert in the history of human civilization, had been seriously wounded by the Blood God in the apocalypse and had to flee to the depths of the primeval relics. With the help of the Time Stop’ technique, he was able to maintain a mysterious state, hoping that a miracle similar to the ‘Light of Life’ would appear one day and revive him.

However, Li Yao could not figure it out.

Wasn’t it the Supreme Emperor who had fled into the ancient relics? Why was the Blood God’s Giant God Soldier here, and in such a miserable state?

It did not make sense that the Blood God had followed the Supreme Emperor into the primeval relics, either. How could Wu Yingqi, the Black Star Emperor, explain his encounter with the star fortress that carried the soul of the Blood God when he left his hometown and wandered in the sea of stars?

There was no need for Wu Yingqi to lie about this.

Besides, Li Yao had not only heard the story from Wu Yingqi, but he had also received his legacy directly and read his memory pieces.

When Li Yao’s consciousness was melded with Wu Yingqi’s memory pieces, he could feel the terror and helplessness of his ‘self’ when he first encountered the remnant soul of the Blood God.

It was impossible to fake the feeling of every nerve ending.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao couldn’t figure out what was going on, but he couldn’t help but raise his vigilance. It was definitely not a good thing that the Blood God’s ride appeared in this place.

“This is… the apocalypse!”

Lu Qingchen exclaimed. He recognized it, too.

Then, Li Yao felt that Lu Qingchen’s soul was fluctuating subtly. The humming of the hammer was a sign that his computational ability was soaring.

“Hey, don’t play with fire. Don’t think that you can achieve your purpose with the help of the Supreme Emperor or the Blood God!

Li Yao saw through Lu Qingchen’s motives immediately. He warned him in advance, “Whether the enemy is the Supreme Emperor, the Blood God, or some other existence, they are not something that you and I can make use of easily. You might get caught in your own trap and get yourself killed!”

Lu Qingchen sniffed and did not comment.

If he still had eyes, they would be rolling crazily right now.

He had always been a man who refused to believe in the supernatural. “I will not turn back even if I break through the southern wall. My life is up to me and not the heavens.” This was his true belief.

However, Lu Qingchen was apparently as curious as Li Yao. Why was the Giant God Soldier of the Blood God in this place and who had beaten him so miserably?

Soon, they saw the answer.

In the blink of an eye, the mist dispersed. Ten thousand gold horns were blown at the same time, and the bright gold giant soldier that seemed to be made of yellow jade appeared in front of them.

Li Yao’s and Lu Qingchen’s soul waves froze at the same time. They were both shocked beyond words.

It was not the most ferocious giant soldier that they had ever seen, nor did it lack the brutality and brutality of the apocalypse.

There were no dazzling plug-in units on the surface of the crystal suit, no aerodynamic acceleration fins or spoilers on the back of the crystal suit, and barely any complicated runes on the composite plate of most of the Titan Warriors. It looked like an ordinary, basic Titan Warrior.

However, the bright gold brightness spreading out from every inch of its armor, every gap, and every mottled spurts gave the feeling that it was warm and reliable. It was like the trust that the intelligent carbon-based creatures had for the sun. It was an instinct born from the deepest part of their genes.

The giant soldier would never be defeated, just like the sun would never be extinguished.

It was the sun, the golden sun, the legendary vehicle of the Supreme Emperor, the strongest expert of mankind!

When they saw the debris of the apocalypse just now, Li Yao’s and Lu Qingchen’s soul waves were both very intense. But right now, they did not know what to think. All that was left was shock and admiration.

In particular, the maintenance of the ‘Gold Sun’ seemed to be much better than that of the apocalypse. There was barely any damage on the surface. The four limbs and the power unit were fully equipped, too. The structure between the abdomen of the user and the mainframe crystal processor was neat and tidy.

In other words, the giant soldier… could still move?

Li Yao was shocked by his own thoughts.

But the passion in his eyes betrayed his real thoughts.

The mysterious golden man finally stopped between the ‘golden sun’ and the ‘apocalyptic apocalypse’ and looked at Li Yao.

Li Yao’s heart was beating so fast that it was about to explode.

“Chi_”

Faint mist leaked out of every gap of the golden crystal suit and wreathed him, making him even more mysterious.

Then, the crystal suit blossomed like a flower, revealing the wearer who was…

“Huh?”

Li Yao was dumbfounded.

Lu Qingchen’s soul waves missed half a beat,

too.

There was no one inside the golden crystal suit. What was supporting the crystal suit was a lump of black gel that looked like jelly. At first glance, it was exactly the same as the material that constituted ‘Little Black’, a certain liquid metal that could think for itself.

The cluster of black gel flew out of the crystal suit and grew two short, small wings, which flapped clumsily in the air as if it were performing a dance that represented ‘invitation’. From this angle, it looked more like ‘Little Black’.

As it turned out, ‘Little Black’ sensed the call of its former companion and moved restlessly, too. It could not wait to get out of the spiritual residence that had been opened by the arsonist and turned back into an innocent ball. It flew to the sky and danced with the ball that crawled out of the golden crystal suit.

Li Yao had never seen Little Black like this before. The seal inside his body seemed to have been completely unlocked by the dancing and resonating of the two balls.

Ripples spread out on the surface of the two balls at the same time. They attracted each other and drew closer to each other. They gradually melded into a giant ball.

Before, when they split into two halves, Little Black and the other party both gave people a very cute and harmless feeling, like an inconspicuous pet.

Once they were combined, it immediately released tremendous pressure, increasing the pressure of the entire space by ten times!

“Little Black?”

UIO

Li Yao did not know whether it was because Little Black had swallowed the enemy, or because the enemy had swallowed Little Black, or because Little Black had finally completed its mission across ten thousand years and returned to its ‘complete form’. In short, glamorous brilliance and glamorous stripes were blossoming on the surface of the giant black ball. It was communicating and processing the information a hundred times faster than usual, transmitting everything that Little Black had experienced in the outside world-from the Heaven’s Origin Sector to the Flying Star Sector, from the federation to the imperium, along with Li Yao’s growth and the reunification of the human civilization.

The perfect black ball was floating in midair, spinning slowly while it pondered. Li Yao had a very weird feeling. Maybe it was because Little Black had secretly sent a message to him, telling him that it was a test program set up by the Supreme Emperor ten thousand years ago. By scanning and analyzing all the information from the outside world, it would determine whether or not the person Little Black brought was qualified to accept something

After a series of crisp beeps, a dominating battle emblem finally appeared on the surface of the black ball and revolved around it.

It was a raging crimson dragon chasing after a glittering sun, as if it were about to open its bloody mouth and swallow the sun and all the stars in the universe.

It was the ‘Red Dragon Swallowing the Sun’, the emblem that represented the tenacity and tenacity of mankind in the era of the Star Ocean Imperium, the most prosperous civilization in the history of mankind!
